35static QUOTE_WRAPPED: Lazy<Regex> = Lazy::new(|| Regex::new("^\"(.+)\"$").unwrap());
36
37/// Maximum nesting depth for package/class loading to prevent infinite recursion.
38/// Perl LaTeXML has no explicit limit but rarely exceeds 20 levels in practice.
39const MAX_INPUT_DEPTH: usize = 500;
40
41thread_local! {
42  /// Current nesting depth of input_definitions calls.
43  static INPUT_DEPTH: std::cell::Cell<usize> = const { std::cell::Cell::new(0) };
44  /// Classes a binding read raw itself (`InputDefinitions!(noltxml)` of a `.cls`, without the class-load options
45  /// handling) that no class load has yet given to K11: the enclosing class load claims them at its end
46  /// (`input_definitions_impl`). A Rust local count, so nested loads pair by the call stack.
47  static UNCLAIMED_RAW_CLASS_READS: std::cell::Cell<usize> = const { std::cell::Cell::new(0) };
48  /// The stand-in bindings loading now, innermost last (`StandInBindingName`; a renamed elsarticle.cls copy's natbib
49  /// load, 1811.10063).
50  static STAND_IN_BINDINGS: std::cell::RefCell<Vec<String>> =
51    const { std::cell::RefCell::new(Vec::new()) };
52  /// The standard-class derivatives read now, innermost last, each with the names its standard class's binding
53  /// assigned in its own load ([`reading_standard_class_derivative`], [`standard_class_binding_assigned`]; aip-cp.cls,
54  /// 1506.04050).
55  static STANDARD_CLASS_DERIVATIVE: std::cell::RefCell<Vec<HashSet<SymStr>>> =
56    const { std::cell::RefCell::new(Vec::new()) };
57  /// Current nesting depth of [`load_class`] calls: above 0, a class is loading,
58  /// and a class it loads is nested.
59  static CLASS_LOAD_DEPTH: std::cell::Cell<usize> = const { std::cell::Cell::new(0) };
60  /// Stack of each active input_definitions frame's `grandparent_in_expl3` flag.
61  /// A native binding that force-raw-loads its own `\ProvidesExplPackage` .sty via
62  /// `InputDefinitions!(noltxml => true)` (e.g. derivative_sty.rs, #630) re-enters
63  /// input_definitions AFTER the outer `\@pushfilename` already ran `\ExplSyntaxOff`
64  /// (`_` -> SUB). A fresh snapshot then reads false, so the exit cleanup over-fires
65  /// `\ExplSyntaxOff` and corrupts `\l__expl_status_stack_tl`. The inner frame
66  /// inherits the outer's flag instead. Witness arXiv:2605.21946 (pomegranate.sty
67  /// -> `\RequirePackage{derivative}`).
68  static INPUT_DEF_EXPL3: std::cell::RefCell<Vec<bool>> =
69    const { std::cell::RefCell::new(Vec::new()) };
70}
71
72/// a configuration for loading LaTeX definition files (such as .sty, .cls, and their bindings)
73pub struct InputDefinitionOptions {
74  /// an optional extension (such as "sty")
75  pub extension:        Option<Cow<'static, str>>,
76  /// package options to pass into the loaded library, appended to `\opt@<name>.<ext>` (`pass_options`). A binding that
77  /// reads its own raw file passes none: the `\usepackage` that loaded the binding recorded them already, and passing
78  /// them again runs every option twice (`\opt@<name>.<ext>` read `noautomatic,noautomatic`).
79  pub options:          Vec<String>,
80  /// Tokens to process after the definition is loaded
81  pub after:            Tokens,
82  /// flag to forbid raw TeX sources
83  pub notex:            bool,
84  /// flag to forbid errors ?
85  pub noerror:          bool,
86  /// flag to forbid binding dispatch
87  pub noltxml:          bool,
88  /// collection of (package) options to process when loading the dependency
89  pub withoptions:      Option<Vec<String>>,
90  /// flag to handle options, or ignore them
91  pub handleoptions:    bool,
92  /// flag to process in .cls mode (default: false)
93  pub as_class:         bool,
94  /// flag to indicate reading the file raw in Gullet
95  pub raw:              bool,
96  /// flag to allow reloading a previously loaded definitions file
97  pub reloadable:       bool,
98  /// `\input` of a raw file already read while reading definitions: TeX
99  /// reads it again. Forbids binding dispatch like `noltxml`, without the
100  /// `\@currname` directory resolution `noltxml` also implies (see `input`).
101  pub reread:           bool,
102  /// flag: set @ catcode to LETTER during loading (default true).
103  /// Set to false for packages like xy.tex that need @ to stay as OTHER.
104  pub at_letter:        bool,
105  /// When set, raw-file lookup is restricted to the user-supplied
106  /// SEARCHPATHS (SOURCEDIRECTORY + graphicspaths), skipping the
107  /// kpsewhich fallback into system texmf. Matches Perl Package.pm's
108  /// `searchpaths_only => 1` — enabled by the `localrawstyles` option
109  /// to latexml.sty. Perl ref: Package.pm L2135, L2674.
110  pub searchpaths_only: bool,
111  /// A class loaded while another class loads (`\LoadClass`): its options are
112  /// its own (`\opt@<name>.cls`), never the global options. latex.ltx sets
113  /// `\@classoptionslist` only at the first class load, `\documentclass`
114  /// (latex.ltx:18716-18718; KPE #304).
115  pub nested_class:     bool,
116}
117impl Default for InputDefinitionOptions {
118  fn default() -> Self {
119    InputDefinitionOptions {
120      extension:        None,
121      options:          Vec::new(),
122      after:            Tokens!(),
123      notex:            false,
124      noerror:          false,
125      noltxml:          false,
126      raw:              false,
127      reloadable:       false,
128      reread:           false,
129      withoptions:      None,
130      handleoptions:    false,
131      as_class:         false,
132      at_letter:        true,
133      searchpaths_only: false,
134      nested_class:     false,
135    }
136  }
137}
138
139/// PLANS P16 xii: fire the kernel's generic package/class load hooks around a
140/// load that bypasses `\@onefilewithoptions` (a binding or a raw read).
141/// latex.ltx:18843-18850 runs `\UseHook{package/before}` +
142/// `\UseOneTimeHook{package/<name>/before}` (or `class/…`) before the file
143/// and :18867-18875 `\UseOneTimeHook{package/<name>/after}` +
144/// `\UseHook{package/after}` after its `-h@@k`; `\InputIfFileExists`'
145/// `\@input@file@exists@with@hooks` adds `file/<name>.<ext>/before|after`
146/// and `file/before|after`. Perl fires none of them (Package.pm L2620-2655),
147/// so `\AddToHook{class/scrbook/after}{…}` (tudapub.cls hooking scrbook's
148/// `\addchap`, DEMO-TUDaPhD) never ran. Only when lthooks is loaded
149/// (`\UseHook` defined) and only for package/class loads. Guard:
150/// `perfect_kernel_batch54::package_after_hook_fires_for_a_binding_load`.
151///
152/// The load is bracketed, as in `\@onefilewithoptions` (latex.ltx:18772/18789)
153/// and `\InputIfFileExists` (:19649/19655), by ltfilehook's file-name stack
154/// (`push_load_file_stack` before, `…@pop@@` after the hooks), which packages
155/// read to learn which files are open: scrlfile-hook.sty:146-158 seeds its own
156/// stack from it and pops once per `file/after`, so with the kernel stack left
157/// empty every file already open when it loaded underflowed ("More file names
158/// popped from stack than put to", 983 corpus manuals, 5 per nomencl load).
159/// Guard: `perfect_kernel_batch56::file_hooks_keep_the_file_name_stack`.
160fn use_load_hooks(name: &str, as_type: &str, when: &str) -> Result<()> {
161  if !load_hooks_active(name)? {
162    return Ok(());
163  }
164  let kind = if as_type == "cls" { "class" } else { "package" };
165  let code = if when == "before" {
166    s!(
167      "\\UseHook{{{kind}/before}}\\UseOneTimeHook{{{kind}/{name}/before}}\
168       \\UseHook{{file/before}}\\UseOneTimeHook{{file/{name}.{as_type}/before}}"
169    )
170  } else {
171    s!(
172      "\\UseOneTimeHook{{file/{name}.{as_type}/after}}\\UseHook{{file/after}}\
173       \\UseOneTimeHook{{{kind}/{name}/after}}\\UseHook{{{kind}/after}}"
174    )
175  };
176  digest(crate::mouth::tokenize_internal(TeXString::assembled(code)))?;
177  Ok(())
178}
179
180/// Push the load onto ltfilehook's file-name stack, outermost as in latex.ltx
181/// (before the `before` hooks); true when pushed. The caller pops exactly when
182/// this pushed, whether or not the file was found: the decision is taken once,
183/// before the load, since the load itself can define the kernel (the
184/// `latexml.sty` preload brings in LaTeX.pool).
185fn push_load_file_stack(name: &str) -> Result<bool> {
186  let active = load_hooks_active(name)?
187    && lookup_definition(&T_CS!("\\@expl@@@filehook@file@push@@"))?.is_some();
188  if active {
189    digest(T_CS!("\\@expl@@@filehook@file@push@@"))?;
190  }
191  Ok(active)
192}
193
194/// Whether `use_load_hooks` runs for this load: lthooks is loaded, and the
195/// name is a file name.
196fn load_hooks_active(name: &str) -> Result<bool> {
197  if lookup_definition(&T_CS!("\\UseHook"))?.is_none() {
198    return Ok(false);
199  }
200  // latex.ltx:18766-18827 runs no hooks for a file that does not exist
201  // (`\@missing@onefilewithoptions`); a request whose name is a control
202  // sequence (frankenstein lips/slemph/blkcntrl/achicago `\usepackage{…}`
203  // reached with `\aftergroup` as the name — already a `missing_file`
204  // error) would otherwise put that CS inside every `\csname __hook…` label,
205  // 12 more errors per document.
206  Ok(!name.contains('\\'))
207}
208
209/// TODO: Flesh out with the full infrastructure, incremental functionality for now.
210pub fn input_definitions(raw_file: &str, options: InputDefinitionOptions) -> Result<()> {
211  // K1 provenance: what this load defines is a pool (`.pool`), a raw format
212  // file, or a raw file the document loaded. A compiled binding found by the
213  // dispatch below narrows its own code to `Binding` (latexml_package /
214  // latexml_contrib `dispatch`), and its `InputDefinitions!(noltxml)` re-enters
215  // here as `File` for the real package's definitions. A file a fallback class requires while it guesses (OmniBus's
216  // `inst_support`, `aas_macros`) is part of that guess: it stays `Fallback`, so the document's own `\newcommand` of
217  // one of its names wins as it does over the class's (2603.21740 `\newcommand{\inst}{\mathcal I}`; OXIDIZED_DESIGN
218  // #408).
219  use crate::definition::origin::{DefinitionOrigin, OriginGuard, current_origin};
220  let requested_by_fallback = current_origin() == DefinitionOrigin::Fallback;
221  // (and a file a borrowed binding requires is borrowed with it: amsthm's amsgen, `require_package_borrowed`)
222  let requested_by_borrow = current_origin() == DefinitionOrigin::Borrowed;
223  // (a compiled binding's own code requests it — its `RequirePackage!`: `<file>:requested_by_binding`, which a repeat
224  // load's options read, `apply_new_options_on_reload`)
225  let requested_by_binding = current_origin() == DefinitionOrigin::Binding;
226  // (a raw file's own request — a copy's `\RequirePackage{natbib}`, neurips_2024.sty:71: the load TeX sees, which a
227  // binding's early load of the package only anticipated; `apply_new_options_on_reload`)
228  let requested_by_file = current_origin() == DefinitionOrigin::File;
229  // (a stand-in's copy's own request, read before its provides-mode is suspended for the load below — by the stand-in
230  // binding it is read after: `apply_repeat_options`; an elsarticle.cls copy's `\RequirePackage[square,…]{natbib}`,
231  // 1811.10063)
232  let requested_by_copy = copy_provides_binding();
233  let base = raw_file
234    .trim()
235    .rsplit(['/', '\\'])
236    .next()
237    .unwrap_or("")
238    .to_string();
239  let origin = if options.extension.as_deref() == Some("pool") || base.ends_with(".pool") {
240    DefinitionOrigin::Pool
241  } else if requested_by_fallback {
242    DefinitionOrigin::Fallback
243  } else if requested_by_borrow {
244    DefinitionOrigin::Borrowed
245  } else if matches!(
246    base.as_str(),
247    "latex.ltx" | "plain.tex" | "expl3-code.tex" | "latex" | "plain" | "expl3-code"
248  ) {
249    DefinitionOrigin::Format
250  } else {
251    DefinitionOrigin::File
252  };
253  let _origin = OriginGuard::new(origin);
254  // A class load is a frame whose definitions are the class's (`state::ClassLoadFrame`); one a stand-in's copy requests
255  // — its `\LoadClass`, or a class loaded while that is open — is a base class of the copy, whose names the real class
256  // has (the reviewer's probes newcmd_base, nc_chain).
257  let class = options.extension.as_deref() == Some("cls") || raw_file.ends_with(".cls");
258  let _class_frame = (class && options.handleoptions).then(|| {
259    let copy = crate::definition::origin::copy_provides();
260    ClassLoadFrame::enter(
261      raw_file,
262      if copy != 0 {
263        Some(copy)
264      } else {
265        open_copy_class_load()
266      },
267    )
268  });
269  // A package or class a stand-in's copy loads (`\RequirePackage`, `\usepackage`, `\LoadClass`) is a real load, read
270  // whole as TeX reads it: the copy's provides-mode is suspended for it (`read_stand_in_copy`). A file the copy pulls
271  // in by `\input` (`\@input`, `\InputIfFileExists`: the `.clo`/`.cfg` it ships) is part of the copy and follows its
272  // rule (main-session ruling 2026-10-18): svepj.clo:280-284's `\AtEndOfClass{\let\maketitle=\epj@maketitle}` and
273  // svepjc3.clo:57/:71's `\def\maketitle`, read through svjour.cls:86's `\InputIfFileExists{sv\CurrentOption.clo}`,
274  // replaced the binding's `\maketitle`, the affiliations lost to "Address(es) of author(s) should be given" (0901.1015,
275  // 1012.5412, 1111.3868, 1512.01957, 2411.18962, 2107.02119). (a binding the `\input` reaches is a load of its own:
276  // the binding branch of `input_definitions_impl`)
277  let _suspended = (crate::definition::origin::copy_provides() != 0 && options.handleoptions)
278    .then(|| crate::definition::origin::CopyProvidesGuard::new(0));
279  // A file a stand-in binding loads is not part of the binding's command set (`StandInLoadGuard::suspend`); a
280  // stand-in it loads records its own (`input_definitions_impl`).
281  // (the files a stand-in binding's own code loads are the files it is built on, which the paper's copy may declare
282  // itself: `copy_is_the_stood_in_file`)
283  if crate::definition::origin::stand_in_loading() && crate::definition::origin::stand_in_own_code()
284  {
285    note_stand_in_file(raw_file);
286  }
287  // (a class it loads is its base class, whose text defaults are not its own: `StandInLoadGuard::suspend_for`)
288  let _nested = crate::definition::origin::stand_in_loading()
289    .then(|| crate::definition::origin::StandInLoadGuard::suspend_for(class));
290  input_definitions_impl(
291    raw_file,
292    options,
293    requested_by_binding,
294    requested_by_file,
295    requested_by_copy,
296  )
297}
298
299/// Is the file now being read one the author shipped: under the document's own source directory
300/// (`SOURCEDIRECTORY`), not the TeX Live tree? K22's ownership rule — the author's files outrank our guesses — reads
301/// it for a fallback meaning (`is_definable_latex`): a `\newcommand` in the paper's own `.sty` replaces a binding's
302/// guess as the document's does (0901.4335's `mn2e_journal_style.sty` `\newcommand{\araa}{ARAA}`).
303pub fn reading_author_file() -> bool {
304  let dir = lookup_string("SOURCEDIRECTORY");
305  if dir.is_empty() {
306    return false;
307  }
308  let source = arena::to_string(gullet::get_locator().get_source());
309  if source.is_empty() {
310    return false;
311  }
312  let canonical = |p: &str| std::fs::canonicalize(p).unwrap_or_else(|_| Path::new(p).to_path_buf());
313  canonical(&source).starts_with(canonical(&dir))
314}
315
316fn input_definitions_impl(
317  raw_file: &str,
318  mut options: InputDefinitionOptions,
319  requested_by_binding: bool,
320  requested_by_file: bool,
321  requested_by_copy: String,
322) -> Result<()> {
323  let trimmed = raw_file.trim();
324  // A compiled binding keyed by BASENAME raw-loads its own name (`noltxml`), but
325  // the dispatch dropped any directory the user wrote — so `\usepackage{DIR/pkg}`
326  // would raw-load a bare `pkg.sty` and miss the author's bundled `DIR/pkg.sty`.
327  // `\@currname` still holds the full request (`DIR/pkg`) from the OUTER load
328  // (set at `before_input_handle_options`, later than here), so load the file the
329  // user actually asked for — exactly as Perl does (it has no such binding and
330  // raw-loads the dir-prefixed path directly, 0 errors; witness 2510.09534,
331  // `\usepackage[preprint]{AISTATS/aistats2026}`). Scoped to `noltxml` + a bare
332  // basename that matches `\@currname`'s basename, so an unrelated `\input` in the
333  // package is NOT auto-resolved in the subdir (stock LaTeX does not do that —
334  // that is what the `import` package is for). This replaces the former
335  // SearchPathGuard, which injected the subdir into SEARCHPATHS instead.
336  let currname_request: Option<String> = if options.noltxml && !trimmed.contains(['/', '\\']) {
337    do_expand(T_CS!("\\@currname"))
338      .ok()
339      .map(|toks| toks.to_string())
340      .filter(|currname| {
341        currname
342          .rsplit_once(['/', '\\'])
343          .is_some_and(|(dir, base)| base == trimmed && !dir.is_empty())
344      })
345  } else {
346    None
347  };
348  let name: &str = currname_request.as_deref().unwrap_or(trimmed);
349  // A re-read (`input`) is raw-only, but not a binding loading its own name.
350  if options.reread {
351    options.noltxml = true;
352  }
353
354  // Guard: prevent infinite recursion from circular or runaway package loading.
355  // When a binding is missing, raw TeX loading can trigger macro loops.
356  let depth = INPUT_DEPTH.with(|d| {
357    let current = d.get();
358    d.set(current + 1);
359    current + 1
360  });
361  if depth > MAX_INPUT_DEPTH {
362    INPUT_DEPTH.with(|d| d.set(d.get() - 1));
363    Fatal!(
364      Stomach,
365      Recursion,
366      s!(
367        "Package loading depth exceeded {} (loading '{}').\
368        This usually means a missing binding causes infinite recursion.",
369        MAX_INPUT_DEPTH,
370        name
371      )
372    );
373  }
374
375  // Ensure depth cleanup on all exit paths via a guard
376  struct InputDepthGuard;
377  impl Drop for InputDepthGuard {
378    fn drop(&mut self) { INPUT_DEPTH.with(|d| d.set(d.get() - 1)); }
379  }
380  let _guard = InputDepthGuard;
381
382  // (A directory-prefixed package name — `\usepackage{DIR/pkg}` — that dispatches
383  // to a basename-keyed binding is resolved above by rewriting `name` to the
384  // `\@currname` request, so the binding's own raw-load targets `DIR/pkg`
385  // directly, as Perl does. No SEARCHPATHS injection is needed.)
386
387  // Note: we always need a gullet to expand, and we sometimes need a stomach to load_definitions...
388  // so let's make stomach a mandatory option.
389  //
390  // Snapshot \@currname/\@currext only when handleoptions=true. Perl
391  // Package.pm:2549-2550 does the same — both prevname and prevext are
392  // gated on options{handleoptions}. The handleoptions=false branch
393  // does NOT mutate \@currname/\@currext (mirrors plain LaTeX `\input`
394  // semantics; Perl L2580 likewise only mutates them inside its
395  // handleoptions=true branch).
396  let prevname = if options.handleoptions && lookup_definition(&T_CS!("\\@currname"))?.is_some() {
397    do_expand(T_CS!("\\@currname"))?.to_string()
398  } else {
399    String::new()
400  };
401  let prevext = if options.handleoptions && lookup_definition(&T_CS!("\\@currext"))?.is_some() {
402    do_expand(T_CS!("\\@currext"))?.to_string()
403  } else {
404    String::new()
405  };
406  // This file will be treated somewhat as if it were a class
407  // IF as_class is true
408  // OR if it is loaded by such a class, and has withoptions true!!! (yikes)
409  if options.handleoptions && options.withoptions.is_some() {
410    with_vecdeque("@masquerading@as@class", |vdq_opt| {
411      if let Some(vdq) = vdq_opt
412        && vdq.iter().any(|x| {
413          if let Stored::String(v) = x {
414            arena::with(*v, |str| str == prevname)
415          } else {
416            false
417          }
418        })
419      {
420        options.as_class = true;
421      }
422    });
423  }
424  if options.noltxml {
425    options.raw = true; // so it will be read as raw by Gullet.
426  }
427  let as_type = if options.as_class {
428    Cow::Borrowed("cls")
429  } else {
430    options
431      .extension
432      .as_ref()
433      .cloned()
434      .unwrap_or(Cow::Borrowed(""))
435  };
436
437  // If loading the document class, record its options as the global ones
438  // (Perl Package.pm:2578-2581). latex.ltx sets `\@classoptionslist` and
439  // `\@raw@classoptionslist` only while `\@classoptionslist` is `\relax`
440  // (latex.ltx:18716-18718): at the first class load, `\documentclass`. A class
441  // that class loads keeps its options to itself (`\opt@<name>.cls`).
442  //
443  // DIVERGENCE from Perl (#334, KPE #304): Rust models "first" as the
444  // outermost `load_class` (`nested_class`). Perl pushes every cls load's
445  // options, so a class's `\LoadClass[fleqn]{article}` handed amsmath a global
446  // `fleqn` and replaced `\@classoptionslist` (webofc 2605.12407, USG
447  // 2605.00042; pdflatex centres their equations and keeps the document's
448  // list). A binding's base class (amsart → ams_core, loaded with no options)
449  // is nested as well, so it cannot empty the list either: babel reads a
450  // global language from it (`\bbl@foreach`, babel.sty L4270; witness
451  // 1911.07001, `[oneside,french,titlepage]{amsart}` + bare
452  // `\usepackage{babel}` → french.ldf must load).
453  //
454  // DIVERGENCE retained from Perl: still define `\@classoptionslist` as EMPTY
455  // for an option-less document class so babel's
456  // `\csname\ds@\@classoptionslist\endcsname` doesn't run away (the kernel
457  // default `\let\@classoptionslist\relax`; witness 2504.00009). The "no class
458  // options recorded yet" gate keeps a later outermost load without options
459  // (an unbound class's alternate, a class after a `--preload`ed one) from
460  // emptying a populated list.
461  if as_type == "cls" && !options.nested_class {
462    for opt in &options.options {
463      push_value("class_options", arena::pin(opt))?;
464    }
465    let class_opts_str = options.options.join(",");
466    let have_recorded_opts = lookup_vecdeque("class_options")
467      .map(|v| !v.is_empty())
468      .unwrap_or(false);
469    // Both lists are STRING-tokenized with standard catcodes (letters 11),
470    // as Perl's `DefMacroI('\@classoptionslist', undef, join(',', …))`
471    // (Package.pm L2564 → TokenizeInternal) and as `\documentclass`'s real
472    // argument read. `Explode!` (every char OTHER) broke every `\ifx` value
473    // match downstream: tudapub.cls L173/L358 forwards an unknown class
474    // option (`parskip=half-`) verbatim to `\KOMAoption`, and scrbase.sty
475    // L2354 `\FamilySetNumerical` `\ifx`-compares it against the literal
476    // `half-` from scrbook.cls L825 → "unknown value" (witness
477    // DEMO-TUDaPhD/TUDaThesis; guard
478    // `perfect_kernel_batch53::classoptionslist_has_letter_catcodes`).
479    if !class_opts_str.is_empty() || !have_recorded_opts {
480      def_macro(
481        T_CS!("\\@classoptionslist"),
482        None,
483        crate::mouth::tokenize_internal(TeXString::assembled(class_opts_str.clone())),
484        None,
485      )?;
486    }
487    // OXIDIZED_DESIGN #164 (class half): the kernel ALSO records the raw,
488    // unprocessed option text of the DOCUMENT class in
489    // `\@raw@classoptionslist` (latex.ltx L18718, guarded to the first
490    // `\documentclass` via `\ifx\@classoptionslist\relax`). Modern babel
491    // (babel.sty L4199 `\bbl@foreach\@raw@classoptionslist`) reads THAT — not
492    // `\@classoptionslist` — to pick up global language options, so without
493    // it `\documentclass[french]{article}` + `\usepackage{babel}` loads
494    // nil.ldf and every French shorthand (`\og`/`\fg`) is undefined (TL doc
495    // corpus: 4+ French bundles; Perl 0.8.8 records it nowhere either).
496    // (`class_options` was pushed just above, so `have_recorded_opts` cannot
497    // gate this — use a dedicated once-flag for the kernel's
498    // first-`\documentclass`-only semantics.)
499    if options.handleoptions && !lookup_bool("@raw@classoptionslist_recorded") {
500      assign_value("@raw@classoptionslist_recorded", true, Some(Scope::Global));
501      def_macro(
502        T_CS!("\\@raw@classoptionslist"),
503        None,
504        crate::mouth::tokenize_internal(TeXString::assembled(class_opts_str.clone())),
505        Some(ExpandableOptions {
506          scope: Some(Scope::Global),
507          ..ExpandableOptions::default()
508        }),
509      )?;
510    }
511  }
512
513  // Compute the exact name based on the type
514  let filename = match &options.extension {
515    None => name.to_string(),
516    Some(ext) => s!("{}.{}", name, ext),
517  };
518  // Store the document class filename for xkeyval's isInClassFile check
519  if as_type == "cls" && options.handleoptions {
520    assign_value(
521      "document_class_filename",
522      filename.clone(),
523      Some(Scope::Global),
524    );
525  }
526  let current_options = options.options.join(",");
527  if !current_options.is_empty()
528    && !lookup_bool(&s!("{filename}_unmarks_itself"))
529    && let Some(Stored::String(prevoptions)) = lookup_value(&s!("{filename}_loaded_with_options"))
530    && arena::with(prevoptions, |prev_str| current_options != prev_str)
531  {
532    let message = s!(
533      "Option clash for file {} with options {:?}, previously loaded with {:?}",
534      filename,
535      current_options,
536      arena::to_string(prevoptions)
537    );
538    Info!("unexpected", "options", message);
539  }
540
541  // TODO: This needs reorganization, bindings are not found as "files" in rust,
542  // we need to have a registry (we don't yet)
543
544  // Perl: early-stop if already loaded (checks request_loaded, name_loaded, etc.)
545  // This prevents double-loading and breaks circular loading chains.
546  // IMPORTANT: check BEFORE printing "Loading..." message to avoid spurious output.
547  //
548  // Per OXIDIZED_DESIGN #23: gate on the flag matching the load path
549  // we'll actually take. CRITICAL invariant: a binding `<file>.rs` is
550  // allowed to call `InputDefinitions(noltxml=>1)` for its same-named
551  // raw .sty/.cls/.def AFTER its own `_loaded` flag was set — the raw
552  // load gates on `_raw_loaded`, not `_loaded`. Examples: babel_sty
553  // → raw babel.sty; cite_sty → raw cite.sty.
554  let opt_noltxml = options.noltxml;
555  let opt_notex = options.notex;
556  // Rust-only `_load_attempted` flag: set in the miss-handler below to
557  // prevent retry loops while keeping `_loaded` reserved for genuine
558  // binding success. Without this split the `_loaded`-on-miss hack
559  // shadowed `require_package`'s `!_loaded && !_raw_loaded`
560  // post-call check, disabling `maybe_require_dependencies` for any
561  // package that had no binding (e.g. paper-local `jinstpub.sty`).
562  let already_handled = |fkey: &str| -> bool {
563    // A file that un-marks itself loaded at its end (fontenc.sty tail:
564    // `\global\let\ver@fontenc.sty\relax`, `\let\opt@fontenc.sty\relax`)
565    // is loaded afresh by every request, each time with the new options —
566    // its binding declares that with `<file>_unmarks_itself` (fontenc_sty.rs).
567    if lookup_bool(&s!("{fkey}_unmarks_itself")) {
568      return false;
569    }
570    if opt_noltxml {
571      lookup_bool(&s!("{fkey}_raw_loaded"))
572    } else if opt_notex {
573      lookup_bool(&s!("{fkey}_loaded")) || lookup_bool(&s!("{fkey}_load_attempted"))
574    } else {
575      lookup_bool(&s!("{fkey}_loaded"))
576        || lookup_bool(&s!("{fkey}_raw_loaded"))
577        || lookup_bool(&s!("{fkey}_load_attempted"))
578    }
579  };
580  // Modern-kernel repeat-load option semantics (OXIDIZED_DESIGN #43).
581  // Classic \DeclareOption packages: after ProcessOptions the `\ds@<opt>`
582  // handlers are \relax, so digesting them on a repeat load is a NO-OP —
583  // i.e. LaTeX's own clash-and-drop outcome (and Perl's silent skip; the
584  // Info above is the shared diagnostic). Packages using the key-value
585  // option processor (\ProcessKeyOptions, LaTeX kernel 2022-06+; e.g.
586  // xcolor v3.02 whose `table` key loads colortbl) raise NO clash in real
587  // LaTeX — the repeat load PROCESSES the new keys. A binding models that
588  // by re-asserting a durable `\ds@<opt>` after ProcessOptions! (first
589  // adopter: xcolor's `table`; witness 2605.00310 — \usepackage{xcolor}
590  // then \usepackage[table]{xcolor} builds cleanly under pdflatex, and a
591  // ~483-paper \cellcolor error cluster in sandbox-arxiv-2605). For each
592  // option of a repeat load that the first load did not have, digest the
593  // surviving handler.
594  //
595  // A key-value package whose binding names its setter in `<file>_keyval_setter` (hyperref's
596  // `\hypersetup`) takes the new options of a repeat load through that setter, as its keys. This is the
597  // load a document makes after a class binding has loaded the package where the real class does not:
598  // revtex4_support, elsarticle, PoS, jmlr and sagej load hyperref, so the author's
599  // `\usepackage[colorlinks]{hyperref}` is the first hyperref load real LaTeX sees, and colorlinks loads
600  // color (hyperref.sty:4532-4537). Witnesses 2609.24844, 2609.13110, 1711.02465 (`\textcolor`).
601  let apply_repeat_options = |fkey: &str| -> Result<()> {
602    if options.options.is_empty() || !options.handleoptions {
603      return Ok(());
604    }
605    let mut prev: HashSet<String> =
606      if let Some(Stored::String(prevoptions)) = lookup_value(&s!("{fkey}_loaded_with_options")) {
607        arena::with(prevoptions, |p| {
608          p.split(',').map(|o| o.trim().to_string()).collect()
609        })
610      } else {
611        HashSet::default()
612      };
613    // (and the options passed to it, `\opt@<file>`, which LaTeX's clash test reads, latex.ltx `\@if@ptions`:
614    // 2110.14346's `\PassOptionsToPackage{numbers, compress}{natbib}` before neurips_2020.sty's load, then
615    // `\usepackage[numbers]{natbib}` — no clash in pdflatex)
616    if let Ok(Some(defn)) = lookup_definition(&T_CS!(s!("\\opt@{fkey}")))
617      && let Some(ExpansionBody::Tokens(body)) = defn.get_expansion()
618    {
619      prev.extend(
620        body
621          .to_string()
622          .split(',')
623          .map(|o| o.trim().to_string())
624          .filter(|o| !o.is_empty()),
625      );
626    }
627    if let Some(Stored::String(setter)) = lookup_value(&s!("{fkey}_keyval_setter")) {
628      let new_options: Vec<&str> = options
629        .options
630        .iter()
631        .map(|opt| opt.trim())
632        .filter(|opt| !opt.is_empty() && !prev.contains(*opt))
633        .collect();
634      // A package with classic options whose binding opts in (`<file>_repeat_clash`, natbib) takes a repeat load's
635      // new options only where TeX would see them: the first load was a compiled binding's own (`<file>:requested_by_
636      // binding`; a class binding loading natbib where the real class loads it later with options, jheppub.sty:37's
637      // `[numbers,sort&compress]`: 2112.14648, 2607.20603), or the request carries a renamed copy's own options
638      // (`<file>:options_of_a_renamed_copy`: NJDnatbib, whose own `\ProcessOptions` runs in TeX; 2506.14995). Otherwise
639      // the first load was the document's world's, and LaTeX keeps its options and stops with "Option clash"
640      // (latex.ltx `\@onefilewithoptions` → `\@if@ptions`), an Error here as there; the new options are dropped.
641      // A class binding that loads the package where the real class does too (jmlr.cls:150, acmart.cls:300,
642      // elsarticle.cls:1243, jheppub.sty:37) records its load as the class's own (`<file>:requested_by_binding` false);
643      // a raw file's own repeat ends the early-load state (`apply_new_options_on_reload` below).
644      // A stand-in's copy that loads the package its binding loaded on the copy's behalf (`<file>:loaded_by_stand_in`,
645      // the first load made while a stand-in binding loaded) makes the load the binding stands in for: TeX sees the
646      // copy's only, so its options apply (a renamed elsarticle.cls copy's `\RequirePackage[square]{natbib}`,
647      // 1811.10063).
648      let copys_own_load = !requested_by_copy.is_empty()
649        && lookup_string(&s!("{fkey}:loaded_by_stand_in")).eq_ignore_ascii_case(&requested_by_copy);
650      if !new_options.is_empty()
651        && lookup_bool(&s!("{fkey}_repeat_clash"))
652        && !lookup_bool(&s!("{fkey}:requested_by_binding"))
653        && !lookup_bool(&s!("{fkey}:options_of_a_renamed_copy"))
654        && !copys_own_load
655      {
656        let package = fkey.rsplit_once('.').map_or(fkey, |(stem, _)| stem);
657        Error!(
658          "latex",
659          "options",
660          s!(
661            "Option clash for package {package}: options '{}' of a repeat load dropped, as LaTeX keeps the first \
662             load's",
663            new_options.join(",")
664          )
665        );
666        return Ok(());
667      }
668      if !new_options.is_empty() {
669        let keyvals = new_options.join(",");
670        Info!(
671          "unexpected",
672          "options",
673          s!(
674            "Applying options '{}' from a repeat load of {}",
675            keyvals,
676            fkey
677          )
678        );
679        let mut tokens = vec![T_CS!(arena::to_string(setter)), T_BEGIN!()];
680        tokens.extend(crate::mouth::tokenize_internal(TeXString::assembled(keyvals)).unlist());
681        tokens.push(T_END!());
682        digest(Tokens::new(tokens))?;
683      }
684      return Ok(());
685    }
686    for opt in &options.options {
687      let opt = opt.trim();
688      if opt.is_empty() || prev.contains(opt) {
689        continue;
690      }
691      // A binding's handler of its own, for a package whose options are keys that outlive its load (xcolor,
692      // `\lx@repeat@xcolor@<opt>`), before the classic `\ds@<opt>`, which `\ProcessOptions` clears and which is any
693      // package's name.
694      let package = fkey.rsplit_once('.').map_or(fkey, |(stem, _)| stem);
695      let own_handler = T_CS!(s!("\\lx@repeat@{package}@{opt}"));
696      let handler = if lookup_definition(&own_handler)?.is_some() {
697        own_handler
698      } else {
699        T_CS!(s!("\\ds@{}", opt))
700      };
701      if lookup_definition(&handler)?.is_some() {
702        Info!(
703          "unexpected",
704          "options",
705          s!("Applying option '{}' from a repeat load of {}", opt, fkey)
706        );
707        digest(Tokens!(handler))?;
708      }
709    }
710    Ok(())
711  };
712  // A raw file's own repeat of a package whose binding opts in (`<file>_repeat_clash`, natbib) after a compiled
713  // binding's early load is the load TeX sees: a stand-in's copy that loads natbib itself (neurips_2024.sty:71
714  // `\RequirePackage{natbib}`, icml2024.sty:95; the reviewer's probes neurips_rep, icml_rep). Its options apply as
715  // above and join the first load's, and the early-load state ends (`<file>:requested_by_binding` false): a later
716  // `\usepackage[numbers]{natbib}` of the document is LaTeX's "Option clash", as pdflatex reports it, while a copy that
717  // comments its load out leaves the binding's early load in place (2506.00573, 2506.01987, 2506.08967, 2506.17103,
718  // 2506.18071, 2506.22567: the document's options apply).
719  let apply_new_options_on_reload = |fkey: &str| -> Result<()> {
720    let file_repeat = options.handleoptions
721      && requested_by_file
722      && lookup_bool(&s!("{fkey}_repeat_clash"))
723      && lookup_bool(&s!("{fkey}:requested_by_binding"));
724    apply_repeat_options(fkey)?;
725    if file_repeat {
726      let key = s!("{fkey}_loaded_with_options");
727      let mut loaded: Vec<String> = match lookup_value(&key) {
728        Some(Stored::String(prev)) => arena::with(prev, |p| {
729          p.split(',')
730            .map(|o| o.trim().to_string())
731            .filter(|o| !o.is_empty())
732            .collect()
733        }),
734        _ => Vec::new(),
735      };
736      for opt in &options.options {
737        let opt = opt.trim();
738        if !opt.is_empty() && !loaded.iter().any(|o| o == opt) {
739          loaded.push(opt.to_string());
740        }
741      }
742      if !loaded.is_empty() {
743        assign_value(&key, loaded.join(","), Some(Scope::Global));
744      }
745      assign_value(
746        &s!("{fkey}:requested_by_binding"),
747        false,
748        Some(Scope::Global),
749      );
750    }
751    Ok(())
752  };
753  // A file a class binding marked loaded on its class's behalf (`<file>:class_marked`, amsart's amsthm) and the
754  // document then unloaded (`\let\ver@<file>\relax`, `state::unmarked_by_document`) loads afresh, as LaTeX's
755  // `\@ifl@aded` finds it not loaded; from then on it is the document's load, no longer the class's mark.
756  if options.handleoptions && unmarked_by_document(&filename) {
757    mark_unloaded(&filename);
758    assign_value(&s!("{filename}:class_marked"), false, Some(Scope::Global));
759  }
760  if !options.reloadable && already_handled(&filename) {
761    // A request our records skip leaves the file loaded, as LaTeX's load would: a mark the document took back since
762    // (`\let\ver@<file>\relax`) is restored, so `state::file_loaded` answers as the loader did (OD #1015).
763    if options.handleoptions && load_recorded(&filename) && !file_loaded(&filename) {
764      mark_version(&filename)?;
765    }
766    apply_new_options_on_reload(&filename)?;
767    return Ok(());
768  }
769  // Also check without extension (Perl checks name_loaded too)
770  if !options.reloadable && name != filename && already_handled(name) {
771    apply_new_options_on_reload(&filename)?;
772    return Ok(());
773  }
774
775  // LaTeX kernel package disablement: \disable@package@load{<name>}{<action>}
776  // stores the action in `\@pkg-disable@<name>.<ext>` (latex.ltx:18771, 19829-19837).
777  // If defined, execute the action (warning/error) and ignore the load request.
778  let disable_cs = T_CS!(s!("\\@pkg-disable@{}", filename));
779  let fallback_cs = T_CS!(s!("\\@pkg-disable@{}", name));
780  let target = if lookup_definition(&disable_cs)?.is_some() {
781    Some(disable_cs)
782  } else if lookup_definition(&fallback_cs)?.is_some() {
783    Some(fallback_cs)
784  } else {
785    None
786  };
787  if let Some(cs) = target {
788    digest(Tokens!(cs))?;
789    Info!(
790      "latex",
791      "disable@package",
792      s!("Package '{filename}' has been disabled. Load request ignored")
793    );
794    // latex.ltx:18771 skips the load without setting `\ver@<file>`, so the
795    // package stays NOT loaded (`\@ifpackageloaded` false; `\reenable@package@load`
796    // + a later request loads it). The hook itself answers every repeat request.
797    return Ok(());
798  }
799
800  // The per-file load note is emitted ONLY when a load truly happens, so
801  // CorTeX's `loaded_file` extraction (which parses the log) counts genuine
802  // loads and nothing else:
803  //   * a NATIVE BINDING load announces `(Loading <name>…)` at its success
804  //     point below (Perl loadLTXML "(Loading <path>…)" analog);
805  //   * a RAW .sty/.cls/.def load is announced by its Mouth's
806  //     `(Processing definitions <path>…)` (fires only on a real read);
807  //   * the early-return paths — already-loaded skip and missing-file — reach
808  //     NEITHER, so they emit no note (matching Perl, whose loadLTXML returns
809  //     before its note when the binding was already loaded).
810  // (Previously a `(Loading …)` banner was emitted unconditionally here,
811  // before those early-returns — over-reporting already-loaded/missing files.)
812
813  // Snapshot options.after / options.options BEFORE handleoptions consumes
814  // them so the fallback-binding recursive call (Step 3 below) can forward
815  // both to the fallback. Without this snapshot, mn1 → mn.cls.ltxml fallback
816  // ran with empty options/after and the user's `[epsfig]` was lost (see
817  // astro-ph0002213 root cause).
818  let original_after = options.after.clone();
819  let original_options = options.options.clone();
820  // Snapshot the GRANDPARENT's expl3 state BEFORE `\@pushfilename`'s
821  // `\ExplSyntaxOff` flips `_` to SUB. The post-load cleanup hook in
822  // load_tex_definitions uses this to know whether the calling context
823  // was in expl3 mode (so it can skip the `\ExplSyntaxOff` cleanup that
824  // would otherwise stick post-`\@popfilename`). Witness cluster:
825  // arXiv:2509.05997 / .07893 / .02344, 2510.13206/.13942/.17317
826  // (xsavebox + sys_load_backend + l3backend-dvips.def chain — minimal
827  // repro: \usepackage{xsavebox}).
828  let self_snapshot = lookup_catcode('_') == Some(Catcode::LETTER);
829  // Inherit the immediate parent frame's flag only when the parent WAS in expl3 but
830  // our own fresh read is false — the double-nested same-file case (an outer
831  // `\RequirePackage{X}` whose X-binding force-raw-loads its own expl3 .sty, so this
832  // inner call snapshots after the outer `\@pushfilename` already flipped `_` to SUB).
833  // Never overrides a correct fresh read, so xsavebox/tcolorbox/lipsum are unaffected.
834  let grandparent_in_expl3 = INPUT_DEF_EXPL3
835    .with(|s| {
836      s.borrow()
837        .last()
838        .copied()
839        .filter(|&parent| parent && !self_snapshot)
840    })
841    .unwrap_or(self_snapshot);
842  INPUT_DEF_EXPL3.with(|s| s.borrow_mut().push(grandparent_in_expl3));
843  struct Expl3FrameGuard;
844  impl Drop for Expl3FrameGuard {
845    fn drop(&mut self) {
846      INPUT_DEF_EXPL3.with(|s| {
847        s.borrow_mut().pop();
848      });
849    }
850  }
851  let _expl3_frame = Expl3FrameGuard;
852  // Strict-LaTeX-kernel order (latex.ltx `\@onefilewithoptions`, L15518-L15519):
853  //   \@pushfilename                        % capture OLD \@currname / \@currext
854  //   \xdef\@currname{ <new name> }         % then update to NEW
855  //
856  // We previously set \@currname/\@currext to the new file's name BEFORE
857  // calling `before_input_handle_options` (which performs the push). That
858  // captured the NEW name in the pushed triple, so `\@currnamestack` never
859  // held the empty `{}{}{<catcode>}` initial-state triple that
860  // expl3-code.tex's `\__file_tmp:w` recursion uses as its termination
861  // sentinel. Result: under raw expl3-code.tex load (LATEXML_NODUMP=1),
862  // the recursion ate past `\group_end:` into subsequent
863  // `\seq_new:N` / `\cs_new:Npn …` lines, producing the cs_end:
864  // cascade documented in .investigation/cs_end_bisect_round22/.
865  //
866  // Now: push first (uses current/OLD \@currname), then update inside
867  // before_input_handle_options (line 756-757). For the
868  // `handleoptions == false` path no push happens, so set the names
869  // directly here.
870  // Perl Package.pm:2578 computes `$pushpop` ONCE, before the push, and reuses it
871  // for the pop (:2637). A load that itself brings in the LaTeX pool + dump (a
872  // `--preload` package, `expl3_sty.rs`'s `LoadPool!("LaTeX")`) pushes with the
873  // native `\lx@pushfilename` (no `\@pushfilename` yet) but, re-checked after the
874  // load, would pop with the dump's `\@popfilename` → `\__hook_curr_name_pop:` on
875  // an empty `\g__hook_name_stack_seq` → "Extra \PopDefaultHookLabel" (counted
876  // `\errmessage` since batch 56g). Witness: every `[luatex]` profile run.
877  // Guard: `perfect_kernel_batch56::preload_that_pulls_in_the_format_pops_with_the_native_stack`.
878  let mut pushpop = false;
879  let mut file_stack_pushed = false;
880  if options.handleoptions {
881    pushpop = before_input_handle_options(&mut options, &prevname, &prevext, name, &as_type)?;
882    def_macro(
883      T_CS!(s!("\\{}.{}-h@@k", name, as_type)),
884      None,
885      options.after,
886      None,
887    )?;
888    // `\@raw@opt@<name>.<ext>` (OXIDIZED_DESIGN #164) is written by
889    // `pass_options`, which `before_input_handle_options` just called for the
890    // explicit `[…]` list, as the kernel's `\load@onefile@withoptions` calls
891    // `\@pass@ptions` (latex.ltx:18832).
892    file_stack_pushed = push_load_file_stack(name)?;
893    use_load_hooks(name, &as_type, "before")?;
894  }
895  // No `else` branch: Perl Package.pm L2580-2611 only mutates
896  // \@currname/\@currext inside the handleoptions=true block. The
897  // handleoptions=false path mirrors plain LaTeX `\input`, which leaves
898  // them untouched. Mutating them here breaks \@currnamestack
899  // discipline: a subsequent inner \RequirePackage's \@pushfilename
900  // captures the leaked name instead of the empty initial-state value,
901  // and expl3-code.tex's \__file_tmp:w stack walk over-reads.
902  // Witnesses: 0805.4519 (inputenc+ansinew), 1705.00041
903  // (\usetikzlibrary{calligraphy}+spath3+expl3).
904
905  if !current_options.is_empty() {
906    assign_value(
907      &s!("{}_loaded_with_options", filename),
908      current_options,
909      Some(Scope::Global),
910    );
911  }
912  if options.handleoptions {
913    // Who asked for the first load: a compiled binding's own code (its `RequirePackage!`, where the real class may load
914    // the package later, or with options, or not at all) or the document's world (a raw file, the document). A repeat
915    // load's options read it (`apply_new_options_on_reload`).
916    assign_value(
917      &s!("{filename}:requested_by_binding"),
918      requested_by_binding,
919      Some(Scope::Global),
920    );
921    // (and which stand-in binding was loading, the innermost: its copy's own load of the package is that load, and
922    // no other copy's, `apply_repeat_options`)
923    let stand_in = if crate::definition::origin::stand_in_loading() {
924      STAND_IN_BINDINGS.with(|names| names.borrow().last().cloned().unwrap_or_default())
925    } else {
926      String::new()
927    };
928    assign_value(
929      &s!("{filename}:loaded_by_stand_in"),
930      stand_in,
931      Some(Scope::Global),
932    );
933  }
934
935  // Track loaded files in \@filelist BEFORE loading (Perl: Package.pm calls
936  // \@addtofilelist before reading the file, so \@filelist is available inside)
937  //
938  // OXIDIZED_DESIGN #166: the kernel stores filelist names through
939  // `\string@makeletter` (latex.ltx L1784-1789), whose `\char@if@alph`
940  // (L1790-1795) tests the ASCII ranges A-Z / a-z ONLY: those characters get
941  // catcode LETTER (`\@expl@char@generate@@nn{`#1}{11}`), everything else —
942  // dots, digits, commas, and non-ASCII alike — keeps its `\string` catcode
943  // (OTHER). Reproduce that rule exactly (NOT `ExplodeText!`, whose
944  // Unicode-`is_alphabetic` letters more than the kernel does).
945  // All-OTHER tokens (`Explode!`) broke every delimited parse of
946  // `\@filelist` whose delimiter is source-typed letters: hep-font.sty's
947  // `\def\hepfont@get@class#1.cls#2\relax` matched nothing (letter `cls`
948  // vs other `cls`), and the resulting empty/mis-split arguments corrupted
949  // the surrounding conditional bookkeeping — the 13-bundle
950  // `expected:\fi` hep-* cluster (2026-08-31 corpus).
951  if options.handleoptions && lookup_definition(&T_CS!("\\@addtofilelist"))?.is_some() {
952    let name_tokens: Vec<Token> = filename
953      .chars()
954      .map(|c| {
955        let mut tmp = [0u8; 4];
956        let s = c.encode_utf8(&mut tmp);
957        if c.is_ascii_alphabetic() {
958          T_LETTER!(s)
959        } else if c == ' ' {
960          T_SPACE!()
961        } else {
962          T_OTHER!(s)
963        }
964      })
965      .collect();
966    digest(Tokens!(
967      T_CS!("\\@addtofilelist"),
968      T_BEGIN!(),
969      name_tokens,
970      T_END!()
971    ))?;
972  }
973
974  // Skip loading entirely if already loaded (unless reloadable)
975  // This prevents double-loading when e.g. smfart calls load_class("amsart")
976  // after the binding already set the _loaded flag.
977  // Per OXIDIZED_DESIGN #23: gate by the load path's flag — same
978  // path-aware logic as the early-skip above. Allows a binding to
979  // load its same-named raw counterpart via `noltxml=>1`.
980  if !options.reloadable && already_handled(&filename) {
981    // Already-loaded early return: emit no load note (nothing was loaded).
982    return Ok(());
983  }
984
985  // Catch Fatal errors during binding loading (e.g., token limit exceeded during
986  // expl3 kernel loading). Convert to non-fatal so document processing continues.
987  // `Some(source)` = a binding was loaded, carrying its on-disk source path
988  // when it is a runtime file binding (`.rhai`) or `None` for a compiled-in one;
989  // outer `None` = no binding loaded. The two `_load_binding` attempts (extra
990  // slot, then the main resolving chain) short-circuit on the first that loads.
991  // K11 (`latexml_engine/src/frontmatter_stores.rs`) tells a binding's own title-page setters from
992  // a raw class's stores by what changed during the binding's load (`store_setters`).
993  let setters_before = if options.noltxml {
994    None
995  } else {
996    Some(crate::binding::store_setters::snapshot()?)
997  };
998  // (the classes a binding loaded now reads raw itself, for K11 at this load's end)
999  let raw_class_reads_before = UNCLAIMED_RAW_CLASS_READS.get();
1000  let loading_mark = (options.handleoptions && !options.noltxml)
1001    .then(|| mark_loading(&filename))
1002    .flatten();
1003  let loaded = if options.noltxml {
1004    None
1005  } else {
1006    // (what a binding standing in for an author's file defines is its own, which the paper's copy read after it only
1007    // provides: `read_stand_in_copy`)
1008    // (and so does the binding a shipped copy's declaration routed its request to: `provided_binding`)
1009    let stands_in = crate::binding::author_files::stands_in_for_an_author_file(&filename)
1010      || loading_for_a_provides_route(name);
1011    let _stand_in = stands_in.then(|| {
1012      crate::definition::origin::StandInLoadGuard::loaded_by(
1013        crate::definition::origin::stand_in_loader_own_code(),
1014      )
1015    });
1016    // (its name, for the packages its load loads first: `<file>:loaded_by_stand_in`)
1017    let _stand_in_name = stands_in.then(|| StandInBindingName::enter(file_stem(&filename)));
1018    // (a binding a stand-in's copy reaches by `\input` is a load of its own, not the copy's code: `input_definitions`)
1019    let _copy_suspended = (crate::definition::origin::copy_provides() != 0)
1020      .then(|| crate::definition::origin::CopyProvidesGuard::new(0));
1021    // (a binding is LaTeXML's, whichever file requires it: xkeyval_sty.rs under acmcopyright.sty, whose `\XKV@s@tkeys` a
1022    // `\setcopyright{acmlicensed}` calls, 2105.13838; a raw file it reads says for itself)
1023    let _not_own_file = OwnFileGuard::new(false);
1024    match _load_binding(false, &filename, options.reloadable).and_then(|ext| match ext {
1025      Some(source) => Ok(Some(source)),
1026      None => _load_binding(true, &filename, options.reloadable),
1027    }) {
1028      Ok(v) => v,
1029      Err(e) if get_status(LogStatus::Fatal) > 0 => {
1030        // A binding that raised a Fatal (ajmacros.sty's pTeX bail, a
1031        // resource cap) aborts the conversion; the top level logs it once.
1032        return Err(e);
1033      },
1034      Err(e) => {
1035        Error!(
1036          "unexpected",
1037          &filename,
1038          s!("Error loading binding for '{}': {}", filename, e)
1039        );
1040        // Mark as loaded even on error to prevent re-loading via raw path
1041        assign_value(&s!("{filename}_loaded"), true, Some(Scope::Global));
1042        None
1043      },
1044    }
1045  };
1046  if let Some(before) = setters_before {
1047    crate::binding::store_setters::mark_bound(&before)?;
1048  }
1049  // (no binding: the raw read below marks its own)
1050  if loaded.is_none() && still_loading_mark(&filename, &loading_mark) {
1051    let was = loading_mark.as_ref().and_then(|(_, was)| was.clone());
1052    assign_meaning(
1053      &T_CS!(s!("\\ver@{filename}")),
1054      was.unwrap_or(Stored::None),
1055      Some(Scope::Global),
1056    );
1057  }
1058  let mut is_found_raw = false;
1059  // Retain the "a binding loaded" fact for the later hook-firing check (`loaded`
1060  // is consumed by the announce branch just below).
1061  let is_binding = loaded.is_some();
1062  if let Some(binding_source) = loaded {
1063    // A native binding was truly loaded: announce it (Perl loadLTXML
1064    // "(Loading <path>…)" analog) so CorTeX's `loaded_file` log-parser counts
1065    // it. Self-contained begin+end note (timing is currently disabled).
1066    // A runtime `.rhai` file binding threads its resolved on-disk path through
1067    // the dispatch result (`BindingSource`, converter.rs `rhai_dispatch`), so it
1068    // is announced by that real path — more useful, and closer to Perl, whose
1069    // note names the actual binding file (#560). A compiled-in binding carries
1070    // no source, so it is announced under its module-proxy name — `tcolorbox.sty`
1071    // → `tcolorbox_sty.rs`, `aa.cls` → `aa_cls.rs` — mirroring Perl's distinct
1072    // `.ltxml` path. This keeps the entry distinguishable from the raw twin's
1073    // "(Processing definitions <path>…)" when a binding raw-loads its same-named
1074    // file, so cortex's loaded_file stats record two artifacts as two entries
1075    // instead of double-counting one file (user, 2026-07-04).
1076    let binding_name = if let Some(path) = binding_source {
1077      path
1078    } else if let Some(stem) = filename.strip_suffix(".sty") {
1079      s!("{stem}_sty.rs")
1080    } else if let Some(stem) = filename.strip_suffix(".cls") {
1081      s!("{stem}_cls.rs")
1082    } else {
1083      filename.clone()
1084    };
1085    note_begin(&s!("Loading {binding_name}"));
1086    note_end("");
1087    // We found and loaded a binding successfully, mark it as such.
1088    // Perl Package.pm::loadLTXML L2315-2316 sets TWO flags: `$request`_loaded
1089    // (e.g. `color.sty_loaded`) AND `$ltxname`_loaded (`color.sty.ltxml_loaded`),
1090    // where `.ltxml` is the suffix of the Perl binding file. Rust's port
1091    // keeps only the former — `.ltxml` is not a suffix in the Rust world, so
1092    // binding-vs-raw-tex distinction is queryable via `*_loaded` directly.
1093    // See OXIDIZED_DESIGN.md. Callers of the legacy `.ltxml_loaded` form
1094    // must be migrated to `_loaded`.
1095    // Per OXIDIZED_DESIGN #23: binding success → `<filename>_loaded`.
1096    // Raw load tracks separately via `<filename>_raw_loaded` (see
1097    // load_tex_definitions). The `_found_loaded` Rust-only flag is
1098    // dropped — read sites check `_loaded || _raw_loaded` instead.
1099    let loaded_flag = format!("{filename}_loaded");
1100    assign_value(&loaded_flag, true, Some(Scope::Global));
1101    // Also set the Perl-equivalent `<filename>.ltxml_loaded` flag —
1102    // some callers (e.g. require_package's deps-scan gate) need to
1103    // distinguish binding-loaded from raw-loaded. Without this,
1104    // paper-bundled .sty files that load natbib (which has a
1105    // binding) re-trigger deps-scan on natbib.sty, which re-finds
1106    // \usepackage{natbib} in natbib.sty's own warning text, looping
1107    // infinitely. Witness 2111.01269 (TIMEOUT from natbib deps loop).
1108    assign_value(&s!("{filename}.ltxml_loaded"), true, Some(Scope::Global));
1109    // Perl L2326: Let(T_CS('\ver@'.$trequest), T_CS('\fmtversion'), 'global');
1110    // Set \ver@name.ext to \fmtversion so LaTeX's \RequirePackage guard works.
1111    // Without this, \RequirePackage date checks fail and packages get re-loaded.
1112    // OXIDIZED_DESIGN #169 sibling: a BINDING declares no version, but the
1113    // real file's `\ProvidesPackage{x}[<date> <version> <info>]` is what
1114    // documents read back — setspace-doc.tex:60-64
1115    // `\def\pkginfo#1 #2 #3\relax{…}` `\expandafter\pkginfo\csname
1116    // ver@setspace.sty\endcsname\relax` splits it at spaces (a
1117    // space-free `\fmtversion` ran to EOF: `Until:\relax`), `\@ifpackagelater`
1118    // compares its date. Read it from the installed file when there is one
1119    // (`provides_version_of`); `\fmtversion` stays the fallback.
1120    if options.handleoptions {
1121      let ver_cs = T_CS!(s!("\\ver@{}", filename));
1122      // (`\relax` is not loaded: a query before the load leaves it, `\ifx\csname ver@X.sty\endcsname\relax`)
1123      if !file_loaded(&filename) || still_loading_mark(&filename, &loading_mark) {
1124        mark_version(&filename)?;
1125      }
1126      // fontenc.sty:tail `\global\let\ver@fontenc.sty\relax` — the file
1127      // reports itself NOT loaded (`\@ifpackageloaded{fontenc}` is false in
1128      // real LaTeX), so the next request loads it again.
1129      if lookup_bool(&s!("{filename}_unmarks_itself")) {
1130        let_i(&ver_cs, &T_CS!("\\relax"), Some(Scope::Global));
1131      }
1132    }
1133    // A stand-in binding is followed by the paper's copy of the file it stands in for, which only provides
1134    // (OXIDIZED_DESIGN_DIVERGENCES #731): inside this load, so the copy's `\AtEndOfClass`/`\AtEndOfPackage` code runs
1135    // at its end with the binding's. So is the binding a shipped copy's declaration routed its request to
1136    // (`provided_binding`).
1137    if crate::binding::author_files::stands_in_for_an_author_file(&filename)
1138      || loading_for_a_provides_route(name)
1139    {
1140      read_stand_in_copy(
1141        &stand_in_copy_request(name),
1142        name,
1143        &as_type,
1144        grandparent_in_expl3,
1145      )?;
1146    }
1147  } else {
1148    // We're inverting the control flow, because it is near-instant to check whether we have an
1149    // available binding dispatcher, in both contributed and core binding names
1150    // Now that we have ensured there is no compiled target of this name, we can start the file
1151    // system search dance, call to kpsewhich, etc.
1152    //
1153    // Perl Package.pm FindFile search order (L2109-2139):
1154    //   1. .ltxml binding (handled above by load_binding/load_external_binding)
1155    //   2. Raw TeX in search paths, BUT only if INTERPRETING_DEFINITIONS is true (i.e. we're inside
1156    //      recursive loading from another raw TeX file)
1157    //   3. FindFile_fallback — strip version suffixes, find generic .ltxml binding (e.g.
1158    //      icml2024.sty → icml.sty.ltxml)
1159    //   4. Raw TeX in search paths (without INTERPRETING_DEFINITIONS gate)
1160    //   5. kpsewhich
1161    //
1162    // This ordering ensures versioned-package fallback bindings take priority
1163    // over raw .sty files that may contain layout checks (like ICML's \ifdim
1164    // page-margin checks) that produce spurious warnings.
1165    let interpreting = lookup_bool_sym(crate::pin!("INTERPRETING_DEFINITIONS"));
1166    // Perl Package.pm:FindFile_aux L2107: `$interpretable =
1167    // LookupMapping('INTERPRETABLE_SOURCES', $file)`. A binding may
1168    // register specific raw sources (keyed by `name.ext`) that MUST be
1169    // raw-loaded directly rather than version-stripped to a fallback
1170    // binding. Driver: xparse.sty.ltxml registers `xparse-2018-04-12.sty`
1171    // so the rollback `\file_input` from the real xparse.sty loads the
1172    // actual definitions (`\NewDocumentCommand`) — WITHOUT this, the
1173    // version-suffix fallback strips `xparse-2018-04-12` → `xparse`, which
1174    // re-enters the in-progress xparse binding (short-circuited) and the
1175    // rollback file is never loaded (`\NewDocumentCommand` undefined →
1176    // xparse/l3 cascade; canvas witness 2309.17288, tests xparse/regex_match).
1177    let interpretable = lookup_mapping("INTERPRETABLE_SOURCES", &filename).is_some();
1178
1179    // Step 2: If we're interpreting raw TeX definitions (or this file is
1180    // explicitly INTERPRETABLE), look for the file directly.
1181    // Perl L2115: `(!notex && ($interpreting || $interpretable) && pathname_find)`.
1182    // Perl Package.pm L2117-2119: `pathname_find($file, paths => $paths)` —
1183    // LOCAL PATHS ONLY, no kpsewhich. Rust must mirror this: kpsewhich
1184    // here would short-circuit Step 3 (fallback ltxml) for any TeX-Live-
1185    // shipped raw file. Witness: `\RequirePackage{caption3}` from raw
1186    // floatrow.sty — Perl finds caption3.sty NOT in user paths, falls
1187    // through to Step 3 → caption.sty.ltxml. Rust with kpsewhich here
1188    // returned the real caption3.sty from TL, raw-loading it and
1189    // triggering the `\DeclareCaptionFormat{hang}[#1#2#3\par]{...}`
1190    // PARAM-leak cascade (arXiv:2506.19291: Rust=30 vs Perl=2).
1191    // A shipped file declaring itself a bound file is that binding's request, read after it as its stand-in's copy
1192    // ([`provided_binding`]; OXIDIZED_DESIGN_DIVERGENCES #731): ahead of every raw read, as a binding always wins over
1193    // a shipped copy (user rulings 2026-10-17, 2026-10-20), and of the versioned fallback, which reads no copy.
1194    let provides = if !options.noltxml && !interpretable {
1195      provided_binding(name, &as_type)
1196    } else {
1197      None
1198    };
1199    let found_raw = if provides.is_none() && (interpreting || interpretable) && !options.notex {
1200      find_file(
1201        &filename,
1202        Some(FindFileOptions {
1203          forbid_ltxml:      options.noltxml,
1204          notex:             false,
1205          ext_type:          options.extension.as_ref().cloned(),
1206          search_paths_only: true,
1207        }),
1208      )
1209    } else {
1210      None
1211    };
1212
1213    // Step 3: Try fallback (strip version suffixes / dir prefix) before raw TeX.
1214    // Perl Package.pm L2118-2121: FindFile_fallback.
1215    //
1216    // Design policy: bindings ALWAYS win over local raw .sty/.cls files.
1217    // The `.rs` bindings are hand-tuned for the conversion, so if a
1218    // fallback name resolves to a registered binding we dispatch there
1219    // unconditionally. Raw TeX is the last-resort path (Step 4).
1220    //
1221    // Two flavors are recorded via [`FallbackKind`] for informational
1222    // log messages only — both always fire when the binding exists:
1223    //   - Versioned: suffix/prefix actually stripped (Perl-faithful). Drivers: 1206.0536 (mysvjour3
1224    //     → svjour3), astro-ph0005021 (./aaspp4 → ./aaspp — aaspp4.sty ships locally with plain-TeX
1225    //     `\startdata`; the engine's alignment-aware binding still wins, matching Perl).
1226    //   - BasenameOnly: only directory prefix removed. Rust-specific extension keyed to our
1227    //     contrib-binding registry. Drivers: 2105.02087 (misc/ieeetran → IEEEtran binding);
1228    //     2405.18387 (assets/equations → equations binding, because we ship a tuned binding for
1229    //     this name).
1230    let found_raw = if found_raw.is_some() {
1231      found_raw
1232    } else if !options.noltxml && !interpretable {
1233      // Perl L2119: `(!noltxml && !$interpretable && FindFile_fallback)` —
1234      // an INTERPRETABLE source must NOT be version-stripped to a fallback
1235      // binding; it falls through to the raw-TeX/kpsewhich path below.
1236      let route = match &provides {
1237        Some((binding, declared)) => {
1238          let fallback = s!("{binding}.{as_type}");
1239          Info!(
1240            "fallback",
1241            name,
1242            s!(
1243              "The paper's {filename} declares itself {declared}: loading the {fallback} binding, then the copy as \
1244               its stand-in's"
1245            )
1246          );
1247          Some(fallback)
1248        },
1249        None => find_file_fallback(name, &as_type).map(|(fallback, _kind)| {
1250          Info!(
1251            "fallback",
1252            name,
1253            s!("Interpreted as versioned package, falling back to {fallback}")
1254          );
1255          fallback
1256        }),
1257      };
1258      if let Some(fallback) = route {
1259        // Load the fallback binding once: a binding the document already loaded under its own name is not run again
1260        // (Perl `loadLTXML`, Package.pm:2328-2330).
1261        let ext_suffix = if as_type == "sty" { ".sty" } else { ".cls" };
1262        let fallback_name = fallback.trim_end_matches(ext_suffix).to_string();
1263        // The request the fallback answers, for a binding that reads its version from its name: Perl's `\@currname`
1264        // holds the request while the fallback loads (Package.pm:2618 defines it from `$name`), ours the fallback's
1265        // name. neurips_sty.rs reads the year of `Styles/neurips_2026` here (2609.20831).
1266        // `fallback_target`: the binding answering it, so a binding tells its own request from an outer load's
1267        // (shipped_class.rs).
1268        // `fallback_provides`: the binding a shipped copy's declaration routed to, which reads the copy after it
1269        // (`loading_for_a_provides_route`); with the copy's options, which its own `\ProcessOptions` takes in TeX, even
1270        // after the binding loaded already (`<file>:options_of_a_renamed_copy`, `apply_repeat_options`)
1271        let outer_request = lookup_string("fallback_request");
1272        let outer_target = lookup_string("fallback_target");
1273        let outer_provides = lookup_string("fallback_provides");
1274        assign_value("fallback_request", name.to_string(), Some(Scope::Global));
1275        assign_value(
1276          "fallback_target",
1277          fallback_name.clone(),
1278          Some(Scope::Global),
1279        );
1280        let renamed_copy_key = s!("{fallback}:options_of_a_renamed_copy");
1281        let outer_renamed_copy = lookup_bool(&renamed_copy_key);
1282        if provides.is_some() {
1283          assign_value(
1284            "fallback_provides",
1285            fallback_name.clone(),
1286            Some(Scope::Global),
1287          );
1288          assign_value(&renamed_copy_key, true, Some(Scope::Global));
1289        } else {
1290          assign_value("fallback_provides", String::new(), Some(Scope::Global));
1291        }
1292        // Forward the original options + after-hook so fallback bindings see
1293        // user-supplied class/package options (Perl-faithful: in Perl FindFile
1294        // returns a path and the caller's options/after stay attached to the
1295        // ORIGINAL `\@currname`-frame). Without this, `\documentstyle[epsfig]{mn1}`
1296        // fell back to mn.cls.ltxml with empty options → mn.cls's option-handler
1297        // never saw `epsfig` → `\compat@loadpackages` after-hook never fired
1298        // → `\psfig` undefined. Witness: astro-ph0002213.
1299        let fb_result = input_definitions(&fallback_name, InputDefinitionOptions {
1300          extension: Some(Cow::Borrowed(if as_type == "sty" { "sty" } else { "cls" })),
1301          options: original_options,
1302          after: original_after,
1303          handleoptions: options.handleoptions,
1304          noerror: true,
1305          reloadable: false,
1306          ..InputDefinitionOptions::default()
1307        });
1308        assign_value("fallback_request", outer_request, Some(Scope::Global));
1309        assign_value("fallback_target", outer_target, Some(Scope::Global));
1310        assign_value("fallback_provides", outer_provides, Some(Scope::Global));
1311        if provides.is_some() {
1312          assign_value(&renamed_copy_key, outer_renamed_copy, Some(Scope::Global));
1313        }
1314        if fb_result.is_ok() {
1315          assign_value(&s!("{filename}_loaded"), true, Some(Scope::Global));
1316          // latex.ltx:18484-18486 `\@pr@videpackage`: the requested name takes the
1317          // loaded file's version (`\ver@\@currpkg@reqd`), so a date test on it
1318          // reads the substitute's: floatrow.sty:40 `\@ifpackagelater{caption3}
1319          // {2007/04/11 v3.0q}` after caption3 fell back to caption (iaria,
1320          // iaria-lite, langscibook, letgut; TeX Live class census 2026-09-24).
1321          let_i(
1322            &T_CS!(s!("\\ver@{filename}")),
1323            &T_CS!(s!("\\ver@{fallback_name}.{as_type}")),
1324            Some(Scope::Global),
1325          );
1326          // NOTE: do NOT set `{filename}.ltxml_loaded` here. The
1327          // fallback name is a DIFFERENT binding (e.g. article for
1328          // myclass); the original `{filename}` (myclass.cls) has
1329          // no binding. Setting it would suppress the downstream
1330          // deps-scan check that picks up myclass's
1331          // \RequirePackage{caption}.
1332        }
1333        None // fallback handled the loading; no raw file to load
1334      } else {
1335        None
1336      }
1337    } else {
1338      None
1339    };
1340
1341    // Step 4: Raw TeX in search paths (without INTERPRETING_DEFINITIONS gate)
1342    // Perl Package.pm L2122-2125
1343    //
1344    // Per OXIDIZED_DESIGN #23: gate by `_raw_loaded` only — when a binding
1345    // explicitly loads its raw counterpart via `noltxml=>1`, the binding's
1346    // own `_loaded` flag is already set, but we MUST still proceed.
1347    //
1348    // EXCEPTION: if Step 3 (fallback ltxml binding) just succeeded, Perl's
1349    // `if/elsif` flow (Package.pm:2118-2125) RETURNS on success and skips
1350    // the raw-tex branch entirely. Rust's port uses sequential `let`
1351    // bindings, so we must explicitly check `_loaded` here. Without this
1352    // gate, `\RequirePackage{caption2}` (before caption2_sty.rs bound it by
1353    // name) loaded `caption.sty.ltxml` via
1354    // `find_file_fallback` (caption2 → caption strips trailing digit) AND
1355    // then ALSO loads raw `caption2.sty`, which fires its
1356    // `\@ifpackageloaded{caption}` mutual-exclusivity error. Same pattern
1357    // applies to any package whose name ends in `[vV]?[-_.\d]+` and whose
1358    // unsuffixed form has its own .ltxml binding.
1359    let found_raw = if found_raw.is_some() {
1360      found_raw
1361    } else if lookup_bool(&s!("{filename}_loaded")) {
1362      // Fallback ltxml binding already loaded — don't double-load the raw.
1363      None
1364    } else if !options.notex && (options.reloadable || !lookup_bool(&s!("{filename}_raw_loaded"))) {
1365      // Perl Package.pm L2121-2125 + L2131-2136: combined raw-search
1366      // step. Tries local paths first, then kpsewhich. Mirrors Perl's
1367      // Step 4 (`!interpreting` local raw) PLUS Step 5 (kpsewhich
1368      // unconditionally — note Perl's kpsewhich block lacks the
1369      // interpreting gate). The previous `!interpreting` guard here
1370      // was wrong: Step 2 now uses `search_paths_only=true`, so
1371      // under interpreting=true we still need kpsewhich for raw
1372      // files that have no fallback ltxml binding.
1373      find_file(
1374        &filename,
1375        Some(FindFileOptions {
1376          forbid_ltxml:      options.noltxml,
1377          notex:             false,
1378          ext_type:          options.extension.as_ref().cloned(),
1379          search_paths_only: options.searchpaths_only,
1380        }),
1381      )
1382    } else {
1383      None
1384    };
1385
1386    if let Some(file) = found_raw {
1387      is_found_raw = true;
1388      // The raw load itself sets `<filename>_raw_loaded` via
1389      // load_tex_definitions (per OXIDIZED_DESIGN #23). Read sites
1390      // check `_loaded || _raw_loaded` to detect "any load happened".
1391      // The title-page setters the file defines are its own, even when a binding reads it
1392      // (`store_setters::mark_raw`).
1393      let setters_before = crate::binding::store_setters::snapshot()?;
1394      let class_read = (as_type == "cls").then(crate::binding::store_setters::RawClassRead::begin);
1395      load_tex_definitions(
1396        &filename,
1397        &file,
1398        options.reloadable,
1399        options.at_letter,
1400        grandparent_in_expl3,
1401        options.handleoptions,
1402      )?;
1403      drop(class_read);
1404      crate::binding::store_setters::mark_raw(&setters_before)?;
1405      if as_type == "cls" && !options.handleoptions {
1406        UNCLAIMED_RAW_CLASS_READS.set(UNCLAIMED_RAW_CLASS_READS.get() + 1);
1407      }
1408    } else if options.reloadable
1409      && options.notex
1410      && options.noerror
1411      && !lookup_bool(&s!("{filename}_loaded"))
1412    {
1413      // A binding-only (`notex`) RELOADABLE probe that loaded nothing in THIS
1414      // call: the file is `_raw_loaded` from an earlier `\usepackage`, so the
1415      // not-loaded branch below is skipped (a Step-3 fallback binding loaded
1416      // in this call sets `_loaded`, so it is excluded) — but a silent `Ok` made
1417      // `\input{pkg.sty}` (`input()`, the only such caller) a no-op for any
1418      // already-loaded package, where real TeX's `\input` always reads the
1419      // file and Perl (Package.pm:2270 `loadTeXDefinitions(reloadable=>1)`)
1420      // re-reads it too. tikzlings-doc.tex:69-70 `\tex_input:D` re-reads each
1421      // animal `.sty` under `\c_other_cctab` to harvest its comment lines;
1422      // the skipped read left `\__tikzlings_process_line:w #1^^M` scanning
1423      // the enclosing document to its end. Guard:
1424      // `perfect_kernel_batch52::input_rereads_loaded_sty`.
1425      return Err(s!("Not loaded by this call: {}", filename).into());
1426    } else if !lookup_bool(&s!("{filename}_loaded")) && !lookup_bool(&s!("{filename}_raw_loaded")) {
1427      if options.noerror {
1428        // With noerror: don't mark as loaded and return Err so callers can
1429        // try fallback names (e.g. tikzlibrary → pgflibrary). Matches Perl's
1430        // InputDefinitions which returns undef on not-found even with noerror=>1.
1431        // Nothing loaded → emit no load note.
1432        return Err(s!("File not found: {}", filename).into());
1433      }
1434      // Perl Package.pm L2679 / L2715: maybeRequireDependencies($name, $type)
1435      // is invoked when InputDefinitions returned undef ($success false).
1436      // We mirror that here in the miss-handler, which is the only point
1437      // where we know neither binding nor raw load occurred. Doing the
1438      // dependency-scan BEFORE marking `_load_attempted` keeps the call
1439      // exactly once-per-package and lets paper-local `.sty` files
1440      // (e.g. jinstpub.sty bundling natbib + amsmath dependencies) wire
1441      // up their transitively-bound prerequisites even when raw .sty
1442      // loading is disabled (`INCLUDE_STYLES=false`, the default).
1443      let scan_type =
1444        options
1445          .extension
1446          .as_deref()
1447          .unwrap_or(if options.as_class { "cls" } else { "sty" });
1448      maybe_require_dependencies(name, scan_type);
1449      // Rust-only retry guard: prevents re-attempting a missing file in
1450      // a loop (raw TeX repeatedly calling \RequirePackage). Use a
1451      // dedicated `_load_attempted` flag — NOT `_loaded` — so the
1452      // post-input_definitions success check in `require_package`
1453      // remains honest about whether anything actually loaded.
1454      //
1455      // Gate the guard on raw-loading having actually been POSSIBLE
1456      // (INCLUDE_STYLES on, or noltxml forced). A miss while raw `.sty` loading
1457      // was OFF is a deliberate DEFERRAL, not a genuine "file absent" — and must
1458      // NOT block a later load once INCLUDE_STYLES turns on (e.g. inside another
1459      // package's raw read). Otherwise a bare `\RequirePackage{pgfcore}` (no
1460      // binding, INCLUDE_STYLES off) permanently STARVES tcolorbox's `skins`
1461      // library, which raw-loads pgfcore under INCLUDE_STYLES=true — whereas
1462      // pdflatex loads pgfcore fine in any order. The guard still fires exactly
1463      // where it is needed: the loop it prevents happens DURING a raw read,
1464      // which is itself INCLUDE_STYLES=true. Witness: nicematrix-then-
1465      // tcolorbox[most] (fairmeta.cls, ar5iv #520/#567/#576): 49 → 0 pgf errors.
1466      if lookup_bool("INCLUDE_STYLES") || options.noltxml {
1467        assign_value(&s!("{filename}_load_attempted"), true, Some(Scope::Global));
1468      }
1469      // Say which of the two things actually happened. `notex` (the DEFAULT
1470      // whenever `INCLUDE_STYLES` is off — see `require_package`, Perl
1471      // `Package.pm` L2671-2672) gates the raw-search branch above, so in that
1472      // case NO disk lookup was performed at all. Claiming "no raw file found
1473      // on disk" there asserts a search that never ran, and the package is
1474      // usually installed and findable: `\usepackage{xstring}` on arXiv
1475      // 2607.21760 warned exactly that while `kpsewhich xstring.sty` resolved
1476      // it fine, sending a reader hunting a file-resolution bug that does not
1477      // exist. A diagnostic must not overclaim what it checked.
1478      let why = if options.notex {
1479        "no dispatcher entry, and raw TeX loading is off (enable with --includestyles)"
1480      } else {
1481        "no dispatcher entry, and no raw file found on disk"
1482      };
1483      Warn!(
1484        "missing_file",
1485        name,
1486        s!("Can't find binding or file for '{filename}'. {why}.")
1487      );
1488    }
1489  }
1490
1491  if options.handleoptions {
1492    if is_binding || is_found_raw {
1493      // latex.ltx:18856 runs `\<name>.<ext>-h@@k` inside `\@onefilewithoptions`,
1494      // BEFORE `\@popfilename` (L18801) restores `\catcode`\@`, so
1495      // `\AtEndOfPackage` code sees `@` as a letter: europecv.cls:27
1496      // `\AtEndOfPackage{\InputIfFileExists{ecven.def}…}` reads `\ecv@utf`
1497      // names from the .def, and a `\ecv` + `@utf` split loops the title
1498      // row to the pushback limit (europecv manual, KNOWN_PERL_ERRORS #132 —
1499      // Perl digests the hook after the raw Mouth's catcode restore too).
1500      // Guard: `perfect_kernel_batch54::at_end_of_package_hook_runs_with_at_letter`.
1501      let saved_at = lookup_catcode('@');
1502      assign_catcode('@', Catcode::LETTER, None);
1503      let hook = digest(T_CS!(s!("\\{name}.{as_type}-h@@k")))
1504        .and_then(|_| unprocessed_options(name, &as_type))
1505        .and_then(|_| use_load_hooks(name, &as_type, "after"));
1506      assign_catcode('@', saved_at.unwrap_or(Catcode::OTHER), None);
1507      hook?;
1508      // K11 (`latexml_engine/src/frontmatter_stores.rs`): a class loaded RAW
1509      // keeps its title page in stores that only its discarded `\@maketitle`
1510      // reads; the kernel reroutes the ones it knows to the frontmatter API.
1511      // One rule for every class read raw on the class-load path: the class
1512      // itself (the rawclasses lever, a raw `\LoadClass`), or the class a
