1513      // binding reads raw (apa6.cls, apa.cls, beilstein.cls, lni.cls, memoir.cls,
1514      // …), whose stores reach the frontmatter as under the lever (68u: lni
1515      // lost `\subtitle`; apa6 its `\shorttitle`/`\affiliation`/`\authornote`).
1516      // A binding's read is rerouted here, after the binding loaded: a setter
1517      // the binding defined after its read, or before it and the class left
1518      // alone, is the binding's (`store_setters::mark_bound` above), never a
1519      // store; one the raw class redefined after the binding defined it is the
1520      // class's, and rerouted as the class's store.
1521      let binding_read_a_class =
1522        is_binding && UNCLAIMED_RAW_CLASS_READS.get() > raw_class_reads_before;
1523      if binding_read_a_class {
1524        UNCLAIMED_RAW_CLASS_READS.set(raw_class_reads_before);
1525      }
1526      if ((is_found_raw && !is_binding) || binding_read_a_class)
1527        && as_type == "cls"
1528        && lookup_definition(&T_CS!("\\lx@class@loaded@raw"))?.is_some()
1529      {
1530        digest(crate::mouth::tokenize_internal(TeXString::assembled(s!(
1531          "\\lx@class@loaded@raw{{{name}}}"
1532        ))))?;
1533      }
1534      // A package loaded raw: its in-place contact setters reach the frontmatter API as a raw class's do
1535      // (`frontmatter_stores.rs` `reroute_in_place_contact_setters`; 11lomcon.sty, hep-ph0312288).
1536      if is_found_raw
1537        && !is_binding
1538        && as_type == "sty"
1539        && lookup_definition(&T_CS!("\\lx@package@loaded@raw"))?.is_some()
1540      {
1541        digest(crate::mouth::tokenize_internal(TeXString::assembled(s!(
1542          "\\lx@package@loaded@raw{{{name}}}"
1543        ))))?;
1544      }
1545    }
1546    if file_stack_pushed {
1547      digest(T_CS!("\\@expl@@@filehook@file@pop@@"))?;
1548    }
1549    // Perl-faithful: Package.pm:2637 —
1550    //   Digest(($pushpop ? T_CS('\@popfilename') : T_CS('\lx@popfilename')));
1551    // Pair with the dispatched push above: the SAME `$pushpop` decision, taken
1552    // before the push (see the declaration at the `handleoptions` branch), so a
1553    // load that defines `\@popfilename` mid-way still pops the stack it pushed.
1554    if pushpop {
1555      digest(T_CS!("\\@popfilename"))?;
1556    } else {
1557      digest(T_CS!("\\lx@popfilename"))?;
1558    }
1559    // Restore @currname/@currext as a FALLBACK — AFTER the pop above, since
1560    // latex.ltx's `\@popfilename` (and a package's patched copy, e.g.
1561    // afterpackage.sty:20-28 firing `\<pkg>.sty-@dd` hooks) reads
1562    // `\@currname` as the file being FINISHED and restores from its own
1563    // stack. Restoring first made a nested load's hook look up the PARENT's
1564    // name (ncclatex → nccsect: ncc's `\openrightorany` never defined).
1565    // Guard: `perfect_kernel_batch54::afterpackage_hook_fires_for_a_nested_load`.
1566    if !prevname.is_empty() {
1567      def_macro(
1568        T_CS!("\\@currname"),
1569        None,
1570        Tokens!(ExplodeText!(prevname)),
1571        None,
1572      )?;
1573    }
1574    if !prevext.is_empty() {
1575      def_macro(
1576        T_CS!("\\@currext"),
1577        None,
1578        Tokens!(ExplodeText!(prevext)),
1579        None,
1580      )?;
1581    }
1582    // Verify @currname was correctly restored, and force-fix if not
1583    let restored_name = if lookup_definition(&T_CS!("\\@currname"))?.is_some() {
1584      do_expand(T_CS!("\\@currname"))?.to_string()
1585    } else {
1586      String::new()
1587    };
1588    if !prevname.is_empty() && restored_name != prevname {
1589      // @popfilename may have popped a stale entry; force correct value
1590      def_macro(
1591        T_CS!("\\@currname"),
1592        None,
1593        Tokens!(ExplodeText!(prevname)),
1594        Some(ExpandableOptions {
1595          scope: Some(Scope::Global),
1596          ..ExpandableOptions::default()
1597        }),
1598      )?;
1599    }
1600    if !prevext.is_empty() {
1601      let restored_ext = if lookup_definition(&T_CS!("\\@currext"))?.is_some() {
1602        do_expand(T_CS!("\\@currext"))?.to_string()
1603      } else {
1604        String::new()
1605      };
1606      if restored_ext != prevext {
1607        def_macro(
1608          T_CS!("\\@currext"),
1609          None,
1610          Tokens!(ExplodeText!(prevext)),
1611          Some(ExpandableOptions {
1612            scope: Some(Scope::Global),
1613            ..ExpandableOptions::default()
1614          }),
1615        )?;
1616      }
1617    }
1618    reset_options()?;
1619  }
1620  // No handleoptions=false cleanup needed: we never mutated
1621  // \@currname/\@currext on that path (matching Perl).
1622  Ok(())
1623}
1624
1625/// loads a binding from the main binding dispatcher, if available+found.
1626/// `Ok(Some(source))` on load — `source` is the on-disk path for a runtime
1627/// `.rhai` file binding, `None` for a compiled-in one; `Ok(None)` = not loaded.
1628pub fn load_binding(file: &str) -> Result<Option<BindingSource>> {
1629  _load_binding(true, file, false)
1630}
1631/// loads a binding from an external binding dispatcher, if available+found
1632pub fn load_external_binding(file: &str) -> Result<Option<BindingSource>> {
1633  _load_binding(false, file, false)
1634}
1635// in the spirit of Perl's Package::loadLTXML
1636fn _load_binding(internal: bool, request: &str, reloadable: bool) -> Result<Option<BindingSource>> {
1637  // Perl loadLTXML L2311-2313: skip if already loaded, unless reloadable
1638  // (e.g. `\inputencoding{cp1251}` re-invokes cp1251.def to re-register
1639  // DeclareInputText mappings after `set_input_encoding` reset them).
1640  // OXIDIZED_DESIGN #23: binding load gates ONLY on the binding-specific
1641  // `_loaded` flag (set on success below). A prior raw load
1642  // (`_raw_loaded`) does NOT preclude the binding from loading — they
1643  // are independent paths. Mirrors Perl `loadLTXML` (Package.pm L2311).
1644  let loaded_key = s!("{request}_loaded");
1645  // A file that un-marks itself loaded (fontenc.sty tail; see `already_handled`)
1646  // is loaded afresh on every request.
1647  let unmarks_itself = lookup_bool(&s!("{request}_unmarks_itself"));
1648  if !reloadable && !unmarks_itself && lookup_bool(&loaded_key) {
1649    // Already loaded; the source path is not retained across loads, and the
1650    // announce site short-circuits on `already_handled` before reaching here,
1651    // so reporting an unknown (`None`) source is correct.
1652    return Ok(Some(None));
1653  }
1654
1655  // Re-entrance guard for binding loads: track which bindings are
1656  // currently mid-load on this thread, so that if a binding body
1657  // transitively calls require_package(SAME_NAME) (e.g. via
1658  // \citet → OmniBus closure → require_package(natbib) firing
1659  // during natbib's own ProcessOptions chain), we short-circuit
1660  // instead of re-entering the dispatcher and looping. Task #260.
1661  thread_local! {
1662    static IN_PROGRESS: std::cell::RefCell<rustc_hash::FxHashSet<String>> =
1663      std::cell::RefCell::new(rustc_hash::FxHashSet::default());
1664  }
1665  let request_key = request.to_string();
1666  let already_loading = IN_PROGRESS.with(|s| s.borrow().contains(&request_key));
1667  if already_loading {
1668    // A package or class that requires a package which requires it back (thmtools.sty:47-49
1669    // `\RequirePackage{thm-restate}`, thm-restate.sty:26 `\RequirePackage{thmtools}`) ends the cycle
1670    // silently in LaTeX: `\ProvidesPackage` defines `\ver@<file>` before the body runs, so the inner
1671    // `\RequirePackage` finds it loaded. Perl does the same by marking `<request>_loaded` before `do
1672    // $pathname` (Package.pm:2332). So a re-entrant .sty/.cls request is an Info; any other re-entrance (a
1673    // pool, task #260's natbib loop) keeps the Warning.
1674    let message = s!(
1675      "Binding-load re-entrance for '{}' (transitive require_package \
1676       during its own LoadDefinitions). Short-circuiting to break the \
1677       loop; binding's pending side-effects (DefMacro, Let, etc.) \
1678       will still complete in the outer frame.",
1679      request_key
1680    );
1681    if request_key.ends_with(".sty") || request_key.ends_with(".cls") {
1682      Info!("recursion", &request_key, message);
1683    } else {
1684      Warn!("recursion", &request_key, message);
1685    }
1686    return Ok(Some(None));
1687  }
1688
1689  // Normalize both dispatcher slots to one resolving closure so the shared
1690  // unlock / in-progress guards wrap a single call. The main slot already
1691  // reports a `BindingSource`; the extra (compiled) slot never has one, so its
1692  // success maps to `None`.
1693  type Resolver = Box<dyn Fn(&str) -> Option<Result<BindingSource>>>;
1694  let taken_dispatcher: Option<Resolver> = if internal {
1695    get_bindings_dispatch().map(|d| Box::new(move |r: &str| d(r)) as Resolver)
1696  } else {
1697    get_extra_bindings_dispatch()
1698      .map(|d| Box::new(move |r: &str| d(r).map(|res| res.map(|()| None))) as Resolver)
1699  };
1700  match taken_dispatcher {
1701    Some(ref dispatcher) => {
1702      // Perl `Package.pm:loadLTXML L2318` wraps the binding-load body in
1703      // `local $UNLOCKED = 1`, allowing bindings to override prior
1704      // (locked) definitions. The guard auto-pops on drop.
1705      let _unlock_guard = local_state_unlocked_guard(true);
1706      // Mark in-progress for the duration of this dispatcher call.
1707      IN_PROGRESS.with(|s| {
1708        s.borrow_mut().insert(request_key.clone());
1709      });
1710      struct InProgressGuard(String);
1711      impl Drop for InProgressGuard {
1712        fn drop(&mut self) {
1713          let key = self.0.clone();
1714          IN_PROGRESS.with(|s| {
1715            s.borrow_mut().remove(&key);
1716          });
1717        }
1718      }
1719      let _in_progress_guard = InProgressGuard(request_key);
1720      let _request_guard = BindingRequestGuard::enter(request);
1721      let result_opt = dispatcher(request);
1722      match result_opt {
1723        Some(result) => {
1724          // Here and only here we are certain we have binding support.
1725          // Preemptively mark as loaded to avoid recursion.
1726
1727          // Mark binding as loaded (raw `<request>_raw_loaded` is tracked
1728          // separately by load_tex_definitions). Per OXIDIZED_DESIGN #23.
1729          assign_value(&loaded_key, true, Some(Scope::Global));
1730          // `result` is `Result<BindingSource>`; wrap the success in `Some` to
1731          // signal "a binding loaded" (carrying its source path, if any).
1732          result.map(Some)
1733        },
1734        None => Ok(None),
1735      }
1736    },
1737    None => Ok(None),
1738  }
1739}
1740
1741thread_local! {
1742  /// The requests the bindings now loading answer, innermost last (`epsf.sty`, `epsf.tex`): [`binding_request`].
1743  static BINDING_REQUESTS: std::cell::RefCell<Vec<String>> =
1744    const { std::cell::RefCell::new(Vec::new()) };
1745}
1746
1747/// [`BINDING_REQUESTS`]' entry for one binding load, popped when the load ends however it ends.
1748struct BindingRequestGuard;
1749impl BindingRequestGuard {
1750  fn enter(request: &str) -> Self {
1751    BINDING_REQUESTS.with(|requests| requests.borrow_mut().push(request.to_string()));
1752    BindingRequestGuard
1753  }
1754}
1755impl Drop for BindingRequestGuard {
1756  fn drop(&mut self) { let _ = BINDING_REQUESTS.try_with(|requests| requests.borrow_mut().pop()); }
1757}
1758
1759/// The file request the innermost binding now loading answers, with its extension (`epsf.sty` for `\usepackage{epsf}`
1760/// and for `\input epsf.sty`, `epsf.tex` for `\input epsf`): the file TeX would read, which a binding serving several
1761/// spellings of one file may need to tell apart where `\@currname`/`\@currext` cannot, an `\input` leaving them unset
1762/// (epsf_sty.rs `epsf_resets_globally`; 0906.1361, astro-ph0006390, astro-ph0109126, astro-ph0410051,
1763/// astro-ph9701125). `None` outside a binding's load.
1764pub fn binding_request() -> Option<String> {
1765  BINDING_REQUESTS.with(|requests| requests.borrow().last().cloned())
1766}
1767
1768// Factor out handling and passing loading options from input_content,
1769// to simplify main routine
1770fn before_input_handle_options(
1771  options: &mut InputDefinitionOptions,
1772  prevname: &str,
1773  prevext: &str,
1774  name: &str,
1775  as_type: &str,
1776) -> Result<bool> {
1777  // Perl-faithful translation of Package.pm:2578-2591:
1778  //
1779  //   my $pushpop = LookupDefinition(T_CS('\@pushfilename'))
1780  //              && LookupDefinition(T_CS('\@popfilename'));
1781  //   if ($pushpop) {
1782  //     Digest(Tokens(T_CS('\@pushfilename'),
1783  //         T_BEGIN, T_END, T_BEGIN, T_END, T_BEGIN, Explode($name), T_END));
1784  //   } else {
1785  //     Digest(T_CS('\lx@pushfilename'));
1786  //   }
1787  //
1788  // The 3 trailing brace-arg pairs `{}{}{name}` feed
1789  // `\@expl@push@filename@aux@@` (which the dump's `\@pushfilename`
1790  // body chains into) — that aux takes 3 args. Without them it reads
1791  // 3 garbage tokens from the input stream, corrupting the
1792  // `\g__hook_name_stack_seq` push. Subsequent `\@popfilename`
1793  // then sees an empty/corrupt seq, fires `\msg_error:nn{hooks}{extra-pop-label}`,
1794  // whose `\use:e` (=`\edef`) chain expands `\q_no_value` and triggers
1795  // recursion-detect. See docs/sandbox_failures_SYNC_STATUS.md
1796  // "\q_no_value cascade" for the full investigation.
1797  let push_defined = lookup_definition(&T_CS!("\\@pushfilename"))?.is_some();
1798  let pop_defined = lookup_definition(&T_CS!("\\@popfilename"))?.is_some();
1799  let pushpop = push_defined && pop_defined;
1800  if pushpop {
1801    let mut pushtoks = vec![
1802      T_CS!("\\@pushfilename"),
1803      T_BEGIN!(),
1804      T_END!(),
1805      T_BEGIN!(),
1806      T_END!(),
1807      T_BEGIN!(),
1808    ];
1809    pushtoks.extend(Explode!(name));
1810    pushtoks.push(T_END!());
1811    digest(Tokens::new(pushtoks))?;
1812  } else {
1813    digest(T_CS!("\\lx@pushfilename"))?;
1814  }
1815
1816  // For \RequirePackageWithOptions, pass the options from the outer class/style to the inner one.
1817  if let Some(with_options_to_pass) = options.withoptions.take()
1818    && !prevname.is_empty()
1819    && has_value(&s!("opt@{}.{}", prevname, prevext))
1820  {
1821    // Only pass those class options that are declared by the package!
1822    let mut topass = Vec::new();
1823    with_vecdeque("@declaredoptions", |vdq_opt| {
1824      if let Some(declared_options) = vdq_opt {
1825        for op in with_options_to_pass.into_iter() {
1826          if declared_options.iter().any(|x| {
1827            if let Stored::String(val) = x {
1828              arena::with(*val, |str| str == op)
1829            } else {
1830              false
1831            }
1832          }) {
1833            topass.push(op)
1834          }
1835        }
1836      }
1837    });
1838    if !topass.is_empty() {
1839      pass_options(name, as_type, topass)?;
1840    }
1841  }
1842  // Use letter-catcode (`ExplodeText`) for `\@currext` / `\@currname` so
1843  // they match `\@pkgextension`-style build-time-tokenized macros under
1844  // `\ifx`. Without this the catcodes diverge — `\@pkgextension` from a
1845  // compile-time `DefMacro!("\\@pkgextension", "sty")` tokenizes "sty"
1846  // as letters (default LaTeX catcode 11), but the previous `Explode!`
1847  // used here produces OTHER catcode tokens, so kvoptions's
1848  // `\ifx\@currext\@pkgextension` always returned false — vendor
1849  // `\PackageError{kvoptions}{\ProcessLocalKeyvalOptions is intended
1850  // for packages only}` then fired on every package that uses kvoptions
1851  // (rerunfilecheck reaches this via the hyperref backend `.def` chain).
1852  // Witnesses: arXiv:cond-mat/9611206, math/9904040, math/9904041.
1853  def_macro(
1854    T_CS!("\\@currname"),
1855    None,
1856    Tokens!(ExplodeText!(name)),
1857    None,
1858  )?;
1859  def_macro(
1860    T_CS!("\\@currext"),
1861    None,
1862    Tokens!(ExplodeText!(as_type)),
1863    None,
1864  )?;
1865  // reset options (Note reset & pass were in opposite order in LoadClass ????)
1866  reset_options()?;
1867  pass_options(name, as_type, options.options.clone())?;
1868
1869  // Note which packages are pretending to be classes.
1870  if options.as_class {
1871    push_value("@masquerading@as@class", arena::pin(name))?;
1872  }
1873  let current_opt_val = with_vecdeque(&s!("opt@{}.{}", name, as_type), |vdq_opt| match vdq_opt {
1874    Some(vdq) => {
1875      // Both item shapes the option list carries: `String` (one option) and
1876      // `Strings` (a `--preload='[a,b]pkg'` / batched pass). The latter used to
1877      // be skipped, leaving `\opt@<pkg>` EMPTY for preload options — harmless
1878      // while `process_options` read the list, fatal once it reads the macro
1879      // (latex.ltx `\@ptionlist`): `rawstyles` silently off.
1880      let mut pieces: Vec<String> = Vec::new();
1881      for x in vdq.iter() {
1882        match x {
1883          Stored::String(val) => pieces.push(arena::with(*val, |str| str.to_string())),
1884          Stored::Strings(vals) => {
1885            for val in vals.iter() {
1886              pieces.push(arena::with(*val, |str| str.to_string()));
1887            }
1888          },
1889          _ => {},
1890        }
1891      }
1892      pieces.join(",")
1893    },
1894    None => String::new(),
1895  });
1896  // Standard-catcode tokenization for the stored option list (as the
1897  // `\@raw@opt@` sibling above): real LaTeX stores the `\usepackage[...]`
1898  // ARGUMENT tokens, so alphabetic chars are LETTER (catcode 11) and `{`/`}`
1899  // group. kvoptions/keyval `\setkeys` then binds a `\DeclareString-Option`
1900  // value (e.g. `\axp@bibliography`) from these tokens, and packages
1901  // validate it with the catcode-SENSITIVE `ifthen` `\equal` (or `\ifx`):
1902  // `Explode!` (all OTHER) made `\equal{\axp@bibliography}{common}`
1903  // spuriously fail and apxproof raised `unsupported option
1904  // bibliography=common` (witness docs/known_crashes — gdsm.tex, apxproof +
1905  // biblatex). `ExplodeText!` fixed the letters but left `{`/`}` as OTHER,
1906  // so a braced value with commas fell apart in every brace-aware consumer:
1907  // l3clist split `thesis={type=dr,dr=rernat}` at its inner comma
1908  // (URspecialopts `\clist_set:cx {…}{\@ptionlist{…}}`; witness
1909  // DEMO-TUDaPhD, `\department`/`\affidavit` undefined because
1910  // tudathesis.cfg was never input) and xkeyval's `\ProcessOptionsX` read
1911  // `stylemods={mcols,bookindex}` as the style `glossary-{mcols.sty`
1912  // (glossaries-extra.sty:811 `\@for`; witness glossaries-user). Guard:
1913  // `perfect_kernel_batch53::opt_macro_keeps_braced_option_values`.
1914  def_macro(
1915    T_CS!(s!("\\opt@{}.{}", name, as_type)),
1916    None,
1917    zapped_options(current_opt_val)?,
1918    None,
1919  )?;
1920  Ok(pushpop)
1921}
1922
1923/// The option list stored in `\opt@<file>`: latex.ltx:18514-18517 `\protected@xdef` of `\zap@space#2 \@empty`. Under
1924/// the kernel's `\zap@space` (sect01.rs keeps a copy, `\lx@kernel@zap@space`) that is [`zap_space`]; a package that lets
1925/// its own `\zap@space` around a load is honored by expanding the list as latex.ltx does: tikz-timing.sty:2331-2341
1926/// `\let\zap@space\tikztiming@zap@space` (`#1 \@empty{#1}`, the list as given) around `\RequirePackage[…]{tikz-timing-…}`,
1927/// so `\usetikztiminglibrary[rising arrows]{clockarrows}` stores `rising arrows`, the key the library declares (witness
1928/// 2409.18120; the zapped `risingarrows` was an unknown key). Guard
1929/// `perfect_kernel_batch79x::a_package_zap_space_keeps_the_option_spaces`.
1930fn zapped_options(options: String) -> Result<Tokens> {
1931  if options.is_empty() || x_equals(&T_CS!("\\zap@space"), &T_CS!("\\lx@kernel@zap@space")) {
1932    return Ok(crate::mouth::tokenize_internal(TeXString::assembled(
1933      zap_space(&options),
1934    )));
1935  }
1936  let mut list = vec![T_CS!("\\zap@space")];
1937  list.extend(crate::mouth::tokenize_internal(TeXString::assembled(options)).unlist());
1938  list.extend([T_SPACE!(), T_CS!("\\@empty")]);
1939  do_expand(Tokens::new(list))
1940}
1941
1942/// latex.ltx:18695-18698 `\zap@space`, as latex.ltx:18517/18757 apply it to the option list stored in `\opt@<file>`
1943/// (`\zap@space#2 \@empty`): every space outside braces goes — the delimiter `#1 ` does not see a space inside a
1944/// group — so `share counter=true` is stored `sharecounter=true`, the name l3keys2e looks up for a key an expl3 package
1945/// declared as `share counter` (spaces are ignored under `\ExplSyntaxOn`; witness 2607.15530, its pomegranate.sty).
1946/// `\@raw@opt@<file>` keeps the list as given. A control space (`\ `) is a control sequence, not a space, and stays, as
1947/// does the space ending a control word (no token: it keeps the word apart when the list is tokenized again).
1948/// Guard `perfect_kernel_batch70b::opt_macro_drops_spaces_as_zap_space`.
1949fn zap_space(options: &str) -> String {
1950  let mut zapped = String::with_capacity(options.len());
1951  let mut depth = 0usize;
1952  let mut escaped = false;
1953  let mut control_word = false;
1954  for c in options.chars() {
1955    if escaped {
1956      escaped = false;
1957      control_word = c.is_ascii_alphabetic();
1958      zapped.push(c);
1959      continue;
1960    }
1961    if control_word && c.is_ascii_alphabetic() {
1962      zapped.push(c);
1963      continue;
1964    }
1965    let ends_word = std::mem::take(&mut control_word);
1966    match c {
1967      '\\' => escaped = true,
1968      '{' => depth += 1,
1969      '}' => depth = depth.saturating_sub(1),
1970      ' ' | '\t' | '\n' | '\r' if depth == 0 && !ends_word => continue,
1971      _ => {},
1972    }
1973    zapped.push(c);
1974  }
1975  zapped
1976}
1977
1978/// configuration for input of a TeX source (content files mostly)
1979#[derive(Debug, Default, Clone)]
1980pub struct InputOptions {
1981  pub noerror:    bool,
1982  pub reloadable: bool,
1983  pub file_type:  Option<String>,
1984  /// LaTeX's `\input{…}` (`\@iinput`, `\InputIfFileExists`): a content file runs the file hooks around it
1985  /// (`input_content_with_file_hooks`); TeX's `\@@input` runs none.
1986  pub file_hooks: bool,
1987}
1988
1989/// Input for cases when the file (or data)
1990/// is plain TeX material that is expected to contribute content
1991/// to the document (as opposed to pure definitions).
1992///
1993/// A Mouth is opened onto the file, and subsequent reading
1994/// and/or digestion will pull Tokens from that Mouth until it is
1995/// exhausted, or closed.
1996///
1997/// In some circumstances it may be useful to provide a string containing
1998/// the TeX material explicitly, rather than referencing a file.
1999/// In this case, the `literal` pseudo-protocal may be used.
2000pub fn input_content(request: &str, options: InputOptions) -> Result<()> {
2001  let filepath = find_file(request, None);
2002  match filepath {
2003    // TODO: type => $options{type}, noltxml => 1
2004    Some(path) => load_tex_content(&path, options),
2005    None => {
2006      // Perl Package.pm L2227-2233: `if (FindFile(...)) { loadTeXContent(...); }
2007      // elsif (!$options{noerror}) { Error('missing_file', $request, ..., ...); }`
2008      // Recoverable Error, NOT Fatal. Pre-fix, the Rust port emitted a
2009      // `fatal!(Package, MissingFile)` that terminated the conversion on any
2010      // missing-but-non-critical input — over-fatal-izing relative to Perl.
2011      if !options.noerror {
2012        Error!(
2013          "missing_file",
2014          request,
2015          format!("Can't find TeX file {request}")
2016        );
2017      }
2018      Ok(())
2019    },
2020  }
2021}
2022
2023/// LaTeX file substitution (latex.ltx:19677-19716 `\declare@file@substitution`,
2024/// applied by `\set@curr@file` → `\__filehook_file_subst_begin:nnn`,
2025/// latex.ltx:19785-19810): `\input`, `\InputIfFileExists` and friends read
2026/// the REPLACEMENT when a `\@file-subst@<name><ext>` macro exists. The key
2027/// is the request's basename (the directory is dropped on both sides,
2028/// `\use_ii_iii:nnn`) with `.tex` supplied for a missing extension
2029/// (`\__filehook_subst_empty_name_chk:NN`); the value is the composed
2030/// replacement name. Chains are followed with the same bound as the
2031/// kernel's tortoise/hare loop. Perl has no such step (its `\input` binding
2032/// bypasses `\set@curr@file`, Package.pm:2248-2270), so it re-reads the
2033/// ORIGINAL file: tikzlings-doc.tex:73-75 substitutes each animal `.sty` by
2034/// the `\jobname.cif` its comment harvester just wrote and `\input`s it —
2035/// without the substitution the raw `.sty` re-executes in the document body
2036/// (`\RequirePackage` "only in the preamble", `@threeD` text leaks) and the
2037/// per-animal documentation is lost. Guard:
2038/// `perfect_kernel_batch52::input_honors_file_substitution`.
2039pub fn substitute_file_request(request: &str) -> Option<String> {
2040  let mut current = request.to_string();
2041  let mut result = None;
2042  for _ in 0..8 {
2043    let base = current
2044      .rsplit(['/', '\\'])
2045      .next()
2046      .unwrap_or(&current)
2047      .to_string();
2048    let key = if base.contains('.') {
2049      base
2050    } else {
2051      s!("{base}.tex")
2052    };
2053    let cs = T_CS!(s!("\\@file-subst@{key}"));
2054    if lookup_definition(&cs).ok().flatten().is_none() {
2055      break;
2056    }
2057    let Ok(next) = do_expand(cs) else { break };
2058    let next = next.to_string().trim().to_string();
2059    if next.is_empty() || next == current {
2060      break;
2061    }
2062    current = next.clone();
2063    result = Some(next);
2064  }
2065  result
2066}
2067
2068/// This is essentially the `\input` equivalent
2069///
2070/// we are most likely expecting to get actual content,
2071/// (possibly with definitions included, as well)
2072/// but might actually be getting pure definitions,
2073/// (like a proper style file)
2074/// in which case we may really want to load a binding.
2075/// Note that generic style files (non-latex) often have a .tex extension.
2076pub fn input(request: &str, options: InputOptions) -> Result<()> {
2077  // A name's surrounding blanks are no part of it: tex.web §526 `scan_file_name` skips the leading ones and a blank
2078  // ends the name, and LaTeX's `\input{ x.tex}` / `\input{dir/x }` read `x.tex` and `dir/x.tex` (pdflatex finds both).
2079  // Perl's `Input` keeps them and finds no file (2109.01356 `\input{ data_table.tex}`, 2509.24347 `\input{paper/encoding
2080  // }`; KNOWN_PERL_ERRORS #1151).
2081  let request = request.trim();
2082  // unwrap if in quotes \input{"file name"} — Perl parity:
2083  // `$request =~ s/^("+)(.+)\g1$/$2/;` (single-pass strip of a matching
2084  // leading+trailing run of quotes). The previous `while` loop checked
2085  // the unchanged `request`, which spun forever on any quoted input
2086  // since the replacement only touches `clean_req`.
2087  let clean_req = QUOTE_WRAPPED.replace(request, "$1");
2088  let clean_req: Cow<str> = match substitute_file_request(&clean_req) {
2089    Some(subst) => Cow::Owned(subst),
2090    None => clean_req,
2091  };
2092  // HEURISTIC! First check if equivalent style file, but only under very specific circumstances
2093  // if pathname_is_literaldata(request) {
2094  //   let (dir, name, ftype) = pathname_split(request);
2095  //   let file = name;
2096  //   if !ftype.is_empty() {
2097  //     file += format!(".{}",ftype);
2098  //   }
2099  //   let path;
2100  //   // Firstly, check if we are going to OVERRIDE the requested raw .tex file
2101  //   // with a latexml binding to a style file.
2102  //   if ((dir.is_empty() && (ftype.is_empty() || (ftype == "tex"))  // No SPECIFIC directory, but
2103  // a raw tex file.       // AND, in preamble; SHOULD be style file, OR also if we can't find the
2104  // raw file.     && (LookupValue!("inPreamble") || !FindFile(file))
2105  //     && (path = FindFile(name, type => 'sty', notex => 1))) { // AND there IS such a style file
2106  //     Info!("ignore", request, stomach.get_gullet(),
2107  //       s!("Ignoring input of tex {}, using package {} instead", request, name));
2108  //     RequirePackage!(name); // Then override, assuming we'll find name as a package file!
2109  //     return;
2110  //   }
2111  // }
2112  // // Next special case: If we were currently reading a "known" style or binding file,
2113  // // then this file, even if .tex, must also be definitions rather than content.!!(?)
2114  // Check for *.latexml source-level bindings first — these are always handled
2115  // as definitions regardless of INTERPRETING_DEFINITIONS state.
2116  // Mirrors Perl's automatic .latexml file loading mechanism.
2117  if clean_req.ends_with(".latexml") {
2118    return input_definitions(&clean_req, InputDefinitionOptions::default());
2119  }
2120  if lookup_bool_sym(crate::pin!("INTERPRETING_DEFINITIONS")) {
2121    // TeX's `\input` reads the file on every call; only `\usepackage` /
2122    // `\RequirePackage` load once (latex.ltx `\@ifl@aded`). Perl routes an
2123    // `\input` met while reading definitions to InputDefinitions
2124    // (Package.pm:2287-2288), whose loadTeXDefinitions skips a file already
2125    // read (:2363 `return if LookupValue($request.'_loaded') && !reloadable`).
2126    // tuenc-greek.def:126 re-inputs greek-fontenc.def with `\LastDeclaredEncoding`
2127    // = TU after lgrenc.def read it for LGR (`\usepackage[LGR,TU]{fontenc}`);
2128    // the skipped read left `\TU\greekscript` and `\TU\LGR@hiatus` undeclared,
2129    // so babel-greek's `\greekscript` fell back to textalpha's switch to LGR
2130    // (`\MakeUppercase{\'u}` → ϒ) and the uppercase hiatus lost its dialytika
2131    // (ΑΥΛΟΣ; greek-fontenc test-tuenc-greek, lualatex ΑΫΛΟΣ). A file read RAW
2132    // before is therefore read again; a binding stands for the whole package
2133    // and stays once-only (the re-read never reaches the binding
2134    // dispatch, `reread` not `noltxml`: no `\@currname` directory
2135    // resolution). Guard: `greek_text::input_rereads_a_raw_definitions_file`.
2136    let reread_raw = |filename: &str| {
2137      lookup_bool(&s!("{filename}_raw_loaded")) && !lookup_bool(&s!("{filename}_loaded"))
2138    };
2139    // Split a binding extension off the request so input_definitions sees
2140    // (name, extension) — matches Perl Package.pm `FindFile` / `Input`
2141    // semantics. Without the split, `find_file_fallback` runs with
2142    // `ext_type=""` and reconstructs `"<base>."` (no extension), which
2143    // never matches a registered binding. Witness: hep-ph9911514 — the
2144    // raw-loaded `elsartwb.sty` issues `\input elsart12\@ptsize.sty` →
2145    // `\input{elsart12.sty}`; the version-strip fallback (elsart12 →
2146    // elsart) needs `ext_type="sty"` to reconstruct `"elsart.sty"` for
2147    // the binding lookup. Perl recovers `\ack` cleanly via this path; the
2148    // earlier Rust port dropped the extension and the fallback never
2149    // resolved.
2150    let has_dir = clean_req.contains('/') || clean_req.contains('\\');
2151    if !has_dir
2152      && let Some((stem, ext)) = clean_req.rsplit_once('.')
2153      && is_binding_extension(ext)
2154    {
2155      let reread = reread_raw(&clean_req);
2156      return input_definitions(stem, InputDefinitionOptions {
2157        extension: Some(Cow::Owned(ext.to_string())),
2158        // A content `.tex` re-reads every time (see below).
2159        reloadable: ext == "tex" || reread,
2160        reread,
2161        ..InputDefinitionOptions::default()
2162      });
2163    }
2164    // A plain content `.tex` (no binding extension) is `\input` material
2165    // even while a `.sty`/`.cls` is being read: TeX re-reads it every time
2166    // (Perl `Input`/`loadTeXContent` likewise), so the once-only PACKAGE
2167    // guard (`already_handled`) must not apply. babel.sty:4210-4227 inputs
2168    // `babel-<lang>.tex` once per language occurrence; with french as BOTH a
2169    // class option and `main=french` the second read was skipped, its
2170    // `\BabelBeforeIni` descriptor never recorded, and french.ldf (→ `\og`,
2171    // `\ieme`) never loaded (paresse-fra; `\documentclass[french]{…}` +
2172    // `main=french`). Guard: `perfect_kernel_batch54::content_tex_reinput_during_definitions_rereads`.
2173    // It is read under the current catcodes, as TeX's `\input` reads (the binding path below does the same): the
2174    // definitions mouth's forced `@`-letter broke CoverPage.sty:58-70, which makes `@` the escape character and
2175    // inputs `\jobname.BibTeX.txt` so that `@article{…}` runs `\article` — the cover page printed "title
2176    // undefined" (coverpage SimpleSample; Perl Package.pm:2287-2288 + Mouth.pm:98-100 alike). Inside a package `@`
2177    // is already a letter, so only a package that changed it sees the difference.
2178    // Guard: `perfect_kernel_batch61::input_from_a_package_keeps_the_catcodes`.
2179    let is_plain_tex = clean_req.ends_with(".tex") || !clean_req.contains('.');
2180    let reread = reread_raw(&clean_req);
2181    return input_definitions(&clean_req, InputDefinitionOptions {
2182      reloadable: is_plain_tex || reread,
2183      reread,
2184      at_letter: false,
2185      ..InputDefinitionOptions::default()
2186    });
2187  }
2188  // Perl Package.pm L2109-2113: FindFile_aux checks for `"$file.ltxml"` in
2189  // $ltxml_paths BEFORE consulting raw TeX paths. In Rust the bindings are
2190  // compile-time dispatch tables rather than on-disk .ltxml files, so the
2191  // equivalent check is: if a binding dispatcher responds to `<name>.tex`,
2192  // load it (matching `\input harvmac` → `harvmac.tex.ltxml` preference
2193  // over a local `harvmac.tex`). Skip when the request carries a directory
2194  // (explicit local path).
2195  let binding_loaded = {
2196    let has_dir = clean_req.contains('/') || clean_req.contains('\\');
2197    // Perl Package.pm:2109-2113 + 2255-2270: when `\input{name}` or
2198    // `\input{name.<ext>}` resolves to a known binding extension AND a
2199    // binding for `(name, ext)` is reachable, route to the binding
2200    // instead of the on-disk raw file. Without this, papers using
2201    // literal `\input{psfig.sty}` (common 1996-2005 idiom) fail because
2202    // TL2025 dropped the on-disk file even though Rust has the binding.
2203    //
2204    // Extensions handled dynamically via `is_binding_extension`: any
2205    // extension registered by `latexml_package` or `latexml_contrib`
2206    // (cls / sty / def / fontmap / ldf / ltx / lua / pool / tex /
2207    // code.tex / ...) is admitted, gating out `\input{foo.eps}`-style
2208    // content paths.
2209    //
2210    // For .tex / no-extension paths we still use `load_binding` (exact
2211    // dispatch lookup on `<name>.tex`) — a `<name>.tex` request is
2212    // semantically "include this content", so suffix-stripping fallback
2213    // (e.g. `mysetup.tex` → `setup.tex.ltxml`) would surprise more than
2214    // it helps.
2215    //
2216    // For .sty / .cls / .def / etc — the binding-extension cases — we
2217    // route through `input_definitions`, which gives us the full Step
2218    // 1 → Step 3 → Step 4 ladder including `find_file_fallback`'s
2219    // version-suffix strip. This is what makes `\input{psfig.sty}`
2220    // pick up `psfig_sty.rs` AND a versioned `\input{<pkg>2.sty}` fall back to
2221    // `<pkg>_sty.rs` exactly as Perl Package.pm:2266 does via
2222    // `RequirePackage($name)`.
2223    if !has_dir {
2224      let ext = clean_req.rsplit('.').next().unwrap_or("");
2225      let no_ext = ext == clean_req.as_ref();
2226      if no_ext || ext == "tex" {
2227        let tex_name = if ext == "tex" {
2228          clean_req.to_string()
2229        } else {
2230          s!("{}.tex", clean_req)
2231        };
2232        let stand_in = crate::binding::author_files::stands_in_for_an_author_file(&tex_name);
2233        let stem = tex_name.strip_suffix(".tex").unwrap_or(&tex_name);
2234        let loaded = {
2235          let _stand_in = stand_in.then(crate::definition::origin::StandInLoadGuard::new);
2236          // (its name, for the packages its load loads first, as a package or class stand-in's: mssymb.tex's
2237          // amsfonts and amssymb, `<file>:loaded_by_stand_in`; guard
2238          // `perfect_kernel_batch77w::tex_stand_in_names_its_package_loads`)
2239          let _stand_in_name = stand_in.then(|| StandInBindingName::enter(file_stem(stem)));
2240          load_binding(&tex_name)?.is_some() || load_external_binding(&tex_name)?.is_some()
2241        };
2242        if loaded && stand_in {
2243          read_stand_in_copy(stem, stem, "tex", false)?;
2244        }
2245        loaded
2246      } else if is_binding_extension(ext)
2247        && !(lookup_catcode('%') != Some(Catcode::COMMENT) && find_file(&clean_req, None).is_some())
2248      {
2249        // Not while `%` is ignored or other: doc.sty:895-897 `\DocInput{#1}` is
2250        // `\MakePercentIgnore\input{#1}\MakePercentComment`, reading the raw
2251        // file as its documentation, which a binding (standing in for the
2252        // package's definitions) does not carry. frankenstein/newclude.tex:65
2253        // `\DocInput{newclude.sty}` came out empty (recall 1.1 %) because the
2254        // Rust-only newclude_sty.rs answered, where its binding-less siblings
2255        // typeset (attrib 93 %, compsci 98 %, sweep 120). `\usepackage` does not
2256        // come here, so the binding still serves the package.
2257        // Route through input_definitions for fallback-aware dispatch.
2258        // The `name` arg expects no extension, so split it off.
2259        let name = clean_req
2260          .strip_suffix(&format!(".{}", ext))
2261          .unwrap_or(&clean_req)
2262          .to_string();
2263        // `notex: true` — only BINDING dispatch here (compiled, external,
2264        // version-strip fallback). A raw on-disk file must instead fall
2265        // through to the content path below, which reads it under the
2266        // CURRENT catcodes like real TeX's `\input`. The definitions
2267        // mouth forces `@`=letter (mouth.rs at_letter), which corrupted
2268        // doc.sty's `\CharacterTable` self-check on every
2269        // `\DocInput{<name>.sty}` re-read: the stored table (built after
2270        // doc.sty L834 \makeatother) has `\@` as control SYMBOL + space,
2271        // the @-letter re-read tokenizes `\@` as a control WORD that
2272        // swallows the spaces, and the L827 \ifx fails — "Character
2273        // table corrupted" ×11 docs (frankenstein bundle, pkgloader,
2274        // whose own log had the A/B: definitions-path load corrupted,
2275        // content-path load of the SAME file correct). Perl passes only
2276        // by skipping the re-read entirely (Package.pm L2265
2277        // reloadable=false) and thereby drops the documentation body.
2278        let result = input_definitions(&name, InputDefinitionOptions {
2279          extension: Some(Cow::Owned(ext.to_string())),
2280          noerror: true,
2281          reloadable: true,
2282          notex: true,
2283          ..InputDefinitionOptions::default()
2284        });
2285        // input_definitions returns Err on not-found with noerror=true;
2286        // treat that as "binding not loaded, fall through to raw".
2287        result.is_ok()
2288      } else {
2289        false
2290      }
2291    } else {
2292      false
2293    }
2294  };
2295  if binding_loaded {
2296    Ok(())
2297  } else if let Some(path) = find_file(&clean_req, None) {
2298    // Found something plausible. Perl Package.pm:2289-2302 routes a
2299    // `pathname_is_raw` non-`.tex` file (sty/cls/def/…) to
2300    // `loadTeXDefinitions` — `@`=letter, text suppressed and, because
2301    // `\input` passes no `reloadable`, a SECOND `\input`/`\DocInput` of the
2302    // same file is a no-op (its `_loaded` gate). Real TeX's `\input` always
2303    // reads the file as CONTENT under the current catcodes, and that is what
2304    // we do (batches 7/52): tikzlings-doc.tex:60-72 harvests each animal
2305    // `.sty` line-by-line through `\tex_input:D` under `\c_other_cctab`, and
2306    // doc.sty's `\DocInput{X.sty}` typesets X's documentation body — both
2307    // are content in pdflatex and nothing in Perl. The frankenstein manuals'
2308    // doc bodies (`\cs\attrib` examples, attrib.tex:43/63) do run here and
2309    // still error (PLANS P66) where Perl skips the whole body. Guards:
2310    // `input_routing_and_bbx::document_body_sty_input_is_content_catcodes`,
2311    // `perfect_kernel_batch52::input_rereads_loaded_sty`.
2312    input_content_with_file_hooks(&clean_req, &path, options)
2313  } else {
2314    // Perl heuristic: if the file has no directory, and is a .tex or no extension,
2315    // try loading it as definitions (which checks for binding dispatchers).
2316    // This handles cases like \input tcilatex where tcilatex.tex.ltxml exists.
2317    let has_dir = clean_req.contains('/') || clean_req.contains('\\');
2318    let ext = clean_req.rsplit('.').next().unwrap_or("");
2319    let is_tex_like = ext == clean_req.as_ref() || ext == "tex"; // no extension or .tex
2320    if !has_dir && is_tex_like {
2321      // Try loading as a .tex binding (e.g. tcilatex → tcilatex.tex)
2322      let tex_name = if ext == "tex" {
2323        clean_req.to_string()
2324      } else {
2325        s!("{}.tex", clean_req)
2326      };
2327      if load_binding(&tex_name)?.is_some() {
2328        return Ok(());
2329      }
2330    }
2331    // Couldn't find anything?
2332    note_status(LogStatus::Missing, Some(request));
2333    Error!(
2334      "missing_file",
2335      request,
2336      s!("Can't find TeX file {}", request)
2337    );
2338    Ok(())
2339  }
2340}
2341
2342/// latex.ltx:19646-19673 `\InputIfFileExists`, around a content file LaTeX's `\input{…}` reads (`\@iinput`; TeX's
2343/// `\@@input` runs no hooks): the file's names are pushed on ltfilehook's stack and set (`\CurrentFile`, `\CurrentFilePath` and their `Used` twins), `file/before` and
2344/// `file/<name>/before` run, the file is read, its names are set again for `file/<name>/after` and `file/after`, and
2345/// the stack pops back to the enclosing file's. The hooks run where the file's tokens are read — the before part ahead
2346/// of them in the file's own input level, the after part after them in the enclosing one — since `\input` expands.
2347/// Package and class loads run the same hooks (`use_load_hooks`). Perl fires none (Package.pm `Input`), so
2348/// filehook's `file/before` code never ran and currfile's `\currfiledir` stayed the main file's inside an `\input`
2349/// file: 2110.08325's `\edef\path{\currfiledir}` + `\input{\path/settings}` read `/settings` (missing; its TikZ
2350/// figures undefined).
2351fn input_content_with_file_hooks(request: &str, path: &str, options: InputOptions) -> Result<()> {
2352  let assign = T_CS!("\\@expl@@@filehook@file@pop@assign@@nnnn");
2353  if !options.file_hooks || !load_hooks_active(request)? || lookup_definition(&assign)?.is_none() {
2354    return load_tex_content(path, options);
2355  }
2356  // `\__filehook_file_parse_full_name:nN`: the directory without its last `/`, the name with `.tex` when the request
2357  // has no extension
2358  let (dir, base) = match request.rsplit_once('/') {
2359    Some((dir, base)) => (dir, base),
2360    None => ("", request),
2361  };
2362  let name = if base.contains('.') {
2363    base.to_string()
2364  } else {
2365    s!("{base}.tex")
2366  };
2367  let set = s!("\\@expl@@@filehook@file@pop@assign@@nnnn{{{dir}}}{{{name}}}{{{dir}}}{{{name}}}");
2368  let pop = lookup_definition(&T_CS!("\\@expl@@@filehook@file@pop@@"))?.is_some();
2369  let push = lookup_definition(&T_CS!("\\@expl@@@filehook@file@push@@"))?.is_some() && pop;
2370  let after = s!(
2371    "{set}\\UseOneTimeHook{{file/{name}/after}}\\UseHook{{file/after}}{}",
2372    if push {
2373      "\\@expl@@@filehook@file@pop@@"
2374    } else {
2375      ""
2376    }
2377  );
2378  gullet::unread_expansion(crate::mouth::tokenize_internal(TeXString::assembled(after)));
2379  load_tex_content(path, options)?;
2380  let before = s!(
2381    "{}{set}\\UseHook{{file/before}}\\UseOneTimeHook{{file/{name}/before}}",
2382    if push {
2383      "\\@expl@@@filehook@file@push@@"
2384    } else {
2385      ""
2386    }
2387  );
2388  gullet::unread_expansion(crate::mouth::tokenize_internal(TeXString::assembled(
2389    before,
2390  )));
2391  Ok(())
2392}
2393
2394thread_local! {
2395  /// The files read raw as definitions in this conversion ([`load_tex_definitions`]), by canonical path: kept out of
2396  /// the state's tables, which the format dumps record (a host path there would tie a dump to its tree).
2397  static RAW_READ_PATHS: std::cell::RefCell<HashSet<String>> =
2398    std::cell::RefCell::new(HashSet::default());
2399}
2400
2401/// The canonical form of `pathname` for [`RAW_READ_PATHS`].
2402fn canonical_path(pathname: &str) -> String {
2403  std::fs::canonicalize(pathname)
2404    .map(|path| path.to_string_lossy().into_owned())
2405    .unwrap_or_else(|_| pathname.to_string())
2406}
2407
2408/// Has the file at `pathname` been read raw as definitions in this conversion ([`load_tex_definitions`])?
2409fn read_raw_already(pathname: &str) -> bool {
2410  let path = canonical_path(pathname);
2411  RAW_READ_PATHS.with(|paths| paths.borrow().contains(&path))
2412}
2413
2414/// The files read raw as definitions in this conversion (`RAW_READ_PATHS`), canonical: the title-page check reads
2415/// those of the document's own directory for the words the author wrote (latexml_engine
2416/// `base_utilities::frontmatter::title_page_check`). Rust-only.
2417pub fn raw_read_paths() -> Vec<String> {
2418  RAW_READ_PATHS.with(|paths| paths.borrow().iter().cloned().collect())
2419}
2420
2421/// Is `pathname`, read raw as definitions, a class or a file of the document's own directory (a `filecontents` one too)?
2422fn is_own_raw_file(pathname: &str) -> bool {
2423  if pathname::is_literaldata(pathname) {
2424    return false;
2425  }
2426  if pathname.ends_with(".cls") || super::virtual_files::vfs_exists(pathname) {
2427    return true;
2428  }
2429  let directory = lookup_string("SOURCEDIRECTORY");
2430  !directory.is_empty()
2431    && std::fs::canonicalize(&directory).is_ok_and(|directory| {
2432      std::fs::canonicalize(pathname).is_ok_and(|path| path.starts_with(directory))
2433    })
2434}
2435
2436/// [`crate::definition::origin::note_reading_own_file`] for a raw load, put back when it ends.
2437struct OwnFileGuard(bool);
2438impl OwnFileGuard {
2439  fn new(own: bool) -> Self { Self(crate::definition::origin::note_reading_own_file(own)) }
2440}
2441impl Drop for OwnFileGuard {
2442  fn drop(&mut self) { crate::definition::origin::note_reading_own_file(self.0); }
2443}
2444
2445/// Forget the files read raw: a new conversion starts with none (`reset_thread_engine`).
2446pub fn reset_raw_read_paths() { RAW_READ_PATHS.with(|paths| paths.borrow_mut().clear()); }
2447
2448/// The file a stand-in binding loaded as `name` answers, as the paper asked for it: the request a fallback answers
2449/// with this binding (`fallback_request` while `fallback_target` names it: `icml2026` → icml2025.sty, `mysvjour3` →
2450/// svjour3.cls, a path-prefixed class), else `name` (with the directory a basename-keyed binding's request had).
2451fn stand_in_copy_request(name: &str) -> String {
2452  let fallback_request = lookup_string("fallback_request");
2453  let fallback_target = lookup_string("fallback_target");
2454  let stem = |file: &str| file.rsplit(['/', '\\']).next().unwrap_or(file).to_string();
2455  if !fallback_request.is_empty() && stem(&fallback_target).eq_ignore_ascii_case(&stem(name)) {
2456    fallback_request
2457  } else {
2458    name.to_string()
2459  }
2460}
2461
2462/// "A stand-in's copy provides" (OXIDIZED_DESIGN_DIVERGENCES #731; user ruling 2026-10-17): after a binding that
2463/// stands in for an author's file ([`crate::binding::author_files`]), or one a shipped file's declaration routed its
2464/// request to ([`provided_binding`]; user ruling 2026-10-20), has loaded, the copy of that file the paper ships
2465/// is read too, raw, at the same `\documentclass`/`\usepackage`/`\RequirePackage`/`\input` point, origin `File`. Its
2466/// definitions of names the binding owns hold only inside its own code ([`crate::state::note_copy_provides_assignment`]),
2467/// so for the document they act as `\providecommand`; the rest stand as TeX makes them — the copy's extras: 1309.5950's
2468/// aas_macros.sty `\kms`, `\lya`; 1805.06442 `\prx`; 2001.05519 `\cac`; 1406.5372's `\plotone`. Its
2469/// `\AtEndOfClass`/`\AtEndOfPackage` code runs at this load's end under the same rule
2470/// ([`crate::state::copy_provides_wrap`]), its document-time hooks are not registered
2471/// ([`crate::state::copy_provides_defers`]), a package or class it loads is a real load, the mode suspended for it
2472/// ([`input_definitions`]: bmvc2k.cls:139 `tabularx`, ws-rv9x6.cls's `ws-crop9x6`), a file it `\input`s is part of it
2473/// (svjour.cls's `.clo`, OXIDIZED_DESIGN_DIVERGENCES #744), its expl3 syntax ends with its load, and an Error in its own code stays
2474/// an Error (user ruling 2026-10-17). The copy is the file `request`.`ext` in the paper's source (the
2475/// document's directory, or a `{filecontents}` virtual file), never one of TeX Live's; one read raw already (by its
2476/// binding: arxiv.sty, cvpr.sty, pasj00.cls, … `InputDefinitions!`) is not read again. Perl never reads it: `FindFile`
2477/// prefers the `.ltxml` (Package.pm:2113-2133).
2478fn read_stand_in_copy(request: &str, binding: &str, ext: &str, in_expl3: bool) -> Result<()> {
2479  if request.is_empty() || ext.is_empty() {
2480    return Ok(());
2481  }
2482  let file = if request.ends_with(&s!(".{ext}")) {
2483    request.to_string()
2484  } else {
2485    s!("{request}.{ext}")
2486  };
2487  let Some(path) = find_file(
2488    &file,
2489    Some(FindFileOptions {
2490      forbid_ltxml: true,
2491      ..Default::default()
2492    }),
2493  ) else {
2494    return Ok(());
2495  };
2496  if !shipped_with_paper(&path) || read_raw_already(&path) || lookup_bool(&s!("{file}_raw_loaded"))
2497  {
2498    return Ok(());
2499  }
2500  // (a copy whose declaration routed its request here is the file by that declaration: [`provided_binding`]; the
2501  // text names its binding declares are its own, as the real class's: `state::routed_copy_text_name_yields`,
2502  // OXIDIZED_DESIGN_DIVERGENCES #731 rule 14, 1402.7103)
2503  let routed = loading_for_a_provides_route(binding);
2504  if !routed && !copy_is_the_stood_in_file(&file, binding, &path) {
2505    return Ok(());
2506  }
2507  if routed && ext == "cls" && is_latex_base_class(binding) {
2508    return read_standard_class_derivative(&file, &path, in_expl3);
2509  }
2510  let id = new_copy_provides_record(
2511    file.rsplit(['/', '\\']).next().unwrap_or(&file),
2512    file_stem(binding),
2513    routed,
2514  );
2515  use crate::definition::origin::{CopyProvidesGuard, DefinitionOrigin, OriginGuard};
2516  // (not a raw class read for K11, `\lx@class@loaded@raw`: the binding owns the title page)
2517  let _origin = OriginGuard::new(DefinitionOrigin::File);
2518  let _provides = CopyProvidesGuard::new(id);
2519  // (a class or package copy ends as a real class or package load ends: latex.ltx's `\@popfilename` restores the
2520  // syntax a `\ProvidesExplClass` turned on — fcs.cls:70, whose expl3 catcodes stayed on for the document and ate its
2521  // spaces, `\theta_` undefined: 2606.16349, 2308.11432; an `\input` copy restores nothing, as TeX's `\input`)
2522  load_tex_definitions(&file, &path, false, ext != "tex", in_expl3, ext != "tex")
2523}
2524
2525/// Is `class` one of LaTeX's own document classes — its file in the format's base distribution (TeX Live's
2526/// `tex/latex/base/`: article, report, book, letter, proc, slides, minimal), whose binding is LaTeXML's model of that
2527/// class: its text names and its structural markup?
2528///
2529/// Looked up in the TeX tree by its directory there (kpathsea's `latex/base/<class>.cls`), not by `find_file`, which
2530/// searches the paper's directory first: a paper that ships a self-named `article.cls` beside its derivative still
2531/// declares a LaTeX class (guard `perfect_kernel_batch77w::standard_class_derivative_beside_a_self_named_article`).
2532fn is_latex_base_class(class: &str) -> bool {
2533  let file = s!("latex/base/{}.cls", file_stem(class));
2534  pathname::kpsewhich(&[file.as_str()]).is_some_and(|path| {
2535    !shipped_with_paper(&path) && path.replace('\\', "/").contains("/tex/latex/base/")
2536  })
2537}
2538
2539/// "A standard-class derivative is read as a raw class over its binding" (OXIDIZED_DESIGN_DIVERGENCES #731 rule 13;
2540/// user rulings 2026-10-20: the route stays, the bindings lose nothing). A class the paper ships that declares itself
2541/// one of LaTeX's own classes ([`is_latex_base_class`]: aip-cp.cls:24 `\ProvidesClass{article}`, 1506.04050,
2542/// 1604.08112; mf.cls, 0709.4467; antonio.cls, 1808.10740; HERON.cls, 1903.06504; mbook.cls, nucl-th0703058) is not
2543/// that class but a derivative of it: what it redefines patches the kernel's class. So after the binding has loaded
2544/// (its defaults: counters and their ids, the class options, `ltx-article.css`), the copy is read as a raw class is,
2545/// not in provides mode: an unlocked name it redefines stands as TeX makes it (its size file's `\normalsize`, so the
2546/// nominal font size; its `\thesection`; its contact setters); its `\newcommand` of a name the binding defined takes
2547/// over only a text name (`\refname`; `state::standard_class_binding_name`), never the binding's structural commands
2548/// (`\appendix`, book's `\frontmatter`: hep-ex0405054, math0301023), and its `\newcounter` of a counter the binding
2549/// declared keeps the binding's ids (`S1.F1`; `counter::dialect::new_counter`); a locked name is refused with the
2550/// records a raw class's takeover leaves — `\lx@dropped@<name>` and `<name>:redefined` — so the kernel's `\author`
2551/// appends each call (OXIDIZED_DESIGN #253) and replays the class's own setter (#482), its `\maketitle` deposits what
2552/// the frontmatter does not carry (#265), and the title-page stores reach the frontmatter (K11,
2553/// `\lx@class@loaded@raw` at the binding load's end). Under provides mode aip-cp's accumulating `\author[aff1]{…}`
2554/// kept only the last author (5 → 1) and its `\affil` list was lost, silently.
2555fn read_standard_class_derivative(file: &str, path: &str, in_expl3: bool) -> Result<()> {
2556  use crate::definition::origin::{CopyProvidesGuard, DefinitionOrigin, OriginGuard};
2557  Info!(
2558    "provide",
2559    file,
2560    s!("The paper's {file} derives from a LaTeX class: read as a raw class over its binding")
2561  );
2562  let _origin = OriginGuard::new(DefinitionOrigin::File);
2563  let _not_a_copy = CopyProvidesGuard::new(0);
2564  let setters_before = crate::binding::store_setters::snapshot()?;
2565  // (the binding loaded last is the standard class's, whose load just ended: its own names are the class's)
2566  let class_names = stand_in_last_load_names();
2567  let derivative = StandardClassDerivativeRead::enter(class_names);
2568  let class_read = crate::binding::store_setters::RawClassRead::begin();
2569  let read = load_tex_definitions(file, path, false, true, in_expl3, true);
2570  drop(class_read);
2571  drop(derivative);
2572  read?;
2573  crate::binding::store_setters::mark_raw(&setters_before)?;
2574  UNCLAIMED_RAW_CLASS_READS.set(UNCLAIMED_RAW_CLASS_READS.get() + 1);
2575  Ok(())
2576}
2577
2578/// A standard-class derivative's read (`STANDARD_CLASS_DERIVATIVE`), popped however it ends: a panic a worker catches
2579/// would leave its class's names for the next paper's reads.
2580struct StandardClassDerivativeRead;
2581
2582impl StandardClassDerivativeRead {
2583  fn enter(class_names: HashSet<SymStr>) -> Self {
2584    STANDARD_CLASS_DERIVATIVE.with(|reads| reads.borrow_mut().push(class_names));
2585    StandardClassDerivativeRead
2586  }
2587}
2588
2589impl Drop for StandardClassDerivativeRead {
2590  fn drop(&mut self) {
2591    let _ = STANDARD_CLASS_DERIVATIVE.try_with(|reads| reads.borrow_mut().pop());
2592  }
2593}
2594
2595/// A stand-in binding's name while its load runs (`STAND_IN_BINDINGS`): a package its load loads first records it
2596/// (`<file>:loaded_by_stand_in`), so that only the copy read after this binding takes that load as its own (a renamed
2597/// elsarticle.cls copy's `\RequirePackage[square]{natbib}`, 1811.10063).
2598struct StandInBindingName;
2599
2600impl StandInBindingName {
2601  fn enter(name: &str) -> Self {
2602    STAND_IN_BINDINGS.with(|names| names.borrow_mut().push(name.to_string()));
2603    StandInBindingName
2604  }
2605}
2606
2607impl Drop for StandInBindingName {
2608  fn drop(&mut self) { let _ = STAND_IN_BINDINGS.try_with(|names| names.borrow_mut().pop()); }
2609}
2610
2611/// Is a standard-class derivative (`read_standard_class_derivative`) being read now, its own code (a file the paper
2612/// ships, `reading_author_file`) rather than a TeX Live file it loads? Its `\newcommand` of a name the standard class's
2613/// binding assigned defines it (`state::standard_class_binding_name`).
2614pub fn reading_standard_class_derivative() -> bool {
2615  STANDARD_CLASS_DERIVATIVE.with(|reads| !reads.borrow().is_empty()) && reading_author_file()
2616}
2617
2618/// Is a standard-class derivative being read now ([`reading_standard_class_derivative`]), and did its standard class's
2619/// binding assign the name `key` in its own load (`state::stand_in_last_load_names` when the derivative's read began)?
2620/// Not a name a stand-in the derivative loads assigned, which LaTeX finds defined (guard
2621/// `perfect_kernel_batch77w::standard_class_derivative_defines_only_its_class_names`).
2622pub fn standard_class_binding_assigned(key: SymStr) -> bool {
2623  reading_standard_class_derivative()
2624    && STANDARD_CLASS_DERIVATIVE.with(|reads| {
2625      reads
2626        .borrow()
2627        .last()
2628        .is_some_and(|names| names.contains(&key))
2629    })
2630}
2631
2632/// Is the file at `path` one the paper ships: a `{filecontents}` virtual file, or one under the document's own source
2633/// directory (`SOURCEDIRECTORY`), not TeX Live's?
2634fn shipped_with_paper(path: &str) -> bool {
2635  crate::binding::virtual_files::vfs_read(path).is_some() || {
2636    let dir = lookup_string("SOURCEDIRECTORY");
2637    let canonical =
2638      |p: &str| std::fs::canonicalize(p).unwrap_or_else(|_| Path::new(p).to_path_buf());
2639    !dir.is_empty() && canonical(path).starts_with(canonical(&dir))
2640  }
2641}
2642
2643/// "A shipped file that `\Provides` a bound name loads that binding" (OXIDIZED_DESIGN_DIVERGENCES #731, the
2644/// declaration route; user ruling 2026-10-20): the binding that answers a request with no binding of its own (`name`,
2645/// a `.sty` or `.cls`) because the file the paper ships under that name declares itself another file a binding stands
2646/// for — `\ProvidesPackage{natbib}` in natbib_vw.sty (0802.4100, 1003.3017, astro-ph0512042), modnatbib.sty,
2647/// natbib_jrm.sty; `\ProvidesClass{revtex4}` in qm2008_abs.cls and snp.cls (0805.2253, 0806.0481, 0805.4759);
2648/// `\ProvidesPackage{aistats2023}` in a renamed aistats style; `\ProvidesPackage{everyshi}` in cvpr_eso.sty. The
2649/// declaration is [`file_declaration`]'s (the declared-macro form included); its kind is the request's (a `.sty`'s
2650/// `\ProvidesPackage` or `\ProvidesFile{X.sty}`, a `.cls`'s `\ProvidesClass` or `\ProvidesFile{X.cls}`: a `.sty`
2651/// that `\ProvidesClass{X}` is not routed); the name is another (a copy declaring its own name, in any case, is that
2652/// file); and the name has a binding of that kind — the registry's, matched as a request is without regard to case,
2653/// or the one its versioned fallback reaches (`aistats2023` → aistats, [`find_file_fallback`]). Only the paper's own
2654/// files are read ([`shipped_with_paper`]): a TeX Live file is its own name's. Returns the binding's name and the
2655/// declared one. In TeX the copy is read whole, redefining everything the package defines; the binding's markup
2656/// for it is the ruled answer, the copy read after it as the stand-in's copy (`read_stand_in_copy`), its own
2657/// commands added. One rule for what were per-name stand-ins (72a's renamed natbib copies, 77o's renamed aistats
2658/// styles, 68v's revtex4 copies).
2659fn provided_binding(name: &str, ext: &str) -> Option<(String, String)> {
2660  if !matches!(ext, "sty" | "cls") {
2661    return None;
2662  }
2663  // (the search paths only, no kpsewhich: a file the paper ships is in its source directory or a `{filecontents}` one)
2664  let path = find_file(
2665    name,
2666    Some(FindFileOptions {
2667      ext_type: Some(Cow::Owned(ext.to_string())),
2668      forbid_ltxml: true,
2669      search_paths_only: true,
2670      ..FindFileOptions::default()
2671    }),
2672  )?;
2673  if !shipped_with_paper(&path) {
2674    return None;
2675  }
2676  let declarations = file_declarations(&source_text(&path)?);
2677  // (a file that declares three files or more is a bundle of them, not a copy of the first: cauchy.sty carries
2678  // theorem, exscale, epsfig and apacite, and routed to the theorem binding the rest was lost after theorem.sty's load
2679  // guard, 1312.3078; two may be a class and a package it embeds, qm2008_abs.cls's textcase)
2680  let mut distinct: Vec<String> = Vec::new();
2681  for (_, name) in &declarations {
2682    let stem = file_stem(name).to_ascii_lowercase();
2683    if !distinct.contains(&stem) {
2684      distinct.push(stem);
2685    }
2686  }
2687  if distinct.len() >= 3 {
2688    return None;
2689  }
2690  let (kind, declared) = declarations.into_iter().next()?;
2691  let declared = declared.rsplit(['/', '\\']).next().unwrap_or(&declared);
2692  let dotted = s!(".{ext}");
2693  let declared = match (kind, ext) {
2694    ("Class", "cls") | ("Package", "sty") => declared.strip_suffix(&dotted).unwrap_or(declared),
2695    ("File", _) => declared.strip_suffix(&dotted)?,
2696    _ => return None,
2697  };
2698  let request = name.rsplit(['/', '\\']).next().unwrap_or(name);
2699  if declared.is_empty() || declared.starts_with('\\') || declared.eq_ignore_ascii_case(request) {
2700    return None;
2701  }
2702  let registered = get_binding_names().iter().find_map(|names| {
2703    names
2704      .iter()
2705      .find(|(n, e)| n.eq_ignore_ascii_case(declared) && *e == ext)
2706      .map(|(n, _)| (*n).to_string())
2707  });
2708  let binding = match registered {
2709    Some(binding) => binding,
2710    None => {
2711      let (fallback, _) = find_file_fallback(declared, ext)?;
2712      fallback
2713        .strip_suffix(&dotted)
2714        .unwrap_or(&fallback)
2715        .to_string()
2716    },
2717  };
2718  // (never the request itself: a declared name whose versioned fallback is the request's name)
2719  if binding.eq_ignore_ascii_case(request) {
2720    return None;
2721  }
2722  Some((binding, declared.to_string()))
2723}
2724
2725/// Is the binding `name` loading now the one a shipped copy's declaration routed its request to
2726/// ([`provided_binding`]; `fallback_provides` names it while the route's load runs)?
2727fn loading_for_a_provides_route(name: &str) -> bool {
2728  let target = lookup_string("fallback_provides");
2729  !target.is_empty() && file_stem(&target).eq_ignore_ascii_case(file_stem(name))
2730}
2731
2732/// One class slot (OXIDIZED_DESIGN_DIVERGENCES #736): does the base class a stand-in class binding loaded stand for the
2733/// class `name` its copy's own `\LoadClass` names? A document has one class: latex.ltx:18800 lets a class file
2734/// `\LoadClass` once (a second is `\@twoloadclasserror`, :18938-18942), and the binding has already loaded the base
2735/// class it models the copy on. A binding loads the class its real file loads (r9: ceurart_cls.rs scrartcl,
2736/// atlasdoc_cls.rs its KOMA class, jgcc_cls.rs amsart), so the copy's `\LoadClass` is a repeat; this is the net for a
2737/// binding still out of step (svmult's book where svmult.cls loads article; daj's tocbase emulation). When the bindings
2738/// loaded OmniBus → article, loading the copy's class too put a second class on top of the first: ceurart.cls:62 `\LoadClass{scrartcl}` raw-read scrartcl, scrkbase and typearea over article,
2739/// whose `\@ptsize` of 0 sends typearea.sty:1400-1413 off its path ("DIV for 0.0pt", then "Conditional limit of
2740/// 48000000 exceeded": 2509.11496, 2608.23214, 2310.02658, 2505.16276); atlasdoc.cls `\LoadClass{\ADOC@mainclass}`
2741/// (scrartcl) redefined `\@startsection` and `\@sect` under the binding (2509.01219, "Bad type area settings"). So
2742/// when the copy's own code (provides-mode on: a file it loads is a real load, [`input_definitions`]) loads a class
2743/// that is not loaded yet (`\ver@<name>.cls` undefined; the binding's own base class is a repeat load, cimart.cls
2744/// `\LoadClass{article}`), the binding's load loaded a class, and the paper does not ship the class (a class the author
2745/// ships is read: the reviewer's nc_chain `\LoadClass{mybase}`), the class is not loaded: the binding's classes are the
2746/// copy's base classes (`state::note_copy_base_classes`; a copy's `\newcommand` of a name they define errs, as over
2747/// the real class), with an Info. Perl never reads the copy (Package.pm:2113-2133 prefers the `.ltxml`).
2748fn binding_base_class_stands_for(name: &str) -> Result<bool> {
2749  // (the copy read now, or the copy whose own `\LoadClass` is open: a class a shipped intermediate class loads, copy →
2750  // mybase.cls → `\LoadClass{scrartcl}`, is the copy's too — 72a review r7 NIT 8)
2751  let id = match crate::definition::origin::copy_provides() {
2752    0 => open_copy_class_load().unwrap_or(0),
2753    id => id,
2754  };
2755  if id == 0 || name.is_empty() {
2756    return Ok(false);
2757  }
2758  let file = class_file_name(name);
2759  if file_loaded(&file) {
2760    return Ok(false);
2761  }
2762  let classes = open_class_load_classes();
2763  if classes.is_empty() {
2764    return Ok(false);
2765  }
2766  if find_file(
2767    name,
2768    Some(FindFileOptions {
2769      ext_type: Some(Cow::Borrowed("cls")),
2770      forbid_ltxml: true,
2771      ..FindFileOptions::default()
2772    }),
2773  )
2774  .is_some_and(|path| shipped_with_paper(&path))
2775  {
2776    return Ok(false);
2777  }
2778  note_copy_base_classes(id, &classes);
2779  Info!(
2780    "provide",
2781    &file,
2782    s!(
2783      "the binding's base class ({}) stands for the copy's \\LoadClass{{{}}}: not loaded",
2784      classes.join(", "),
2785      name
2786    )
2787  );
2788  Ok(true)
2789}
2790
2791/// The text of the file at `path`: a `{filecontents}` virtual file, else the file on disk, each byte a character.
2792fn source_text(path: &str) -> Option<String> {
2793  crate::binding::virtual_files::vfs_read(path).or_else(|| {
2794    std::fs::read(path)
2795      .ok()
2796      .map(|bytes| bytes.iter().map(|&b| b as char).collect::<String>())
2797  })
2798}
2799
2800/// The code of each line of `text`, its `%` comment dropped; a line ends at CR or LF, as in TeX (2402.17342's
2801/// sn-jnl.cls has CR-only ends).
2802fn source_code_lines(text: &str) -> impl Iterator<Item = &str> {
2803  text
2804    .split(['\n', '\r'])
2805    .map(|line| line.split('%').next().unwrap_or(""))
2806}
2807
2808/// `name` without its directory and its `.cls`/`.sty`/`.tex`/`.clo`/`.def` extension.
2809fn file_stem(name: &str) -> &str {
2810  let base = name.rsplit(['/', '\\']).next().unwrap_or(name);
2811  base
2812    .rsplit_once('.')
2813    .filter(|(_, ext)| matches!(*ext, "cls" | "sty" | "tex" | "clo" | "def"))
2814    .map_or(base, |(stem, _)| stem)
2815}
2816
2817/// What a file declares itself, read as text: its first `\ProvidesClass`/`\ProvidesPackage`/`\ProvidesFile` (or the
2818/// `\ProvidesExpl…` form) outside a comment, as the kind (`Class`, `Package`, `File`) and the name. A declaration naming
2819/// a macro reads its expansion, as `\ProvidesClass` does: cimart.cls:13-14
2820/// `\def\ClassName@CiM{cimart}\ProvidesClass{\ClassName@CiM}`, 2412.00032. The file's first parameterless `\def` or
2821/// `\newcommand` of the name is taken, wherever it stands, not the one in force at the declaration; a name defined
2822/// with parameters or in another file is not found, and the macro's own name is returned. Later declarations do not
2823/// count: a class may carry the packages it is built on (qm2008_abs.cls, a REVTeX 4 copy, embeds textcase.sty's
2824/// `\ProvidesPackage{textcase}`, :3680).
2825fn file_declaration(text: &str) -> Option<(&'static str, String)> {
2826  file_declarations(text).into_iter().next()
2827}
2828
2829/// Every declaration of [`file_declaration`]'s kind in `text`, in order, up to the file's first top-level `\endinput`
2830/// — a code line of it alone, outside any brace group — where TeX stops reading the file: a declaration in the
2831/// documentation after it (a `.dtx`-style trailer) is not the file's (77w review 8).
2832fn file_declarations(text: &str) -> Vec<(&'static str, String)> {
2833  static PROVIDES: Lazy<Regex> = Lazy::new(|| {
2834    Regex::new(r"\\Provides(?:Expl)?(Class|Package|File)\s*\{\s*([^}\s]+)\s*\}").unwrap()
2835  });
2836  let expansion = |name: &str| {
2837    let def = Regex::new(&s!(
2838      r"\\[egx]?def\s*{}\s*\{{\s*([^{{}}\s]+)\s*\}}|\\(?:re)?newcommand\*?\s*\{{?\s*{}\s*\}}?\s*\{{\s*([^{{}}\s]+)\s*\}}",
2839      regex::escape(name),
2840      regex::escape(name)
2841    ))
2842    .ok()?;
2843    source_code_lines(text).find_map(|code| {
2844      def
2845        .captures(code)
2846        .and_then(|cap| cap.get(1).or_else(|| cap.get(2)))
2847        .map(|value| value.as_str().to_string())
2848    })
2849  };
2850  let mut depth = 0_i64;
2851  let mut declarations = Vec::new();
2852  for code in source_code_lines(text) {
2853    if depth == 0 && code.trim() == "\\endinput" {
2854      break;
2855    }
2856    for cap in PROVIDES.captures_iter(code) {
2857      let kind = match &cap[1] {
2858        "Class" => "Class",
2859        "Package" => "Package",
2860        _ => "File",
2861      };
2862      let name = &cap[2];
2863      let name = if name.starts_with('\\') {
2864        expansion(name).unwrap_or_else(|| name.to_string())
2865      } else {
2866        name.to_string()
2867      };
2868      declarations.push((kind, name));
2869    }
2870    // (the braces of the line's code, an escaped one not counted)
2871    let mut escaped = false;
2872    for c in code.chars() {
2873      match c {
2874        '{' if !escaped => depth += 1,
2875        '}' if !escaped => depth -= 1,
2876        _ => {},
2877      }
2878      escaped = c == '\\' && !escaped;
2879    }
2880  }
2881  declarations
2882}
2883
2884/// Is the paper's file at `path`, of the name a stand-in binding answers (`file`), the file the binding `binding`
2885/// stands in for (OXIDIZED_DESIGN_DIVERGENCES #731 rule 0)? Two tests, in order:
2886/// 1. **Its declaration.** A `\ProvidesClass`/`\ProvidesPackage`/`\ProvidesFile` (or the `\ProvidesExpl…` form) naming
2887///    the binding that answered (the file's own name only when the binding is of that name: a fallback answers a renamed
2888///    file too, 1705.03989's aaart.cls under `aa`), or a file the binding is built on (its own `LoadClass!`/`RequirePackage!`,
2889///    `state::stand_in_built_on`) is that file, whatever its version: 1609.03892's fcs.cls of 2016, older than the
2890///    `\fcssetup` the binding models; 2308.11432's fcs.cls `\ProvidesExplClass{fcs}`; apjfonts.sty (1202.3442,
2891///    1009.4702); 2011.13564's IEEEtai.cls, IEEE's own file, which declares itself `IEEEtran`, the class the binding
2892///    loads.
2893/// 2. **Its command set**, for a copy that declares another file or none (2.09-era files declare nothing): the copy
2894///    defines at least a quarter of the public names (letters only) the binding's own code assigned, a stand-in it loads
2895///    included (`state::stand_in_public_names`; the diagrams binding's diagrams.tex). Over the reviewer's simulation of
2896///    the census's 4,346 distinct copies (`rev72a/ident_sim2.json`), the undeclared copies' shares are bimodal — 124
2897///    under a tenth, 28 between a tenth and four tenths, 1,028 above — and a quarter admits the 1,047 that read as the
2898///    file (aas_macros.sty's journal names, an icml or neurips style's `\icmltitle`, `\AND`); the earlier absolute (20
2899///    names) and copy-side (a quarter of the copy's names) clauses admitted no copy the quarter did not.
2900///
2901/// A binding with no public name of its own and a copy with no matching declaration: not read (the copy cannot be told
2902/// from an author's own file of that name: aipcheck.tex, mssymb.tex, citesort.sty, revtex.sty). An author's own file
2903/// that only shares the name is not read either: hep-ph9609379's diagrams.sty (fifteen `\special` PostScript macros,
2904/// one of them `\diagram`), 2411.02780's equations.sty (`\ProvidesPackage{annotate-equations}`). Logged at Info.
2905fn copy_is_the_stood_in_file(file: &str, binding: &str, path: &str) -> bool {
2906  static DEFINED: Lazy<Regex> = Lazy::new(|| {
2907    Regex::new(
2908      r"\\(?:[egx]?def|let|(?:re|provide)?newcommand\*?|DeclareRobustCommand\*?|(?:New|Renew|Provide|Declare)DocumentCommand|newif|chardef|mathchardef|newlength|newcount|newdimen|newskip|newtoks|newbox)\s*\{?\s*(\\[A-Za-z]+)(?:[^A-Za-z@]|$)|\\(?:(?:re)?newenvironment\*?|(?:New|Renew|Provide|Declare)DocumentEnvironment)\s*\{([A-Za-z]+)\}|\\newtheorem\*?\s*\{([A-Za-z]+)\}|\\newcounter\s*\{([A-Za-z]+)\}",
2909    )
2910    .unwrap()
2911  });
2912  let Some(text) = source_text(path) else {
2913    return false;
2914  };
2915  let stem = |name: &str| file_stem(name).to_ascii_lowercase();
2916  // (a line ends at CR or LF, as in TeX: 2402.17342's sn-jnl.cls has CR-only ends)
2917  let lines = || source_code_lines(&text);
2918  let declared = file_declaration(&text).map(|(_, name)| stem(&name));
2919  let built_on = stand_in_built_on();
2920  // (the binding that answered, not the name asked for: a fallback answers a renamed file too — 1705.03989's aaart.cls,
2921  // amsart renamed and declaring itself `aaart`, which the `aa` binding answers — so a copy declaring the requested
2922  // name is that binding's file only when the binding is of that name; else the command set below decides)
2923  if let Some(declared) = &declared
2924    && (*declared == stem(binding) || built_on.iter().any(|base| stem(base) == *declared))
2925  {
2926    Info!(
2927      "provide",
2928      file,
2929      s!(
2930        "The paper's {file} declares itself {declared}: read as the file its binding stands in for"
2931      )
2932    );
2933    return true;
2934  }
2935  let public = stand_in_public_names();
2936  let mut defined: HashSet<String> = HashSet::default();
2937  for code in lines() {
2938    for cap in DEFINED.captures_iter(code) {
2939      // (the names a declaration makes: `\newif\ifX` also `\Xtrue`/`\Xfalse`, an environment `\X` and `\endX`, a
2940      // counter `\theX`)
2941      if let Some(name) = cap.get(1) {
2942        let name = name.as_str();
2943        if let Some(flag) = name.strip_prefix("\\if")
2944          && code[cap.get(0).map_or(0, |m| m.start())..].starts_with("\\newif")
2945        {
2946          defined.insert(s!("\\{flag}true"));
2947          defined.insert(s!("\\{flag}false"));
2948        }
2949        defined.insert(name.to_string());
2950      } else if let Some(env) = cap.get(2).or_else(|| cap.get(3)) {
2951        defined.insert(s!("\\{}", env.as_str()));
2952        defined.insert(s!("\\end{}", env.as_str()));
2953      } else if let Some(counter) = cap.get(4) {
2954        defined.insert(s!("\\the{}", counter.as_str()));
2955      }
2956    }
2957  }
2958  let shared = public.iter().filter(|name| defined.contains(*name)).count();
2959  let the_file = !public.is_empty() && shared * 4 >= public.len();
2960  Info!(
2961    "provide",
2962    file,
2963    s!(
2964      "The paper's {file} defines {shared} of the {} public names its binding defines ({} names of its own{}): {}",
2965      public.len(),
2966      defined.len(),
2967      declared.map_or(String::new(), |name| s!(", declared {name}")),
2968      if the_file {
2969        "read as the file its binding stands in for"
2970      } else {
2971        "not the file its binding stands in for, not read"
2972      }
2973    )
2974  );
2975  the_file
2976}
2977
2978fn load_tex_definitions(
2979  request: &str,
2980  pathname: &str,
2981  reloadable: bool,
2982  at_letter: bool,
2983  grandparent_in_expl3: bool,
2984  handleoptions: bool,
2985) -> Result<()> {
2986  // Perl Package.pm L2334: $STATE->getStomach->leaveHorizontal_internal;
2987  // Defensive cleanup before reading definitions — if we're somehow in
2988  // horizontal mode while bound to vertical (e.g. after \par-less inline
2989  // text), repack and flip MODE in-place. No-op in the common case but
2990  // matches Perl's pre-load state hygiene.
2991  leave_horizontal_internal();
2992
2993  // Snapshot expl3-state at load entry. The cleanup hook below should
2994  // only restore catcodes if THIS load activated expl3; if the calling
2995  // context was already in expl3 mode (e.g. tasks.sty has run
2996  // `\ExplSyntaxOn` and is now `\file_input:n` ing a child file like
2997  // tasks.cfg), we must preserve the active state for the caller.
2998  // Without this guard, the nested cleanup would reset `_` and `:` to
2999  // OTHER/SUB inside the parent's processing, breaking everything past
3000  // the nested load (e.g. tasks.sty line 817's `\file_input_stop:`).
3001  // Witness for this exact failure: arXiv:2602.21210, 2604.21347,
3002  // 2604.22630, 2604.23234, 2604.22528 (tasks.sty + expl3 cluster,
3003  // Task #20).
3004
3005  if !pathname::is_literaldata(pathname) {
3006    // We can't analyze literal data's pathnames!
3007    // let (dir, name, extension) = pathname::split(pathname);
3008
3009    // Don't load if we've already loaded it before.
3010    // Note that we'll still load it if we've already loaded only the ltxml version
3011    // since someone's presumably asking _explicitly_ for the raw TeX version.
3012    // It's probably even the ltxml version is asking for it!!
3013    // Of course, now it will be marked and wont get reloaded!
3014    // Per OXIDIZED_DESIGN #23: raw .sty/.cls/.def load tracks
3015    // `<request>_raw_loaded`, separate from the binding `<request>_loaded`.
3016    // This lets a binding .rs load the raw file of the same name without
3017    // the flags clobbering each other.
3018    if lookup_bool(&s!("{request}_raw_loaded")) && !reloadable && !pathname::is_reloadable(pathname)
3019    {
3020      return Ok(());
3021    }
