3022    assign_value(&s!("{request}_raw_loaded"), true, Some(Scope::Global));
3023    // (the file itself, by its path: a stand-in binding that reads the paper's copy raw has read it,
3024    // `read_stand_in_copy`. A speculative run's read is undone with it, `<request>_raw_loaded` too: the document's own read of
3025    // that copy is still to come, `\author{First\input{aas_macros.sty}}\author{Ann}\maketitle \input{aas_macros.sty}\kms`)
3026    if !gullet::in_speculative_digestion() {
3027      let path = canonical_path(pathname);
3028      RAW_READ_PATHS.with(|paths| paths.borrow_mut().insert(path));
3029    }
3030  }
3031  // (what it defines is a raw class's or an own-directory file's: the title-page check's third source, whose setters
3032  // these are, `Expandable::watched_call`; a raw TeX Live package is neither)
3033  let _own_file = OwnFileGuard::new(is_own_raw_file(pathname));
3034  let ver_cs = T_CS!(s!("\\ver@{request}"));
3035  let loading_mark = handleoptions.then(|| mark_loading(request)).flatten();
3036  let ver_before = lookup_meaning(&ver_cs);
3037
3038  // Note that we are reading definitions (and recursive input is assumed also definitions)
3039  let was_interpreting = lookup_bool_sym(crate::pin!("INTERPRETING_DEFINITIONS"));
3040  // And that if we're interpreting this TeX file of definitions,
3041  // we probably should interpret any TeX files IT loads.
3042  let was_including_styles = lookup_bool("INCLUDE_STYLES");
3043  assign_value_sym(crate::pin!("INTERPRETING_DEFINITIONS"), true, None);
3044  // If we're reading in these definitions, probaly will accept included ones?
3045  // (but not forbid ltxml ?)
3046  assign_value("INCLUDE_STYLES", true, None);
3047  // When set, this variable allows redefinitions of locked defns.
3048  // It is set in before/after methods to allow local rebinding of commands
3049  // but loading of sources & bindings is typically done in before/after methods of constructors!
3050  // This re-locks defns during reading of TeX packages.
3051  local_state_unlocked(false);
3052  // An EMPTY virtual file still exists (`\tcbwritetemp` over an empty
3053  // `posterboxenv` body, tcbposter.code.tex:168-171) — `Some("")` keeps the
3054  // mouth off the disk; only `None` falls through to a real file.
3055  let content = crate::binding::virtual_files::vfs_read(pathname);
3056  let pathname_mouth = Mouth::create(pathname, MouthOptions {
3057    fordefinitions: true,
3058    at_letter,
3059    notes: true,
3060    content,
3061    ..MouthOptions::default()
3062  })?;
3063
3064  gullet::reading_from_mouth(pathname_mouth, move || -> Result<()> {
3065    while let Some(token) = gullet::read_x_token(Some(false), false, None)? {
3066      if token != T_SPACE!() {
3067        invoke_token(&token)?;
3068      }
3069    }
3070    Ok(())
3071  })?;
3072
3073  // Expl3 scope-exit cleanup: if a raw .sty load activated expl3 catcodes
3074  // via `\ProvidesExplPackage` or explicit `\ExplSyntaxOn` and forgot to
3075  // pair it with `\ExplSyntaxOff` (e.g. lipsum.sty, which relies on an
3076  // `\AtEndOfPackage`-style hook the autoload chain doesn't register),
3077  // digest `\ExplSyntaxOff` now so the pending `\group_begin:` frame pops
3078  // and catcodes restore before the next package loads.
3079  //
3080  // Perl's `TeX.pool.ltxml` L44-47 acknowledges this as a known edge-
3081  // case of the `\ProvidesExplPackage` autoload pattern.
3082  //
3083  // Skip expl3 / xparse / l3keys2e / expl3-code — those legitimately
3084  // leave expl3 active for their callers.
3085  {
3086    let (_, base, _ext) = pathname::split(pathname);
3087    let is_expl3_core = matches!(
3088      base.as_str(),
3089      "expl3" | "xparse" | "l3keys2e" | "expl3-code"
3090    );
3091    // Use grandparent_in_expl3 (snapshotted before `\@pushfilename`)
3092    // rather than entered_expl3 (snapshotted after the push flipped `_`
3093    // to SUB). Without this, sub-loads inside an active expl3 frame
3094    // saw entered_expl3=false (because of the push's `\ExplSyntaxOff`)
3095    // and over-fired `\ExplSyntaxOff` at exit, which then leaks SUB
3096    // into the grandparent's continued reading once `\@popfilename`
3097    // pops the status stack and would otherwise restore `\ExplSyntaxOn`.
3098    // Witness: `\usepackage{xsavebox}` minimal repro (xsavebox →
3099    // sys_load_backend → l3backend-dvips.def); arXiv:2509.05997/.07893/
3100    // .02344, 2510.13206/.13942/.17317.
3101    // One-directional cleanup (grandparent-guarded, as before — the real
3102    // `\@pushfilename` expl3 hook runs BEFORE our entry snapshot, so a
3103    // bidirectional entry/exit restore mis-reads a grandparent-ON chain as
3104    // OFF and re-breaks the xsavebox witness):
3105    if std::env::var("LATEXML_EXPL_TRACE").is_ok() {
3106      eprintln!(
3107        "EXPL-EXIT request={request:?} is_core={is_expl3_core} grandparent={grandparent_in_expl3} handleopts={handleoptions} us_letter={} expl_on={} off_defined={}",
3108        lookup_catcode('_') == Some(Catcode::LETTER),
3109        expl_syntax_on(),
3110        lookup_definition(&T_CS!("\\ExplSyntaxOff"))?.is_some()
3111      );
3112    }
3113    // Fire ONLY for handleoptions frames (\usepackage/\documentclass —
3114    // where the real kernel's \@popfilename restores the expl status).
3115    // A PLAIN \input (handleoptions=false: ctex's `\file_input:n` of
3116    // ctex-engine-pdftex.def, .cfg, .spa sub-files) restores NOTHING in
3117    // real TeX — the loading code manages its own state (ctexhook's
3118    // \ctex_push_file:/\ctex_pop_file: expl-status stack). Firing here
3119    // mid-class fought that machinery and leaked FULL expl3 state past
3120    // `\documentclass{ctexart}` (space=9/`:`=11/`_`=11 at document
3121    // level), garbling pgfutil-common's space-trick and cascading 1001
3122    // errors in every ctex+tikz doc (hfutexam ×4, beautybook, gckanbun).
3123    // Only when the file left expl3 syntax ON, by expl3's own flag (`\l__kernel_expl_bool`, which `\ExplSyntaxOn`
3124    // sets and the `\ExplSyntaxOff` it arms clears, expl3-code.tex:242-272), not by `_`'s catcode: etextools.sty:35-56
3125    // makes `_` a letter for its own code and restores it in its own `\AtEndOfPackage` (:55); firing here left `/`
3126    // catcode 8 after the load and broke siunitx's `\AtBeginDocument` code (2606.06323).
3127    if !is_expl3_core
3128      && handleoptions
3129      && !grandparent_in_expl3
3130      && expl_syntax_on()
3131      && lookup_definition(&T_CS!("\\ExplSyntaxOff"))?.is_some()
3132    {
3133      let _ = invoke_token(&T_CS!("\\ExplSyntaxOff"));
3134    }
3135    // PARTIAL-leak normalization: space=catcode 9 (ignored) while `_` is
3136    // NOT a letter is an invalid half-ExplSyntax state no legitimate flow
3137    // produces (`\ExplSyntaxOn` sets both, `Off` clears both). l3backend-
3138    // *.def loaded nested under a raw class (jlreq/ctex → expl3.sty →
3139    // l3backend) left exactly this state, and every later raw file then
3140    // lost its space tokens: pgfkeys' space-delimited `\def\: {…}` idiom
3141    // broke and its .code/.style handler bodies EXECUTED, leaking `#1`
3142    // to the stomach (misdefined:# — gckanbun/ctex/jlreq tail; Perl
3143    // clean; ctex+tikz min-repro 1001→101 errors with this fix).
3144    if lookup_catcode(' ') == Some(Catcode::IGNORE) && lookup_catcode('_') != Some(Catcode::LETTER)
3145    {
3146      assign_catcode(' ', Catcode::SPACE, None);
3147    }
3148  }
3149
3150  assign_value_sym(
3151    crate::pin!("INTERPRETING_DEFINITIONS"),
3152    was_interpreting,
3153    None,
3154  );
3155  assign_value("INCLUDE_STYLES", was_including_styles, None);
3156  expire_state_unlocked();
3157
3158  // Perl Package.pm L2376/L2393 Lets \ver@<file> to \fmtversion
3159  // UNCONDITIONALLY — clobbering the version string the file's own
3160  // \ProvidesPackage just recorded, so every date guard downstream
3161  // (\GetFileInfo, \@ifpackagelater, toptesi.cls L44-73's version
3162  // comparison) reads the format date instead of the package's. Real LaTeX
3163  // keeps the declared string. OXIDIZED_DESIGN #169 (surpass-Perl,
3164  // user-approved 2026-08-31): apply \fmtversion only as a FALLBACK when
3165  // the file declared nothing — the same guarded idiom the LTXML-binding
3166  // loader above already uses. Witnesses: the 12-doc toptesi cluster
3167  // ("the sty file you are using has a date of <empty>" abort). Guard:
3168  // cluster_package_guards::raw_provides_version_survives.
3169  // (not when the file set the macro itself, fontenc.sty:123's `\relax`: only a query's `\relax` from before the read)
3170  if still_loading_mark(request, &loading_mark)
3171    || (!file_loaded(request) && lookup_meaning(&ver_cs) == ver_before)
3172  {
3173    let_i(&ver_cs, &T_CS!("\\fmtversion"), Some(Scope::Global));
3174  }
3175
3176  Ok(())
3177}
3178
3179/// The state value listing the files read as the document's content ([`load_tex_content`]: the main file, each
3180/// `\input`/`\include` of content, a `literal:` source as its text), parted by NUL: the document's own text, which
3181/// the title-page check reads for the words the document wrote (latexml_engine
3182/// `base_utilities::frontmatter::title_page_check`). Rust-only.
3183pub const CONTENT_FILES: &str = "lx_content_files";
3184
3185/// Note `path` as one of the document's content files ([`CONTENT_FILES`]), once.
3186fn note_content_file(path: &str) {
3187  let mut files = lookup_string(CONTENT_FILES);
3188  if files.split('\0').any(|file| file == path) {
3189    return;
3190  }
3191  if !files.is_empty() {
3192    files.push('\0');
3193  }
3194  files.push_str(path);
3195  assign_value(CONTENT_FILES, files, Some(Scope::Global));
3196}
3197
3198pub fn load_tex_content(path: &str, _options: InputOptions) -> Result<()> {
3199  // If there is a file-specific declaration file (name_tex.rs), load it first!
3200  // TODO: is this `.latexml` variation still relevant in the Rust port?
3201  let _has_binding = if !pathname::is_literaldata(path) {
3202    let (_dir, base, _ext) = pathname::split(path);
3203    load_external_binding(&base)?.is_some() || load_binding(&base)?.is_some()
3204  } else {
3205    false
3206  };
3207
3208  note_content_file(path);
3209  // Open a mouth for that TeX content
3210  // Empty virtual files exist too (see load_definitions above).
3211  let cached_opt = crate::binding::virtual_files::vfs_read(path);
3212  gullet::open_mouth(
3213    Mouth::create(path, MouthOptions {
3214      notes: true,
3215      content: cached_opt,
3216      ..MouthOptions::default()
3217    })?,
3218    true,
3219  );
3220  // eTeX inserts the CURRENT `\everyeof` token list at the end of EVERY
3221  // `\input` file, not only a `\scantokens` pseudo-file (etex.web §362: the
3222  // list is begun before `end_file_reading`, so a delimited scan may consume
3223  // it and no more). Line-reader idioms depend on that terminator: tikzlings'
3224  // `\CommentInput` (tikzmarmots-doc.tex:44-105, `\tex_everyeof:D{\q_nil^^M}`
3225  // + `\tex_input:D`, one `\__tikzlings_process_line:w #1^^M` per line),
3226  // stex.sty:2633 smsmode, expl3 `\file_get:nnN` (expl3-code.tex:12530-12551,
3227  // `\__file_get_do:Nw #1#2 \c__file_marker_tl` scanning across the file).
3228  // Without the mark the reader ran into the enclosing document and looped at
3229  // its true end (tikzmarmots-doc 0→501 errors + Fatal, tikzlings-doc 35→536,
3230  // sweep 28). Guard: `perfect_kernel_batch51::input_file_end_inserts_everyeof`.
3231  gullet::mark_everyeof_mouth()
3232}
3233
3234/// Pass the sequence of @options to the package $name (if $ext is 'sty'),
3235/// or class $name (if $ext is 'cls').
3236/// Perl Package.pm: PassOptions($name, $ext, @options)
3237/// Stores options to be processed when the package/class is loaded.
3238///
3239/// The raw record is the options re-read with standard catcodes
3240/// ([`option_argument_tokens`]): these lists come from the loader's explicit
3241/// `[…]` or from a binding, already expanded. `\PassOptionsToPackage` /
3242/// `\PassOptionsToClass` keep their argument tokens instead, through
3243/// [`pass_options_with_raw`].
3244pub fn pass_options(name: &str, ext: &str, options: Vec<String>) -> Result<()> {
3245  let raw = option_argument_tokens(&options.join(","));
3246  pass_options_with_raw(name, ext, options, raw)
3247}
3248
3249/// latex.ltx:18509-18526 `\@pass@ptions`, the single writer of both option
3250/// records. `\opt@<name>.<ext>` is the `\protected@xdef`ed list the loader
3251/// and `\ProcessOptions` read (here a State list of strings). `\@raw@opt@<name>.<ext>`
3252/// holds the unprocessed argument tokens, accumulated comma-separated
3253/// (:18521-18525): `\gdef` on the first pass, `\g@addto@macro{,#2}` after.
3254/// ltkeys' `\ProcessKeyOptions` reads only this record (:19398, :19457-19470).
3255///
3256/// OXIDIZED_DESIGN #164. LaTeXML's binding-level loader bypasses the kernel's,
3257/// so the record was never made and every `\ProcessKeyOptions` package dropped
3258/// its options (codedescribe `[strict,infograb]`, 10 TL-doc bundles). It is
3259/// written from `\PassOptionsTo*` too, so tudapub.cls:194's `\exp_args:Nx
3260/// \PassOptionsToPackage{paper=…}{tudarules}` reaches tudarules
3261/// (DEMO-TUDaPhD). It is written at pass time, so it exists before the load and
3262/// holds the tokens themselves: fduthesis.cls:193-202 and hustthesis.cls:197
3263/// pass `linespread = \c__fdu_line_spread_fp` to ctexbook, whose
3264/// x-expanded, re-tokenized spelling fell apart into `\s`, `__fp`… at
3265/// ctexbook.cls:336 `\ProcessKeyOptions` (TeX Live class census 2026-09-24).
3266/// It is defined for every load, an option-less one included (the empty
3267/// explicit list): scrhack.sty:284-293 forwards `\use:c{@raw@opt@setspace.sty}`
3268/// as an option of setspaceenhanced (ijsra, sweep 72; batch 56bq). Guards:
3269/// `perfect_kernel_batch53::process_key_options_sees_passed_options`,
3270/// `class_census::passed_options_keep_their_tokens`.
3271pub fn pass_options_with_raw(
3272  name: &str,
3273  ext: &str,
3274  options: Vec<String>,
3275  raw: Tokens,
3276) -> Result<()> {
3277  let key = s!("opt@{}.{}", name, ext);
3278  for opt in options {
3279    push_value(&key, arena::pin(&opt))?;
3280  }
3281  let raw_opt_cs = T_CS!(s!("\\@raw@opt@{}.{}", name, ext));
3282  // `\@ifundefined`: a macro is a record, anything else (`\relax`) is not.
3283  let previous = match lookup_meaning(&raw_opt_cs) {
3284    Some(Stored::Expandable(defn)) => match defn.get_expansion() {
3285      Some(ExpansionBody::Tokens(tokens)) => Some(tokens.clone().unlist()),
3286      None => Some(Vec::new()),
3287      Some(_) => None,
3288    },
3289    _ => None,
3290  };
3291  let body = match previous {
3292    Some(mut tokens) => {
3293      tokens.push(T_OTHER!(","));
3294      tokens.extend(raw.unlist());
3295      Tokens::new(tokens)
3296    },
3297    None => raw,
3298  };
3299  def_macro(
3300    raw_opt_cs,
3301    None,
3302    body,
3303    Some(ExpandableOptions {
3304      scope: Some(Scope::Global),
3305      ..ExpandableOptions::default()
3306    }),
3307  )?;
3308  Ok(())
3309}
3310
3311/// latex.ltx:18854-18867, after a package's end hook: options its `\ProcessOptions` marked processed (or the legacy
3312/// `\@unprocessedoptions` flag, `\relax` after kvoptions' `\ProcessKeyvalOptions`, kvoptions.sty:739) are done with;
3313/// otherwise `\@@unprocessedoptions` (:18923-18928) leaves the package's own options in `\@curroptions` — so a package
3314/// required with none sees what the last one left (epstopdf-base.sty:151). Its unknown-option errors are not raised here.
3315fn unprocessed_options(name: &str, ext: &str) -> Result<()> {
3316  let processed = remove_value(&s!("lx@options@processed@{name}.{ext}")).is_some();
3317  let legacy_relax = lookup_meaning(&T_CS!("\\@unprocessedoptions"))
3318    .is_some_and(|meaning| lookup_meaning(&T_RELAX!()).is_some_and(|relax| meaning == relax));
3319  if legacy_relax {
3320    let_i(
3321      &T_CS!("\\@unprocessedoptions"),
3322      &T_CS!("\\@undefined"),
3323      None,
3324    );
3325  }
3326  if processed || legacy_relax || ext != "sty" {
3327    return Ok(());
3328  }
3329  let opt_cs = T_CS!(s!("\\opt@{name}.{ext}"));
3330  let options = if lookup_definition(&opt_cs)?.is_some() {
3331    do_expand(opt_cs)?
3332  } else {
3333    Tokens::default()
3334  };
3335  def_macro(T_CS!("\\@curroptions"), None, options, None)
3336}
3337
3338/// Perl Package.pm L2430-2465: ProcessOptions / ProcessOptions*
3339/// `inorder=false` (\ProcessOptions) — execute in declared order, default handler for undeclared
3340/// `inorder=true` (\ProcessOptions*) — execute in order passed, class options silently skipped
3341pub fn process_options(inorder: bool, keysets: &[&str]) -> Result<()> {
3342  process_options_by(inorder, keysets, true)
3343}
3344
3345/// The options run as [`process_options`] runs them, for a package whose own options a key=value processor reads:
3346/// the kernel's `\ProcessKeyOptions` (latex.ltx:19379), kvoptions (kvoptions.sty:739), xkeyval (xkeyval.sty:132),
3347/// pgfopts (pgfopts.sty:32), l3keys2e (l3keys2e.sty:67), caption3 (caption3.sty:399). Each marks the options processed
3348/// at the package's end and leaves `\@curroptions` as it was.
3349pub fn process_key_options(inorder: bool, keysets: &[&str]) -> Result<()> {
3350  process_options_by(inorder, keysets, false)
3351}
3352
3353/// A key=value processor's mark without running options here ([`process_key_options`]): the current package's
3354/// options are processed, and `\@curroptions` stays as it was at its end.
3355pub fn key_options_processed() -> Result<()> {
3356  let name = expand_if_defined(T_CS!("\\@currname"))?;
3357  let ext = expand_if_defined(T_CS!("\\@currext"))?;
3358  assign_value(
3359    &s!("lx@options@processed@{name}.{ext}"),
3360    true,
3361    Some(Scope::Global),
3362  );
3363  Ok(())
3364}
3365
3366fn expand_if_defined(token: Token) -> Result<String> {
3367  Ok(if lookup_definition(&token)?.is_some() {
3368    do_expand(token)?.to_string()
3369  } else {
3370    String::new()
3371  })
3372}
3373
3374fn process_options_by(inorder: bool, keysets: &[&str], sets_curroptions: bool) -> Result<()> {
3375  let currname_token = T_CS!("\\@currname");
3376  let currext_token = T_CS!("\\@currext");
3377  let name = if lookup_definition(&currname_token)?.is_some() {
3378    do_expand(currname_token)?.to_string()
3379  } else {
3380    String::new()
3381  };
3382  let ext = if lookup_definition(&currext_token)?.is_some() {
3383    do_expand(currext_token)?.to_string()
3384  } else {
3385    String::new()
3386  };
3387  let declared_options: VecDeque<Stored> = lookup_vecdeque("@declaredoptions").unwrap_or_default();
3388  let opt_key = s!("opt@{}.{}", name, ext);
3389  // latex.ltx:18557 `\ProcessOptions` reads `\@curroptions` from `\@ptionlist
3390  // {\@currname.\@currext}` (:18393) — the MACRO `\opt@<name>.<ext>`, which a
3391  // package may rewrite before processing: babel.sty:316-347 strips the
3392  // `language.modifier` syntax (`greek.polutoniko` → `greek`, the modifier
3393  // kept in `\bbl@mod@greek`) by redefining `\opt@babel.sty`. Perl
3394  // (Package.pm:2430-2465) and the former Rust read the State list the loader
3395  // stored, so babel still saw `greek.polutoniko` and raised "Unknown option"
3396  // (alphabeta-doc, hyperref-with-greek; SHARED). The macro is the source of
3397  // truth when it exists (the loader defines it from the same list, so they
3398  // agree unless a package rewrote it); the list is the fallback. Guard:
3399  // `perfect_kernel_batch54::processoptions_reads_the_rewritten_opt_macro`.
3400  // The read is :18557's `\protected@edef\@curroptions{…}`: a robust or protected command in an option value stands
3401  // (`\usepackage[figurename=\textsc{Fig}]{caption}` names the figures "Fig", not `\textsc`'s expansion; a full
3402  // expansion gave two undefined-command errors). Guard: `perfect_kernel_batch68h::caption_option_values_unexpanded`.
3403  let opt_cs = T_CS!(s!("\\{opt_key}"));
3404  let current_options: VecDeque<Stored> = if lookup_definition(&opt_cs)?.is_some() {
3405    let mut items: VecDeque<Stored> = VecDeque::new();
3406    let mut cur: Vec<Token> = Vec::new();
3407    let mut depth: i32 = 0;
3408    let push_item = |cur: &mut Vec<Token>, items: &mut VecDeque<Stored>| {
3409      let text = Tokens::new(std::mem::take(cur)).to_string();
3410      let text = text.trim();
3411      if !text.is_empty() {
3412        items.push_back(Stored::String(arena::pin(text)));
3413      }
3414    };
3415    let protect_regime = T_CS!("\\@unexpandable@protect");
3416    let read = || gullet::do_expand_partially(opt_cs);
3417    let expanded = if lookup_meaning(&protect_regime).is_some() {
3418      with_let(&T_CS!("\\protect"), &protect_regime, read)?
3419    } else {
3420      read()?
3421    };
3422    for t in expanded.unlist() {
3423      match t.get_catcode() {
3424        Catcode::BEGIN => depth += 1,
3425        Catcode::END => depth -= 1,
3426        _ => {},
3427      }
3428      if depth == 0 && t.get_catcode() == Catcode::OTHER && t.with_str(|s| s == ",") {
3429        push_item(&mut cur, &mut items);
3430      } else {
3431        cur.push(t);
3432      }
3433    }
3434    push_item(&mut cur, &mut items);
3435    items
3436  } else {
3437    lookup_vecdeque(&opt_key).unwrap_or_default()
3438  };
3439  let class_options = lookup_vecdeque("class_options").unwrap_or_default();
3440
3441  let collect_syms = |vdq: &VecDeque<Stored>| -> Vec<SymStr> {
3442    let mut list = Vec::new();
3443    for item in vdq.iter() {
3444      match item {
3445        Stored::String(s) => {
3446          list.push(*s);
3447        },
3448        Stored::Strings(ss) => {
3449          for s in ss.iter() {
3450            list.push(*s);
3451          }
3452        },
3453        _ => {},
3454      }
3455    }
3456    list
3457  };
3458  let cur_options_list = collect_syms(&current_options);
3459  let cls_options_list = collect_syms(&class_options);
3460  // latex.ltx:18555-18558: `\ProcessOptions` leaves the options it reads in `\@curroptions` (a key=value processor
3461  // does not), and at the package's end (:18597-18599) marks them processed, so the loader's `\@@unprocessedoptions`
3462  // does not reset it (`unprocessed_options`). epstopdf-base.sty:151 reads it.
3463  let cur_options_text = cur_options_list
3464    .iter()
3465    .map(|option| arena::with(*option, |o| o.to_string()))
3466    .collect::<Vec<_>>()
3467    .join(",");
3468  if sets_curroptions {
3469    def_macro(
3470      T_CS!("\\@curroptions"),
3471      None,
3472      Tokens!(ExplodeText!(cur_options_text)),
3473      None,
3474    )?;
3475  }
3476  assign_value(
3477    &s!("lx@options@processed@{name}.{ext}"),
3478    true,
3479    Some(Scope::Global),
3480  );
3481
3482  if inorder {
3483    // Perl L2447-2453: ProcessOptions* — execute in the order passed
3484    // Class options: try executeOption_internal only (no default fallback)
3485    for option in &cls_options_list {
3486      let _ = execute_option_internal(*option, keysets)?;
3487    }
3488    // Current options: try executeOption, then default handler
3489    for option in &cur_options_list {
3490      if !execute_option_internal(*option, keysets)? {
3491        execute_default_option_internal(*option)?;
3492      }
3493    }
3494  } else {
3495    // Perl L2454-2461: ProcessOptions — execute in declared order
3496    let mut cur_set: HashSet<SymStr> = cur_options_list.iter().copied().collect();
3497    let mut cls_set: HashSet<SymStr> = cls_options_list.iter().copied().collect();
3498
3499    for option in declared_options.iter() {
3500      match option {
3501        Stored::String(content) if cur_set.remove(content) || cls_set.remove(content) => {
3502          execute_option_internal(*content, keysets)?;
3503        },
3504        Stored::Strings(contents) => {
3505          for content in contents.iter() {
3506            if cur_set.remove(content) || cls_set.remove(content) {
3507              execute_option_internal(*content, keysets)?;
3508            }
3509          }
3510        },
3511        _ => {},
3512      }
3513    }
3514    // Only undeclared CURRENT options go to default handler (not class options).
3515    // Perl L2460-2461: "foreach my $option (@curroptions)" — class options excluded.
3516    // Iterate cur_options_list (Vec, ordered) instead of cur_set (HashSet,
3517    // unordered) so unknown options enter `@unusedoptionlist` in source
3518    // order. Otherwise `\documentstyle[a,b,c]` produces an arbitrary
3519    // dispatch order, which breaks paper-local option chains that depend
3520    // on left-to-right evaluation (e.g. `[aaspp4,tighten]` requires
3521    // aaspp4's bindings — \tightenlines — to be defined before tighten.sty
3522    // body fires; driver: astro-ph9707180).
3523    // latex.ltx:18587-18592 `\@process@pti@ns`: such an option still runs its `\ds@<option>` when one is defined
3524    // (`\@ifundefined`: not `\relax`) — a 2.09-style class `\def`s its handlers without `\DeclareOption` (gji.cls:61-70,
3525    // 2605.19951; biom.cls:48 `\def\ds@usenatbib`, 2606.12015) — and `\default@ds` only otherwise. Perl
3526    // (Package.pm:2476-2478) sends every one to the default handler (KNOWN_PERL_ERRORS #657). Only for the kernel's
3527    // `\ProcessOptions` (`sets_curroptions`): the key=value processors (`ProcessKeyOptions!`: kvoptions, xkeyval,
3528    // l3keys) do not look an undeclared option up as `\ds@<option>`.
3529    for option in &cur_options_list {
3530      if cur_set.contains(option) && !(sets_curroptions && execute_option_internal(*option, &[])?) {
3531        execute_default_option_internal(*option)?;
3532      }
3533    }
3534  }
3535  // Now, undefine the handlers
3536  for option in declared_options.iter() {
3537    let_i(&T_CS!(s!("\\ds@{}", option)), &T_RELAX!(), None);
3538  }
3539  Ok(())
3540}
3541
3542/// Whether expl3 syntax is on, by expl3's own flag: expl3-code.tex:242 `\chardef\l__kernel_expl_bool`, set to 1 by
3543/// `\ExplSyntaxOn` and back to 0 by the `\ExplSyntaxOff` it arms (:243-272). False before expl3 is loaded.
3544fn expl_syntax_on() -> bool {
3545  lookup_definition(&T_CS!("\\l__kernel_expl_bool"))
3546    .ok()
3547    .flatten()
3548    .filter(|defn| defn.is_register())
3549    .and_then(|defn| defn.value_of(Vec::new()))
3550    .is_some_and(|value| crate::common::numeric_ops::NumericOps::value_of(value) == 1)
3551}
3552
3553fn execute_option_internal(option: SymStr, keysets: &[&str]) -> Result<bool> {
3554  if let Some((qname, value)) = keyval_option_qname(option, keysets) {
3555    // Perl Package.pm handles `key=value` package options before normal
3556    // `\ds@...` lookup when ProcessOptions was given keysets. It digests
3557    // `\KV@<keyset>@<key>{<value>}`. Rust DefKeyVal entries without code
3558    // are ordinary macros, so also store the value under the qname for
3559    // package bindings that apply keyvals after ProcessOptions.
3560    assign_value(
3561      &qname,
3562      Stored::String(arena::pin(value.trim())),
3563      Some(Scope::Global),
3564    );
3565    // The value is the option's ARGUMENT tokens (latex.ltx:18514 stores them
3566    // by `\protected@xdef`): standard catcodes, braces group, and keyval
3567    // strips one brace level (`\KV@@sp@def`) — `vmargin={0mm,0mm}` hands
3568    // `0mm,0mm` to the key, not `{0mm,0mm}` with OTHER braces (geometry
3569    // package options gave "Missing number" ×4; elzcards root-causer).
3570    let bare = value
3571      .strip_prefix('{')
3572      .and_then(|v| v.strip_suffix('}'))
3573      .unwrap_or(&value);
3574    let mut call = vec![T_CS!(s!("\\{qname}")), T_BEGIN!()];
3575    call.extend(option_argument_tokens(bare).unlist());
3576    call.push(T_END!());
3577    digest(Tokens::new(call))?;
3578    return Ok(true);
3579  }
3580
3581  let cs = T_CS!(arena::with(option, |opt| s!("\\ds@{opt}")));
3582  // latex.ltx `\@use@ption`: `\@ifundefined{ds@<option>}` — a handler
3583  // `\let` to `\relax` (the class's `landscape`/`a4paper` after its own
3584  // `\ProcessOptions`, cleared below) counts as UNDEFINED, so the package's
3585  // `\DeclareOption*` default runs for it (geometry's `landscape,a4paper`
3586  // were silently swallowed as "declared"; Perl Package.pm:2481-2486 retains
3587  // the swallow — Rust matches `\@ifundefined`, OXIDIZED_DESIGN_DIVERGENCES
3588  // #223).
3589  if lookup_definition(&cs)?.is_some() && !x_equals(&cs, &T_RELAX!()) {
3590    // Perl Package.pm L2482: `DefMacroI('\CurrentOption', undef, $option)` —
3591    // tokenizes `$option` via Tokens(Explode($option)) so letters get
3592    // catcode LETTER and others OTHER. Babel's `\ifx\CurrentOption\bbl@tempa`
3593    // (where `\bbl@tempa{frenchb}` produces LETTER tokens) only matches when
3594    // our `\CurrentOption` body has the same catcodes. Real LaTeX's
3595    // `\CurrentOption` is the ARGUMENT tokens (latex.ltx:18514), so braces
3596    // GROUP: geometry's `\DeclareOption*{…\CurrentOption}` → `\setkeys*{Gm}
3597    // {vmargin={0mm,0mm}}` needs them (OTHER braces split the pair at its
3598    // comma: "Missing number" ×4). Standard-catcode tokenization keeps
3599    // babel's LETTER match and the braces.
3600    def_macro(
3601      T_CS!("\\CurrentOption"),
3602      None,
3603      option_argument_tokens(&arena::to_string(option)),
3604      None,
3605    )?;
3606
3607    let unused = match remove_vecdeque("@unusedoptionlist") {
3608      Some(list) => list
3609        .into_iter()
3610        .filter(|item| {
3611          if let Stored::String(content) = item {
3612            *content != option
3613          } else {
3614            false
3615          }
3616        })
3617        .collect(),
3618      None => VecDeque::new(),
3619    };
3620    assign_value("@unusedoptionlist", Stored::VecDequeStored(unused), None);
3621    digest(cs)?;
3622    Ok(true)
3623  } else {
3624    Ok(false)
3625  }
3626}
3627
3628/// An option's ARGUMENT tokens (latex.ltx:18514 `\@pass@ptions` stores them by
3629/// `\protected@xdef`): letters LETTER (babel's `\ifx\CurrentOption\bbl@tempa`
3630/// match), braces GROUP (`vmargin={0mm,0mm}` is one pair for `\setkeys`), and
3631/// `@` a LETTER too — the list crossed a string, and a control word in it
3632/// came from package/class code where `@` is a letter (xebaposter.cls passes
3633/// `paperwidth=\xebaposter@finalpaperwidth`, a register token, to geometry;
3634/// re-read with `@` OTHER it split into the undefined `\xebaposter`; the
3635/// `\@raw@opt@` writer makes the same choice) — without the tokenizer's
3636/// end-of-line space, which is not part of the argument (`landscape␣` matched
3637/// no key).
3638pub fn option_argument_tokens(text: &str) -> Tokens {
3639  let mut toks = crate::mouth::tokenize_internal(TeXString::assembled(text.to_string())).unlist();
3640  while toks
3641    .last()
3642    .is_some_and(|t| t.get_catcode() == Catcode::SPACE)
3643  {
3644    toks.pop();
3645  }
3646  Tokens::new(toks)
3647}
3648
3649fn keyval_option_qname(option: SymStr, keysets: &[&str]) -> Option<(String, String)> {
3650  if keysets.is_empty() {
3651    return None;
3652  }
3653  let (key, value) = arena::with(option, |opt| {
3654    opt
3655      .split_once('=')
3656      .map(|(key, value)| (key.trim().to_string(), value.trim().to_string()))
3657  })?;
3658  if key.is_empty() {
3659    return None;
3660  }
3661  for keyset in keysets {
3662    let qname = crate::keyval::keyval_qname("KV", keyset, &key);
3663    if crate::keyval::keyval_get(&qname, "type").is_some() {
3664      return Some((qname, value));
3665    }
3666  }
3667  None
3668}
3669
3670fn execute_default_option_internal(option: SymStr) -> Result<bool> {
3671  // Perl Package.pm L2494: `DefMacroI('\CurrentOption', undef, $option)`.
3672  // Same argument-token spelling as execute_option_internal.
3673  def_macro(
3674    T_CS!("\\CurrentOption"),
3675    None,
3676    option_argument_tokens(&arena::to_string(option)),
3677    None,
3678  )?;
3679  digest(T_CS!("\\default@ds"))?;
3680  Ok(true)
3681}
3682
3683fn reset_options() -> Result<()> {
3684  assign_value(
3685    "@declaredoptions",
3686    Stored::VecDequeStored(VecDeque::new()),
3687    None,
3688  );
3689  let opt_unused_cs = if do_expand(T_CS!("\\@currext"))?.eq_text("cls") {
3690    "\\OptionNotUsed"
3691  } else {
3692    "\\@unknownoptionerror"
3693  };
3694  let_i(&T_CS!("\\default@ds"), &T_CS!(opt_unused_cs), None);
3695  Ok(())
3696}
3697
3698/// Execute a list of options (Perl: ExecuteOptions).
3699/// Tries each option's \ds@{option} definition; logs unexpected ones.
3700pub fn execute_options(options: &[&str]) -> Result<()> {
3701  let mut unhandled = Vec::new();
3702  for option in options {
3703    let sym = arena::pin(*option);
3704    if !execute_option_internal(sym, &[])? {
3705      unhandled.push(*option);
3706    }
3707  }
3708  for option in &unhandled {
3709    Info!(
3710      "unexpected",
3711      *option,
3712      s!("Unexpected options passed to ExecuteOptions '{option}'")
3713    );
3714  }
3715  Ok(())
3716}
3717
3718pub struct RequireOptions {
3719  pub options:          Vec<String>,
3720  pub withoptions:      Option<Vec<String>>,
3721  pub extension:        Option<Cow<'static, str>>,
3722  pub searchpaths_only: bool,
3723  pub as_class:         bool,
3724  pub noltxml:          Option<bool>,
3725  pub notex:            Option<bool>,
3726  pub after:            Tokens,
3727}
3728impl Default for RequireOptions {
3729  fn default() -> Self {
3730    RequireOptions {
3731      options:          Vec::new(),
3732      withoptions:      None,
3733      extension:        None,
3734      notex:            None,
3735      noltxml:          None,
3736      as_class:         false,
3737      searchpaths_only: false,
3738      after:            Tokens!(),
3739    }
3740  }
3741}
3742
3743/// An opinionated binding for \RequirePackage.
3744///
3745/// This (and `FindFile`) needs to evolve a bit to support reading raw .sty (.def, etc) files from
3746/// the standard texmf directories.  Maybe even use kpsewhich itself (INSTEAD of `pathname_find`
3747/// ???) Another potentially useful option might be that if we are reading a raw file,
3748/// perhaps it should just get digested immediately, since it shouldn't contribute any boxes.
3749/// OXIDIZED_DESIGN #177: a package requested by a source-tree relative path
3750/// (`\usepackage{../tex/tikzpingus}` — the CTAN layout where `doc/` and
3751/// `tex/` are siblings; 61 TL doc manuals) falls back to its basename when
3752/// the literal path resolves nowhere but the basename does. pdflatex and
3753/// Perl both fail in the installed tree (kpathsea misses `../tex/`), and the
3754/// whole manual's error mass follows from the package never loading. Only a
3755/// MISS is rerouted: a path that resolves (a paper's own `./mypkg`,
3756/// arXiv 0906.3507) still loads the local file. Guard:
3757/// `perfect_kernel_batch54::relative_package_path_falls_back_to_basename`.
3758/// A path that resolves to the very file its basename does (`\usepackage{graphics/graphicx}`, kpathsea's
3759/// `tex/latex/graphics/graphicx.sty`) is that package, and loads through the basename's binding when it has one:
3760/// the raw graphicx.sty over our graphics binding exhausted memory at the first `\rotatebox` (2309.12794).
3761fn source_tree_basename<'a>(name: &'a str, ext: &str) -> Cow<'a, str> {
3762  if !name.contains('/') {
3763    return Cow::Borrowed(name);
3764  }
3765  let base = name.rsplit('/').next().unwrap_or(name);
3766  if let Some(path) = find_file(&s!("{name}.{ext}"), None) {
3767    if !base.is_empty()
3768      && binding_exists(base, ext)
3769      && find_file(&s!("{base}.{ext}"), None).as_deref() == Some(path.as_str())
3770    {
3771      Info!(
3772        "loading",
3773        name,
3774        s!("'{name}.{ext}' is the installed '{base}.{ext}'; loading it as '{base}'")
3775      );
3776      return Cow::Owned(base.to_string());
3777    }
3778    return Cow::Borrowed(name);
3779  }
3780  if base.is_empty() || find_file(&s!("{base}.{ext}"), None).is_none() {
3781    return Cow::Borrowed(name);
3782  }
3783  Info!(
3784    "loading",
3785    name,
3786    s!("'{name}.{ext}' is not on disk; loading the installed '{base}.{ext}' instead")
3787  );
3788  Cow::Owned(base.to_string())
3789}
3790
3791pub fn require_package(name: &str, mut options: RequireOptions) -> Result<()> {
3792  let ext = options.extension.as_deref().unwrap_or("sty").to_string();
3793  // A `"` quotes spaces in a file name and is not part of it (tex.ch `more_name`, as `tex_file_name` reads `\input`):
3794  // `\usepackage{"mydefs"}` opens `mydefs.sty` (2407.00688: not found, its `\r`/`\b`/`\g` kept their accent meanings).
3795  let unquoted: Cow<str> = if name.contains('"') {
3796    Cow::Owned(name.replace('"', ""))
3797  } else {
3798    Cow::Borrowed(name)
3799  };
3800  let name = source_tree_basename(&unquoted, &ext);
3801  let name = name.as_ref();
3802  // A stand-in's copy read after its binding (`read_stand_in_copy`) runs the very `\usepackage`/`\RequirePackage`
3803  // lines the binding's dependency scan stands in for, so they take the scan's test (`marked_loaded_unrecorded`,
3804  // latex.ltx:18750 `\@ifl@aded`): a package the document marked loaded is skipped (sn-jnl.cls:311
3805  // `\usepackage{program}` under sn-jnl-setup.sty's `\AddToHook{class/sn-jnl/before}`, 2201.11056).
3806  if crate::definition::origin::copy_provides() != 0 && marked_loaded_unrecorded(name, &ext) {
3807    return Ok(());
3808  }
3809  // Perl Package.pm L2671-2672: notex defaults to true unless the user
3810  // explicitly set it, or INCLUDE_STYLES is true, or noltxml was passed
3811  // (a raw-only load explicitly requests raw TeX).
3812  // A source registered in `INTERPRETABLE_SOURCES` is one a binding (or the
3813  // kernel) has vouched for as safe to interpret — that is the point of the
3814  // registration — so it is raw-loaded even without `--includestyles`
3815  // (physics2.sty and its `phy-*` modules, fontawesome7.sty:
3816  // latex_constructs_rust_only.rs §10; the alternative was the wrong
3817  // glued-suffix fallback binding). Perl gates it on `notex` too, and so
3818  // misloads physics2 under its default configuration.
3819  if options.notex.is_none()
3820    && !lookup_bool("INCLUDE_STYLES")
3821    && !matches!(options.noltxml, Some(true))
3822    && lookup_mapping("INTERPRETABLE_SOURCES", &s!("{name}.{ext}")).is_none()
3823  {
3824    options.notex = Some(true);
3825  }
3826  // Perl Package.pm L2674: top-level \RequirePackage can be limited to
3827  // local sources via searchpaths_only. Triggered by the `localrawstyles`
3828  // option to latexml.sty (sets `INCLUDE_STYLES => 'searchpaths'`).
3829  // Only applies when raw TeX is allowed (notex==false); otherwise the
3830  // gate is moot since find_file won't search on-disk anyway.
3831  if !options.searchpaths_only
3832    && !matches!(options.notex, Some(true))
3833    && lookup_string("INCLUDE_STYLES") == "searchpaths"
3834  {
3835    options.searchpaths_only = true;
3836  }
3837  if options.extension.is_none() {
3838    options.extension = Some("sty".into());
3839  }
3840  // OXIDIZED_DESIGN #65 (#311): a package loaded inside a bracket LaTeXML ITSELF
3841  // opened must still end up defined at the outermost level, so hoist the load's
3842  // meaning-delta past it. Real LaTeX has no such bracket — `\@fileswithoptions`
3843  // (latex.ltx L18700) refuses to load at `\currentgrouplevel > 0` — but we
3844  // execute a subfile preamble the real `standalone.sty` gobbles (divergence
3845  // #63), which splits the package in half: frame-local definitions, global
3846  // hooks. Witness: `pgfcoreexternal.code.tex` L152
3847  // `\newif\ifpgf@external@grabshipout` popped with the child while its L171-179
3848  // `\AtEndDocument` reaches the parent's `\end{document}`. `tex.rs::def_autoload`
3849  // already used this snapshot/hoist pair for the mirror-image autoload failure
3850  // (witness 1711.11576).
3851  //
3852  // `subfile:<depth>`, not a bare depth test: a group the AUTHOR wrote must keep real
3853  // LaTeX's verdict — `{\usepackage{amsthm}}` leaves `\theoremstyle` undefined in
3854  // pdflatex and Perl alike, and rescuing it would emit fewer errors than Perl on
3855  // an authoring mistake (guards
3856  // `06_cluster_regressions::author_written_group_around_usepackage_still_loses_the_package`,
3857  // and `100_stale_autoload_no_runaway` from a fresh process). The
3858  // two cases are indistinguishable by anything cheaper: both are a group opened
3859  // inside the current file, while `inPreamble` is true, at `\currentgrouplevel`
3860  // 1. Refuted alternatives in #65 — dropping the brackets, and `\globaldefs=1`.
3861  let pre_keys = if is_scope_active(subfile_scope_here()) {
3862    Some(snapshot_top_frame_meaning_keys())
3863  } else {
3864    None
3865  };
3866  let subfile_load = SubfilePackageLoad::enter(pre_keys.is_some());
3867  let result = input_definitions(name, InputDefinitionOptions {
3868    extension: options.extension,
3869    handleoptions: true,
3870    // Pass classes options if we have NONE!
3871    withoptions: if options.options.is_empty() {
3872      Some(Vec::new())
3873    } else {
3874      None
3875    }, // fake boolean use, multi-type in latexml... refactor?
3876    options: options.options,
3877    as_class: options.as_class,
3878    noltxml: options.noltxml.unwrap_or(false),
3879    notex: options.notex.unwrap_or(false),
3880    searchpaths_only: options.searchpaths_only,
3881    after: options.after,
3882    ..InputDefinitionOptions::default()
3883  });
3884  drop(subfile_load);
3885  if let Some(pre_keys) = pre_keys {
3886    hoist_top_frame_conditional_delta(&pre_keys);
3887  }
3888  // Perl Package.pm L2679 maybeRequireDependencies is invoked from
3889  // input_definitions's miss-handler. But that handler only runs when
3890  // the file was NOT found at all. For paper-bundled .sty files that
3891  // raw-load successfully without a .sty.ltxml binding, we still need
3892  // to scan transitive \RequirePackage so that bound deps fire too.
3893  // Mirrors the same fix for load_class (search "cls.ltxml_loaded").
3894  // Witness 2208.07400 (paper-bundled emnlp2022.sty has
3895  // \RequirePackage{caption} + others; without scan, \captionsetup
3896  // and similar are undefined).
3897  if !lookup_bool(&s!("{name}.sty.ltxml_loaded")) {
3898    maybe_require_dependencies(name, "sty");
3899  }
3900  result
3901}
3902
3903/// Load the binding of package `name` for another binding to build on, without the package counting as loaded — a
3904/// binding's way of borrowing a package the real one does not load (theorem.sty's and ntheorem.sty's bindings
3905/// borrow amsthm's theorem machinery; neither real package loads amsthm). Unless the document had loaded `name`
3906/// already, it is then marked unloaded ([`crate::state::mark_unloaded`]), and so is every file first loaded during the
3907/// borrow (amsthm's amsgen, [`crate::state::end_borrow`]): `\@ifpackageloaded{name}` is false, as in LaTeX, and the
3908/// document's own later `\usepackage{name}` loads the binding again, whose definitions then take over (amsthm.sty:77's
3909/// `\newtheorem`, amsthm_sty.rs `\lx@amsthm@newtheorem`); `"{name}.sty:borrowed"` tells that load it follows a
3910/// borrow. What the borrow defines carries `DefinitionOrigin::Borrowed`: in LaTeX those names do not exist, so they
3911/// yield to any later definition — the document's, or any raw file's, TeX Live's or the author's (`\newcommand`,
3912/// `\newenvironment`, `\providecommand`; latex_constructs `is_definable_latex`) — and the binding sets no lock while
3913/// `"{name}.sty:borrowing"` is set (amsthm's proof lock). The names the borrowing package's real file defines too the
3914/// binding claims as its own ([`crate::state::claim_borrowed_meanings`]: ntheorem's `[amsthm]` proof, theorem.sty's
3915/// skips), and they do not yield. Not covered: a conditional (`\@ifundefined{proof}`) still
3916/// sees a borrowed name defined, and a local assignment of a borrowed name clears its mark for good
3917/// (`fallback_meanings`). Witnesses 2211.14033, 2512.06733 (ifacconf), 1507.02955 (cc.cls's ccproof.sty:56). Guards
3918/// `perfect_kernel_batch67p::{theorem_then_amsthm_takes_over, amsthm_before_theorem_stays_loaded}`,
3919/// `perfect_kernel_batch67ps::*`.
3920pub fn require_package_borrowed(name: &str) -> Result<()> {
3921  use crate::definition::origin::{DefinitionOrigin, OriginGuard};
3922  let file = s!("{name}.sty");
3923  let was_loaded = lookup_bool(&s!("{file}_loaded"));
3924  if was_loaded {
3925    return require_package(name, RequireOptions::default());
3926  }
3927  /// Clears `"{file}:borrowing"` and ends the borrow's file tracking on every path out of the load.
3928  struct Borrowing(String, Option<Vec<String>>);
3929  impl Drop for Borrowing {
3930    fn drop(&mut self) {
3931      assign_value(&s!("{}:borrowing", self.0), false, Some(Scope::Global));
3932      if let Some(outer) = self.1.take() {
3933        end_borrow(outer);
3934      }
3935    }
3936  }
3937  assign_value(&s!("{file}:borrowing"), true, Some(Scope::Global));
3938  let borrowing = Borrowing(file.clone(), Some(begin_borrow()));
3939  let result = {
3940    let _origin = OriginGuard::new(DefinitionOrigin::Borrowed);
3941    require_package(name, RequireOptions::default())
3942  };
3943  drop(borrowing);
3944  // (the borrowed package itself, also when it was tracked under another name)
3945  mark_unloaded(&file);
3946  assign_value(&s!("{file}:borrowed"), true, Some(Scope::Global));
3947  result
3948}
3949
3950thread_local! {
3951  /// How many package loads in a standalone subfile bracket ([`require_package`]) are running.
3952  static SUBFILE_PACKAGE_LOAD: std::cell::Cell<usize> = const { std::cell::Cell::new(0) };
3953}
3954
3955/// One package load's place in [`SUBFILE_PACKAGE_LOAD`]: entering counts the load when it runs in a
3956/// standalone subfile bracket, and dropping restores the outer count — also when the load unwinds (a
3957/// panic caught per paper by a reused worker thread), so a later conversion's `AddToMacro` is not
3958/// left local.
3959struct SubfilePackageLoad {
3960  outer: usize,
3961}
3962
3963impl SubfilePackageLoad {
3964  fn enter(in_bracket: bool) -> Self {
3965    let outer = SUBFILE_PACKAGE_LOAD.with(|depth| {
3966      let outer = depth.get();
3967      if in_bracket {
3968        depth.set(outer + 1);
3969      }
3970      outer
3971    });
3972    SubfilePackageLoad { outer }
3973  }
3974}
3975
3976impl Drop for SubfilePackageLoad {
3977  fn drop(&mut self) { let _ = SUBFILE_PACKAGE_LOAD.try_with(|depth| depth.set(self.outer)); }
3978}
3979
3980/// Clears the count of package loads in a standalone subfile bracket, for a thread that starts over
3981/// ([`crate::reset_thread_engine`]).
3982pub fn reset_subfile_package_load() { SUBFILE_PACKAGE_LOAD.with(|depth| depth.set(0)); }
3983
3984/// The scope of a binding's `AddToMacro`: global (Perl Package.pm:2545-2547), except in a package
3985/// loaded inside a standalone subfile bracket (OXIDIZED_DESIGN #63/#65), where the package's own
3986/// definitions are local to the bracket: a global addition outlived them, and every later table of
3987/// the main document called xcolor's undefined `\@xcolor@tabular@before` (2512.09430; Perl the
3988/// same, KNOWN_PERL_ERRORS #614). The addition then lives as long as the package does — and as in
3989/// pdflatex, where standalone.sty skips the sub-file's preamble, the main document's tables carry
3990/// no hook of a package only a sub-file loaded. OXIDIZED_DESIGN #543.
3991pub fn add_to_macro_scope() -> Scope {
3992  if SUBFILE_PACKAGE_LOAD.with(std::cell::Cell::get) > 0 {
3993    Scope::Local
3994  } else {
3995    Scope::Global
3996  }
3997}
3998
3999/// The calling file's option list — latex.ltx:18637 `\@loadwithoptions`
4000/// `\let`s `\opt@<callee>` to `\opt@\@currname.\@currext`, the shared half
4001/// of `\RequirePackageWithOptions` and `\LoadClassWithOptions` (Perl
4002/// `withoptions => 1`, Package.pm:2610 `opt@prevname.prevext`). Empty when no
4003/// file is being loaded.
4004pub fn calling_file_options() -> Result<Vec<String>> {
4005  let currname = if lookup_definition(&T_CS!("\\@currname"))?.is_some() {
4006    do_expand(T_CS!("\\@currname"))?.to_string()
4007  } else {
4008    String::new()
4009  };
4010  let currext = if lookup_definition(&T_CS!("\\@currext"))?.is_some() {
4011    do_expand(T_CS!("\\@currext"))?.to_string()
4012  } else {
4013    String::new()
4014  };
4015  Ok(if !currname.is_empty() {
4016    let key = s!("opt@{}.{}", currname, currext);
4017    lookup_vecdeque(&key)
4018      .unwrap_or_default()
4019      .iter()
4020      .filter_map(|item| match item {
4021        Stored::String(s) => Some(arena::to_string(*s)),
4022        _ => None,
4023      })
4024      .collect()
4025  } else {
4026    Vec::new()
4027  })
4028}
4029
4030/// Perl: `RequirePackage($name, withoptions => 1)` — forward the calling
4031/// package/class's options ([`calling_file_options`]) to the required child
4032/// package as its explicit options list. Mirrors `load_class_with_options`
4033/// for the package path.
4034pub fn require_package_with_options(name: &str) -> Result<()> {
4035  let options = calling_file_options()?;
4036  require_package(name, RequireOptions {
4037    options,
4038    ..RequireOptions::default()
4039  })
4040}
4041
4042/// Perl Package.pm L2759-2796: maybeRequireDependencies
4043/// When a package/class file has no binding AND raw TeX loading is disabled,
4044/// scan the raw file for \RequirePackage/\usepackage/\LoadClass declarations
4045/// and load any dependencies that DO have bindings. This is a "best effort"
4046/// fallback that gives us the dependency chain without interpreting raw TeX.
4047// Strict translation of Perl `Package.pm:maybeRequireDependencies`
4048// (L2759-L2796). Scan a raw .sty/.cls file for transitive
4049// `\RequirePackage`, `\usepackage`, and (for classes) `\LoadClass`
4050// declarations and route them through `require_package` / `load_class`
4051// so the corresponding bindings get pulled in even when the original
4052// file has no .ltxml binding.
4053/// The `\addbibresource` names declared in a raw `.sty`/`.cls` body.
4054///
4055/// Split out of [`maybe_require_dependencies`] so the extraction is testable
4056/// without state: it is the whole of the BEYOND-PERL behaviour described there.
4057/// A comma-separated argument declares several resources at once, as biblatex
4058/// allows.
4059fn scan_bib_resources(code: &str) -> Vec<String> {
4060  use once_cell::sync::Lazy;
4061  use regex::Regex;
4062  static BIBRES_RE: Lazy<Regex> =
4063    Lazy::new(|| Regex::new(r"\\addbibresource\s*(?:\[[^\]]*\])?\s*\{([^\}]*)\}").unwrap());
4064  let mut out = Vec::new();
4065  for cap in BIBRES_RE.captures_iter(code) {
4066    for part in cap[1].split(',') {
4067      let name = part.trim();
4068      // A name holding a control sequence (`\jobname.bib`, incgraph-doc.sty:58;
4069      // `\gitLog@bibfile`, gitlog.sty:111; cleanthesis `\cthesis@bibfile`) is
4070      // resolvable only at runtime, where the native `\addbibresource[]
4071      // Expanded` (biblatex_sty.rs) already pushes its expansion; the literal
4072      // can never name a file, and `\biblatex@printbibliography`'s exact-string
4073      // dedup kept both, so MakeBibliography reported the literal as a
4074      // missing bibliography (`Error:missing_file:\jobname.bib`; batch 56ga).
4075      if !name.is_empty() && !name.contains('\\') && !out.iter().any(|o: &String| o == name) {
4076        out.push(name.to_string());
4077      }
4078    }
4079  }
4080  out
4081}
4082
4083#[cfg(test)]
4084mod bib_resource_scan_tests {
4085  use super::scan_bib_resources;
4086
4087  /// A resource named through a control sequence is left to the runtime path.
4088  #[test]
4089  fn unexpanded_macro_names_are_skipped() {
4090    assert_eq!(
4091      scan_bib_resources(
4092        "\\addbibresource{\\jobname.bib}\n\\addbibresource{\\gitLog@bibfile}\n\\addbibresource{refs.bib}"
4093      ),
4094      vec!["refs.bib".to_string()]
4095    );
4096  }
4097
4098  /// A journal class shipped with the paper declares the bibliography itself —
4099  /// `journaleducation.cls` (witness 2605.23724, 0 -> 35 entries),
4100  /// `cai26.cls` (2605.00270, 0 -> 25), `tau.cls` (2605.02720, 0 -> 31) — and
4101  /// the document then only writes `\printbibliography`. We never interpret
4102  /// that class, so this scan is the only place the declaration is visible.
4103  #[test]
4104  fn harvests_addbibresource_from_a_shipped_class() {
4105    assert_eq!(
4106      scan_bib_resources(r"\addbibresource{references.bib}"),
4107      vec!["references.bib"]
4108    );
4109    // A path, as journaleducation.cls writes it.
4110    assert_eq!(
4111      scan_bib_resources(r"\addbibresource{letters/refs.bib}"),
4112      vec!["letters/refs.bib"]
4113    );
4114    // biblatex's optional argument is skipped, not treated as the resource.
4115    assert_eq!(
4116      scan_bib_resources(r"\addbibresource[location=local]{tau.bib}"),
4117      vec!["tau.bib"]
4118    );
4119    // Several resources, and repeats collapse — naming a `.bib` twice would
4120    // make `\lx@bibliography` read it twice and double every entry.
4121    assert_eq!(
4122      scan_bib_resources("\\addbibresource{a.bib, b.bib}\n\\addbibresource{a.bib}"),
4123      vec!["a.bib", "b.bib"]
4124    );
4125    // Nothing to harvest is the common case and must stay empty.
4126    assert!(scan_bib_resources(r"\RequirePackage{biblatex}").is_empty());
4127  }
4128}
4129
4130fn maybe_require_dependencies(file: &str, ext_type: &str) {
4131  require_dependencies_except(file, ext_type, &[]);
4132}
4133
4134/// Perl's `maybeRequireDependencies` (Package.pm:2776-2813) for `file` — load
4135/// the classes and packages its source names that have a binding — except the
4136/// classes and packages in `except`, for a binding that stands in for an
4137/// unbound class and chooses or has already loaded those itself.
4138pub fn require_dependencies_except(file: &str, ext_type: &str, except: &[&str]) {
4139  require_dependencies_filtered(file, ext_type, &|name| !except.contains(&name));
4140}
4141
4142/// The same scan kept to the classes and packages in `only`: a binding that stands in for a file it does not
4143/// interpret, and loads its own choice of that file's packages, leaves to the paper's copy of the file the ones
4144/// whose choice differs between copies (the algorithm packages: icml2017.sty:72-73, which a paper edits to drop
4145/// `algorithmic` for `algpseudocode`; bmvc2k.cls, `algorithmic` or `algpseudocode` by year). The copy's
4146/// `\addbibresource` files are registered as by every scan (the bibliography deduplicates them). `false` when the
4147/// source has no such file (or it cannot be read), or when another scan is running (the re-entrancy guard), so the
4148/// binding takes its own default.
4149pub fn require_dependencies_only(file: &str, ext_type: &str, only: &[&str]) -> bool {
4150  require_dependencies_filtered(file, ext_type, &|name| only.contains(&name))
4151}
4152
4153/// [`require_dependencies_except`] / [`require_dependencies_only`]: scan `file` and load the classes and packages
4154/// it names for which `keep` holds; `false` when there is no file to scan.
4155fn require_dependencies_filtered(file: &str, ext_type: &str, keep: &dyn Fn(&str) -> bool) -> bool {
4156  use once_cell::sync::Lazy;
4157  use regex::Regex;
4158
4159  // Rust-only re-entrancy guard. Perl avoids this case by other means
4160  // (the call-site of `maybeRequireDependencies` is the only entry).
4161  thread_local! { static SCANNING: std::cell::Cell<bool> = const { std::cell::Cell::new(false) }; }
4162  if SCANNING.with(|s| s.get()) {
4163    return false;
4164  }
4165  SCANNING.with(|s| s.set(true));
4166  struct ResetGuard;
4167  impl Drop for ResetGuard {
4168    fn drop(&mut self) { SCANNING.with(|s| s.set(false)); }
4169  }
4170  let _guard = ResetGuard;
4171
4172  // EXECUTED-SET GATE (Rust-only, more-robust-than-Perl). When this file was
4173  // actually RAW-LOADED, its `\usepackage`/`\RequirePackage` constructors ran
4174  // for every require the load REACHED — recording `<pkg>.usepackage_executed`.
4175  // A candidate the regex finds in the text but that is NOT in that set is one
4176  // whose `\usepackage` never executed — i.e. it sits inside a FALSE `\if…\fi`
4177  // the raw-load already skipped (e.g. `\ifpdf … \usepackage{hyperref} … \fi`
4178  // with `\ifpdf` false). Perl never dep-scans a raw-loaded file, so it never
4179  // anticipates such a package; mirror that by skipping it. The gate applies
4180  // ONLY when the file raw-loaded (key `<file>.<ext>_raw_loaded`): in the
4181  // miss-handler / `INCLUDE_STYLES=false` path no constructor ran, so the set
4182  // is empty and we must NOT filter (keep the anticipation). The flag is
4183  // cumulative+global, so a candidate that DID execute anywhere is kept — only
4184  // never-executed ones are dropped, which can never be a false skip.
4185  // Witnesses 1910.05586 (hyperref in false `\ifpdf` → cleveref "must be loaded
4186  // after hyperref"), 1804.09301 (xcolor in false `\ifacl@hyperref`).
4187  let raw_loaded = lookup_bool(&s!("{file}.{ext_type}_raw_loaded"));
4188
4189  // Perl L2776: `s/%[^\n]*\n//gs` — drop comment AND its trailing newline,
4190  // replacement is the empty string.
4191  // A comment on a last line without its newline is one too (Perl's pattern keeps it): a `{filecontents}` copy is
4192  // stored without that newline, so an edited-out `%\RequirePackage{algorithmic}` ending one was loaded (batch 67l,
4193  // repro icml_style_copy_without_algorithmic.tex).
4194  static COMMENT_RE: Lazy<Regex> = Lazy::new(|| Regex::new(r"%[^\n]*(?:\n|$)").unwrap());
4195  // `comment`-package block: `\begin{comment}…\end{comment}` is a verbatim-SKIP
4196  // environment, so a `\usepackage`/`\RequirePackage` inside it is NEVER loaded
4197  // by LaTeX. The dep-scan must not anticipate it. Same "more-robust than Perl"
4198  // rationale as the macro-def-body skip below. Witness 1901.05713: thesis.sty
4199  // has a commented-out `\usepackage{hyperref}` inside `\begin{comment}`, which
4200  // the scan otherwise loaded — tripping cleveref's "must be loaded after
4201  // hyperref" `\AtBeginDocument` order-check (hyperref appears loaded though the
4202  // author commented it out). `comment` doesn't nest, so a non-greedy match to
4203  // the first `\end{comment}` is correct.
4204  static COMMENT_ENV_RE: Lazy<Regex> =
4205    Lazy::new(|| Regex::new(r"(?s)\\begin\s*\{comment\}.*?\\end\s*\{comment\}").unwrap());
4206  // Perl L2777-2779 runs two separate substitutions, in this order:
4207  // first `\RequirePackage`, then `\usepackage`. Use two regexes so that
4208  // collected order matches Perl's call order to `$collect`.
4209  static REQ_RE: Lazy<Regex> =
4210    Lazy::new(|| Regex::new(r"\\RequirePackage\s*(?:\[([^\]]*)\])?\s*\{([^\}]*)\}").unwrap());
4211  static USE_RE: Lazy<Regex> =
4212    Lazy::new(|| Regex::new(r"\\usepackage\s*(?:\[([^\]]*)\])?\s*\{([^\}]*)\}").unwrap());
4213  static CLS_RE: Lazy<Regex> =
4214    Lazy::new(|| Regex::new(r"\\LoadClass\s*(?:\[([^\]]*)\])?\s*\{([^\}]*)\}").unwrap());
4215  // Matches a `\newcommand`/`\def`-family DEFINITION HEADER ending exactly at a
4216  // `{` — i.e. the brace that follows opens the macro BODY. Used to detect a
4217  // `\usepackage` that lives in a deferred macro body (loads only when the
4218  // macro is later expanded) vs one inside a load-time conditional.
4219  static DEF_BODY_HEADER_RE: Lazy<Regex> = Lazy::new(|| {
4220    Regex::new(
4221      r"(?s)(?:\\(?:re|provide)?newcommand|\\DeclareRobustCommand)\*?\s*(?:\{\s*\\[A-Za-z@]+\s*\}|\\[A-Za-z@]+)\s*(?:\[[^\]]*\]\s*)*$|\\[egx]?def\s*\\[A-Za-z@]+[^{}]*$",
4222    )
4223    .unwrap()
4224  });
4225  // The `\def`-family sub-case of DEF_BODY_HEADER_RE, capturing the defined
4226  // macro NAME (without backslash). A `\def\<m>{… \RequirePackage{P} …}` body
4227  // is only truly deferred if `\<m>` is never invoked; the AMS-class idiom
4228  // `\def\@tempa{\RequirePackage{amsmath}}…\@tempa` invokes it immediately and
4229  // so DOES load P (witness ijnam.cls → amsmath → `{aligned}`, 1911.03415).
4230  static DEF_NAME_RE: Lazy<Regex> =
4231    Lazy::new(|| Regex::new(r"(?s)\\[egx]?def\s*\\([A-Za-z@]+)[^{}]*$").unwrap());
4232  // A require inside a `\DeclareOption{<opt>}{…}` (or `\DeclareOption*{…}`)
4233  // code-arm is CONDITIONAL on the option being selected (resolved later by
4234  // `\ProcessOptions`), so the dep-scan must not eagerly load it. Matches when a
4235  // group's preceding window ends at `…\DeclareOption{<opt>}` / `…\DeclareOption*`
4236  // — i.e. the `{` it precedes opens the option's code arm. (Does NOT match
4237  // `\DeclareOptionX`/`\ExecuteOptions`/`\ProcessOptions`.)
4238  static DECLARE_OPTION_RE: Lazy<Regex> =
4239    Lazy::new(|| Regex::new(r"(?s)\\DeclareOption\*?\s*(?:\{[^{}]*\})?\s*$").unwrap());
4240
4241  // Perl L2761: `FindFile($file, type => $type, noltxml => 1)`. `$file`
4242  // is BARE — `FindFile` glues on `.$type` itself per L2073-2076.
4243  let raw_path = find_file(
4244    file,
4245    Some(FindFileOptions {
4246      ext_type: Some(Cow::Owned(ext_type.to_string())),
4247      forbid_ltxml: true, // Perl `noltxml => 1`
4248      ..FindFileOptions::default()
4249    }),
4250  );
4251  let Some(path) = raw_path else { return false };
4252
4253  // Perl L2762-2766: slurp file. Check filecontents-cache first for the
4254  // inline-cls/sty case (e.g. `\begin{filecontents}{alggeom.cls}`), then
4255  // fall through to disk. Without the cache check, papers that bundle
4256  // their .cls inline via filecontents miss the dep-scan and downstream
4257  // CSes that the (now-cached) cls would have hand-loaded stay
4258  // undefined. Witness: arXiv:2604.09738.
4259  let code = if let Some(cached) = crate::binding::virtual_files::vfs_read(&path) {
4260    cached
4261  } else {
4262    // Use read (bytes) + UTF-8 conversion so non-UTF-8 cls/sty files
4263    // (ISO-8859 with vendor copyright headers, e.g. cpc-hepnp.cls with
4264    // Chinese comments) still get scanned. read_to_string strict UTF-8
4265    // validation would error out, leaving \RequirePackage{fancyhdr} and
4266    // friends silently undiscovered. Witness 2203.16500.
4267    //
4268    // Fast path for valid UTF-8 (the overwhelming majority): `str::from_utf8`
4269    // SIMD-validates the whole slice in one pass, where `from_utf8_lossy`
4270    // walks it through the grapheme-aware `Utf8Chunks` iterator hunting for
4271    // invalid bytes — measurable on a 197 KB class file like ieeeconf.cls.
4272    // Byte-identical to the lossy path for valid UTF-8; fall back to lossy
4273    // only on invalid bytes. Same pattern as the per-line reader in `mouth.rs`.
4274    match std::fs::read(&path) {
4275      Ok(bytes) => match str::from_utf8(&bytes) {
4276        Ok(s) => s.to_string(),
4277        Err(_) => String::from_utf8_lossy(&bytes).into_owned(),
4278      },
4279      Err(_) => {
4280        Warn!(
4281          "I/O",
4282          "read",
4283          s!("Couldn't open {} to scan dependencies, $!", path)
4284        );
4285        return false;
4286      },
4287    }
4288  };
4289
4290  // BEYOND PERL (DIVERGENCES #333): a class written with CR-only line ends
4291  // (2402.17342's sn-jnl.cls) is one line to the comment pattern below, which
4292  // needs `\n` — Perl's identical regex then strips no comment and loads every
4293  // commented-out `\usepackage`. TeX ends a line at either (web2c), so read it
4294  // as `\n`-ended first.
4295  let code = if code.contains('\r') {
4296    code.replace("\r\n", "\n").replace('\r', "\n")
4297  } else {
4298    code
4299  };
4300  // Perl L2776: strip comments (replacement empty).
4301  let code = COMMENT_RE.replace_all(&code, "");
4302  // Strip `\begin{comment}…\end{comment}` blocks (see COMMENT_ENV_RE above).
4303  let code = COMMENT_ENV_RE.replace_all(&code, "");
4304
4305  // DIVERGENCE FROM PERL (deliberate, more-robust): do NOT dep-load a
4306  // `\usepackage` / `\RequirePackage` that sits in a DEFERRED macro-definition
4307  // body (a `\newcommand` / `\def`-family body) — it loads only if that macro
4308  // is later expanded, which the dep-scan must not eagerly force. Requires
4309  // inside LOAD-TIME conditionals (`\IfFileExists{X.sty}{\usepackage{X}}`,
4310  // `\@ifundefined{..}{..}`, `\if…\usepackage…\fi`) DO execute during raw-load
4311  // and MUST still be picked up. (Perl never force-loads the deferred ones: for
4312  // a normally raw-loaded file it doesn't dep-scan at all, and the bundled deps
4313  // it wants in the binding-bypass case run at load.) Witnesses:
4314  //   - 1506.06200 — categorytheory.sty `\newcommand{\usediagrams}{\usepackage {diagrams}}` (never
4315  //     invoked) must be SKIPPED (else the `diagrams` stub's `locked` `\begin{diagram}` shadows the
4316  //     paper's tikz `{diagram}`).
4317  //   - 1703.03673 — iau.cls `\IfFileExists{amssymb.sty}{…\usepackage{amssymb}…}` must be KEPT
4318  //     (else `\bigstar` is undefined). An earlier brace-DEPTH filter wrongly skipped this
4319  //     conditional; the def-body check is precise.
4320  // A `\usepackage` is deferred iff ANY enclosing `{…}` group is opened directly
4321  // by a `\newcommand`/`\def` definition header.
4322  // Is `\<name>` INVOKED (not merely defined) somewhere in the file? A bare
4323  // control-sequence occurrence not directly preceded by a `\…def`/`\let`
4324  // introducer counts as an invocation. Used to tell an executed deferred-load
4325  // idiom (`\def\@tempa{…\RequirePackage{P}…}\@tempa`) from a never-called one.
4326  let is_invoked = |name: &str| -> bool {
4327    let pat = s!("\\{name}");
4328    let mut from = 0usize;
4329    while let Some(rel) = code[from..].find(&pat) {
4330      let at = from + rel;
4331      from = at + pat.len();
4332      // Full control sequence: next char must not extend the name.
4333      let after_ok = code[at + pat.len()..]
4334        .chars()
4335        .next()
4336        .is_none_or(|c| !c.is_ascii_alphabetic() && c != '@');
4337      if !after_ok {
4338        continue;
4339      }
4340      // Not a (re)definition: the chars just before `\name` aren't `…def`/`…let`.
4341      // Back up to a char boundary: a multi-byte char within 8 bytes of
4342      // `\name` would split a byte slice.
4343      let mut start = at.saturating_sub(8);
4344      while !code.is_char_boundary(start) {
4345        start -= 1;
4346      }
4347      let before = code[start..at].trim_end();
4348      if before.ends_with("def") || before.ends_with("let") {
4349        continue;
4350      }
4351      return true;
4352    }
4353    false
4354  };
4355  let in_macro_def_body = |start: usize| -> bool {
4356    let bytes = code.as_bytes();
4357    let mut stack: Vec<usize> = Vec::new();
4358    let mut i = 0usize;
4359    while i < start {
4360      match bytes[i] {
4361        b'\\' => {
4362          i += 2;
4363          continue;
4364        },
4365        b'{' => stack.push(i),
4366        b'}' => {
4367          stack.pop();
4368        },
4369        _ => {},
4370      }
4371      i += 1;
4372    }
4373    stack.iter().any(|&ob| {
4374      let lo = ob.saturating_sub(400);
4375      let window = code.get(lo..ob).unwrap_or(&code[..ob]);
4376      // A require inside a `\DeclareOption` code-arm is conditional (only the
4377      // option-selected arm runs, via `\ProcessOptions`) → defer it, even when
4378      // the arm `\def`s an INVOKED macro: aa/myaa.cls have
4379      //   \DeclareOption{ascii}{\def\aa@inputenc{\RequirePackage[ascii]{inputenc}}}
4380      //   …\DeclareOption{utf8}{…[utf8]…}  …  \aa@inputenc
4381      // where the single `\aa@inputenc` call would otherwise un-defer the FIRST
4382      // (ascii) arm via the `is_invoked` rule below and wrongly force
4383      // inputenc[ascii] (→ UTF-8 `ç` rejected). Perl defers all such (loads no
4384      // inputenc; the actual `utf8` default is the right answer and `ç` passes
4385      // through either way). Witness 1504.05963 (Perl 0 / Rust 1, now parity).
4386      if DECLARE_OPTION_RE.is_match(window) {
4387        return true;
4388      }
4389      // A `\def\<m>{…}` body only defers if `\<m>` is never invoked; an invoked
4390      // scratch macro (`\@tempa`) runs its body at load, so its require loads.
4391      if let Some(caps) = DEF_NAME_RE.captures(window) {
4392        return !is_invoked(&caps[1]);
4393      }
4394      // `\newcommand`/`\DeclareRobustCommand` user-command bodies stay deferred
4395      // (witness 1506.06200 `\newcommand{\usediagrams}{\usepackage{diagrams}}`).
4396      DEF_BODY_HEADER_RE.is_match(window)
4397    })
4398  };
4399  let top_level = |cap: &regex::Captures| -> bool {
4400    cap
4401      .get(0)
4402      .map(|m| !in_macro_def_body(m.start()))
4403      .unwrap_or(true)
4404  };
4405
4406  // NOTE — a former Rust-only "conflicting option sets" heuristic was REMOVED
4407  // here (was: drop a package `\RequirePackage`'d / `\usepackage`'d with two or
4408  // more DIFFERENT option sets, as the signature of a deferred require inside a
4409  // `\def`/`\DeclareOption` body, e.g. aa.cls's
4410  // `\DeclareOption{ascii}{\def\aa@inputenc{\RequirePackage[ascii]{inputenc}}}`
4411  // …`{utf8}{…[utf8]…}`). It over-fired on a package required in BOTH arms of a
4412  // load-time `\if…\else…\fi` with different options — mutually exclusive, so
4413  // exactly one branch loads and the package MUST be kept. Witness: rist.cls's
4414  // `\ifpdf \RequirePackage[pdftex,…]{hyperref} \else \RequirePackage[dvipdfm,…]
4415  // {hyperref} \fi` (1912.00781: hyperref dropped → `\url` undefined). The
4416  // heuristic was also provably REDUNDANT: it only ever saw TOP-LEVEL requires
4417  // (the captures feeding it were `top_level`-filtered), but aa.cls's inputenc
4418  // lives in a `\def`/`\DeclareOption` BODY — already dropped by the def-body
4419  // `top_level` skip below, so it never entered the conflicting set at all. Perl
4420  // has no such gate (L2767-2774 is a plain dedup); the def-body `top_level` skip
4421  // plus the executed-set gate cover the legitimate cases faithfully.
4422  static OPT_SPLIT: Lazy<Regex> = Lazy::new(|| Regex::new(r"\s*,\s*").unwrap());
4423  // Perl Package.pm:2806/2810 `options => [split(/\s*,\s*/, $options)]`. An option naming a
4424  // macro undefined at the scan (easychair.cls:365 `\LoadClass[\@PaperFormat,…]{article}`)
4425  // names the scanned file's own definitions, which never run (the file is not executed,
4426  // OmniBus stands in): Perl keeps it an inert string, where the loaded class's option
4427  // processing here expands it, "undefined" for each. An option whose macros are defined
4428  // (`headheight=\baselineskip`) passes. Witnesses 2011.11995, 2211.09353, 2607.12736.
4429  static OPTION_CS: Lazy<Regex> = Lazy::new(|| Regex::new(r"\\([A-Za-z@]+|.)").unwrap());
4430  let scanned_options = |raw_opts: &Option<String>| -> Vec<String> {
4431    raw_opts
4432      .as_deref()
4433      .map(|s| {
4434        OPT_SPLIT
4435          .split(s)
4436          .filter(|option| {
4437            OPTION_CS
4438              .captures_iter(option)
4439              .all(|cs| has_meaning(&crate::T_CS!(format!("\\{}", &cs[1]))))
4440          })
4441          .map(|x| x.to_string())
4442          .collect()
4443      })
4444      .unwrap_or_default()
4445  };
4446
4447  // Perl L2767-2774: shared `%dups` map, $collect closure splits on
4448  // `\s*,\s*` and only enrolls a package once, AND only if its
4449  // `.sty.ltxml_loaded` flag is unset.
4450  let mut packages: Vec<(String, Option<String>)> = Vec::new();
4451  let mut dups: rustc_hash::FxHashSet<String> = rustc_hash::FxHashSet::default();
4452  let mut collect = |pkg_csv: &str, raw_options: Option<&str>| {
4453    for p in OPT_SPLIT.split(pkg_csv) {
4454      if p.is_empty() || !keep(p) {
4455        continue;
4456      }
4457      // Executed-set gate (see top of fn): when this file raw-loaded, drop a
4458      // candidate whose `\usepackage` never executed anywhere — it was inside a
4459      // false conditional the raw-load skipped.
4460      if raw_loaded && !lookup_bool(&s!("{p}.usepackage_executed")) {
4461        continue;
4462      }
4463      // Perl L2773: `!$dups{$p} && !LookupValue($p . '.sty.ltxml_loaded')`
4464      if !dups.contains(p) && !lookup_bool(&s!("{p}.sty.ltxml_loaded")) {
4465        packages.push((p.to_string(), raw_options.map(|s| s.to_string())));
4466        dups.insert(p.to_string());
4467      }
4468    }
4469  };
4470
4471  // Perl L2777: `\RequirePackage` first.
4472  for cap in REQ_RE.captures_iter(&code) {
4473    if !top_level(&cap) {
4474      continue;
4475    }
4476    collect(&cap[2], cap.get(1).map(|m| m.as_str()));
4477  }
4478  // Perl L2778-2779: `\usepackage` second.
4479  for cap in USE_RE.captures_iter(&code) {
4480    if !top_level(&cap) {
4481      continue;
4482    }
4483    collect(&cap[2], cap.get(1).map(|m| m.as_str()));
4484  }
4485
4486  // Perl L2767/L2781-2782: `@classes` is class-only, NO dup-check.
4487  let mut classes: Vec<(String, Option<String>)> = Vec::new();
4488  if ext_type == "cls" {
4489    for cap in CLS_RE.captures_iter(&code) {
4490      if !top_level(&cap) {
4491        continue;
4492      }
4493      let class = cap[2].to_string();
4494      if !class.is_empty() && keep(&class) {
4495        classes.push((class, cap.get(1).map(|m| m.as_str().to_string())));
4496      }
4497    }
4498  }
4499
4500  // BEYOND PERL: harvest `\addbibresource` too.
4501  //
4502  // A journal class shipped with the paper routinely declares the bibliography
4503  // itself — `journaleducation.cls` has `\addbibresource{letters/refs.bib}`,
4504  // `cai26.cls` has `references.bib`, `tau.cls` has `tau.bib` — and the
4505  // document then only writes `\printbibliography`. We do not interpret that
4506  // class (no binding, OmniBus fallback), so the resource was never registered
4507  // and `\printbibliography` had nothing to print: no `MakeBibliography` line
4508  // at all and an empty References section, even though the `.bib` ships with
4509  // the paper. This scanner is already reading the file for its dependencies,
4510  // so it is the one place that can see the declaration.
4511  //
4512  // Registering the name is enough — `\biblatex@printbibliography` pops the
4513  // list and routes it through `\lx@bibliography`, which resolves the file (and
4514  // deduplicates, so a document that ALSO declares the same resource does not
4515  // get a doubled bibliography). Papers that never load biblatex simply leave
4516  // the value unread. Audit family F4(c); witnesses 2605.23724, 2605.00270,
4517  // 2605.02720.
4518  for res in scan_bib_resources(&code) {
4519    let _ = push_value("biblatex_resources", Stored::String(arena::pin(res)));
4520  }
4521
4522  // Perl L2784-2785: Info iff EITHER list is non-empty; message lists
4523  // class names then package names, separated by ',' (no space).
4524  if !classes.is_empty() || !packages.is_empty() {
4525    let names: Vec<&str> = classes
4526      .iter()
4527      .map(|(n, _)| n.as_str())
4528      .chain(packages.iter().map(|(n, _)| n.as_str()))
4529      .collect();
4530    Info!(
4531      "dependencies",
4532      "dependencies",
4533      s!("Loading dependencies for {}: {}", path, names.join(","))
4534    );
4535  }
4536
4537  // Perl L2786-2789: foreach class — gate by `FindFile($class, type=>'cls',
4538  // notex=>1)`, then `LoadClass(..., options=>[split ...])`.
4539  for (class, raw_opts) in classes {
4540    if marked_loaded_unrecorded(&class, "cls") {
4541      continue;
4542    }
4543    if find_file(
4544      &class,
4545      Some(FindFileOptions {
4546        ext_type: Some(Cow::Borrowed("cls")),
4547        notex: true, // Perl `notex => 1`
4548        ..FindFileOptions::default()
4549      }),
4550    )
4551    .is_some()
4552    {
4553      let _ = load_class(&class, scanned_options(&raw_opts), Tokens::default());
4554    }
4555  }
4556
4557  // Perl L2790-2793: foreach package — gate by `FindFile($pkg, type=>'sty',
4558  // notex=>1)`, then `RequirePackage(..., options=>[split ...])`.
4559  for (pkg, raw_opts) in packages {
4560    if marked_loaded_unrecorded(&pkg, "sty") {
4561      continue;
4562    }
4563    if find_file(
4564      &pkg,
4565      Some(FindFileOptions {
4566        ext_type: Some(Cow::Borrowed("sty")),
4567        notex: true, // Perl `notex => 1`
4568        ..FindFileOptions::default()
4569      }),
4570    )
4571    .is_some()
4572    {
4573      let _ = require_package(&pkg, RequireOptions {
4574        options: scanned_options(&raw_opts),
4575        ..RequireOptions::default()
4576      });
4577    }
4578  }
4579  true
4580}
4581
4582/// Is a dependency-scan candidate marked loaded while our records say it is not? The scan stands in for the scanned
4583/// file's own `\usepackage`/`\RequirePackage`/`\LoadClass` lines, and LaTeX skips such a line when `\ver@<name>.<ext>`
4584/// is defined and not `\relax` (latex.ltx:18750 `\@onefilewithoptions` → `\@ifl@aded`, latex.ltx:18397-18402): a
4585/// document that sets the marker before the class runs (`\AddToHook{class/sn-jnl/before}{…\gdef\csname
4586/// ver@program.sty\endcsname{9999/12/31}}`, sn-jnl-setup.sty of 2201.11056 — program.sty's active `|` breaks pgf)
4587/// suppresses the class's load. The marker can also come from a class or package read raw (memoir's
4588/// `\EmulatedPackage`, imakeidx.sty:61 `ver@makeidx.sty`, xcolor.sty:315 `ver@color.sty`) or from a binding
4589/// (biblatex's); LaTeX skips the line for each of them alike. Our own records (`_loaded`/`_raw_loaded`) say it is not
4590/// loaded, so without this the scan loads it (Perl Package.pm:2328-2330 consults only those records; KNOWN_PERL_ERRORS
4591/// #780, DIVERGENCES #605). Scan-only, by ruling (2026-10-16): `\usepackage`/`\RequirePackage` themselves keep the
4592/// records-only test (ruling 2026-10-14, no general loader `\ver@` rule). Guard:
4593/// `perfect_kernel_batch68f::scan_skips_a_package_the_document_marked_loaded`.
4594fn marked_loaded_unrecorded(name: &str, ext: &str) -> bool {
4595  let file = s!("{name}.{ext}");
4596  if lookup_bool(&s!("{file}_loaded")) || lookup_bool(&s!("{file}_raw_loaded")) {
4597    return false;
4598  }
4599  if !crate::binding::def::dialect::is_defined_token(&crate::T_CS!(s!("\\ver@{file}"))) {
4600    return false;
4601  }
4602  Info!(
4603    "dependencies",
4604    file.as_str(),
4605    s!("Skipping dependency {file}: it is marked loaded (\\ver@{file} is defined)")
4606  );
4607  true
4608}
4609
4610/// Attach a resource (CSS, JavaScript, …) to the document being built.
4611///
4612/// Port of Perl `Package.pm:RequireResource` L3139-3158. A resource needs
4613/// either a pathname or inline content, and a mime-type: an absent type is
4614/// inferred from the pathname's extension (case-sensitively, as Perl's
4615/// `$resource_types{$ext}` lookup is), and a resource still lacking one after
4616/// that is skipped with a warning rather than emitted untyped.
4617///
4618/// The resource is queued as *pending* and folded into the document later,
