4619/// covering Perl's `$LaTeXML::DOCUMENT ? addResource : PushValue(PENDING_RESOURCES)`
4620/// split — bindings routinely call this from a preamble, before any document
4621/// exists.
4622pub fn require_resource(mut resource: Resource) {
4623  if resource.name.is_empty() && resource.content.is_empty() {
4624    Warn!(
4625      "expected",
4626      "resource",
4627      "Resource must have a resource pathname or content; skipping"
4628    );
4629    return;
4630  }
4631  if resource.mimetype.is_empty() && !resource.name.is_empty() {
4632    // Perl Package.pm L3129: `my $ext = pathname_type($resource);` — no
4633    // case-folding; `$resource_types{$ext}` is a case-sensitive lookup.
4634    let ext = pathname::extension(&resource.name);
4635    resource.mimetype = resource_type(&ext);
4636  }
4637  if resource.mimetype.is_empty() {
4638    Warn!(
4639      "expected",
4640      "mime-type",
4641      "Resource must have a mime-type; skipping"
4642    );
4643    return;
4644  }
4645
4646  // If we've got a document, go ahead & put the resource in.
4647  // if (document.is_some()) {
4648  //   document.as_mut().unwrap().add_resource(resource, resource);
4649  // } else {
4650  push_pending_resource(resource);
4651  // }
4652}
4653
4654/// Does the class `name` resolve to a file of the TeX distribution, neither beside the document (its own directory or
4655/// below it, a `filecontents` one) nor beside `binding`'s own class?
4656fn is_unrelated_distribution_class(name: &str, binding: &str) -> bool {
4657  let Some(path) = find_file(
4658    name,
4659    Some(FindFileOptions {
4660      ext_type: Some(Cow::Borrowed("cls")),
4661      forbid_ltxml: true,
4662      ..FindFileOptions::default()
4663    }),
4664  ) else {
4665    return false;
4666  };
4667  if pathname::is_literaldata(&path) || super::virtual_files::vfs_exists(&path) {
4668    return false;
4669  }
4670  let Ok(path) = std::fs::canonicalize(&path) else {
4671    return false;
4672  };
4673  if path.with_file_name(format!("{binding}.cls")).exists() {
4674    return false;
4675  }
4676  let directory = lookup_string("SOURCEDIRECTORY");
4677  directory.is_empty()
4678    || std::fs::canonicalize(&directory).is_ok_and(|directory| !path.starts_with(directory))
4679}

4681/// Perl: `LoadClass($name, withoptions => 1)` — load a class passing the
4682/// caller's class options through to the child. Reads `class_options` from
4683/// state (populated by the outer `\documentclass` invocation) and forwards
4684/// those as the child's options list, matching Perl Package.pm LoadClass's
4685/// `withoptions` branch.
4686/// `\LoadClassWithOptions` — the calling class's options go to the loaded
4687/// class (latex.ltx:18637 `\@loadwithoptions`, [`calling_file_options`]);
4688/// with no file on the load stack (a class binding delegating directly, e.g.
4689/// amsart → ams_core) the document class's recorded options stand in. The
4690/// `\LoadClassWithOptions` constructor once passed an EMPTY list, so a raw
4691/// class delegating its options (oblivoir.cls → oblivoir-utf.cls:77
4692/// `\if@AMSmath\RequirePackage{amsmath}`) never saw `[amsmath]` (istgame-doc).
4693pub fn load_class_with_options(name: &str, after: Tokens) -> Result<()> {
4694  let mut options = calling_file_options()?;
4695  if options.is_empty() {
4696    let class_opts = lookup_vecdeque("class_options").unwrap_or_default();
4697    options = class_opts
4698      .iter()
4699      .filter_map(|item| match item {
4700        Stored::String(s) => Some(arena::to_string(*s)),
4701        _ => None,
4702      })
4703      .collect();
4704  }
4705  load_class(name, options, after)
4706}
4707/// The format the class file `name` (the one a raw read would take: [`find_file`], no binding) declares, when it is
4708/// not LaTeX2e: its first `\NeedsTeXFormat{…}` outside a comment names another (`pLaTeX2e`: jarticle.cls:16 and the
4709/// other 18 pLaTeX/upLaTeX classes of TeX Live 2025). Read as text, the class is not run: each line up to its first
4710/// `%` not escaped by a backslash, the braced argument of the first `\NeedsTeXFormat` given one, its spaces trimmed. LaTeX
4711/// compares it with `\fmtname` and, when they differ, raises an error and stops reading the file
4712/// (latex.ltx:18663-18674 `\NeedsTeXFormat`: "The current input file will not be processed further, because it was
4713/// written for some other flavor of TeX"). A file without the declaration, or without a readable one, is LaTeX's (`None`).
4714fn declared_other_format(name: &str) -> Option<String> {
4715  let path = find_file(
4716    name,
4717    Some(FindFileOptions {
4718      ext_type: Some(Cow::Borrowed("cls")),
4719      forbid_ltxml: true,
4720      ..FindFileOptions::default()
4721    }),
4722  )?;
4723  let text = match crate::binding::virtual_files::vfs_read(&path) {
4724    Some(cached) => cached,
4725    None => match std::fs::read(&path) {
4726      Ok(bytes) => String::from_utf8_lossy(&bytes).into_owned(),
4727      Err(_) => return None,
4728    },
4729  };
4730  for line in text.split(['\n', '\r']) {
4731    let mut code_end = line.len();
4732    let mut escaped = false;
4733    for (at, c) in line.char_indices() {
4734      match c {
4735        '\\' => escaped = !escaped,
4736        '%' if !escaped => {
4737          code_end = at;
4738          break;
4739        },
4740        _ => escaped = false,
4741      }
4742    }
4743    // (a use without an argument is not the declaration: `\ifx\NeedsTeXFormat\undefined`, a LaTeX 2.09 test)
4744    for (at, _) in line[..code_end].match_indices("\\NeedsTeXFormat") {
4745      let rest = line[at + "\\NeedsTeXFormat".len()..code_end].trim_start();
4746      if let Some((arg, _)) = rest.strip_prefix('{').and_then(|r| r.split_once('}')) {
4747        let format = arg.trim();
4748        return (format != "LaTeX2e").then(|| format.to_string());
4749      }
4750    }
4751  }
4752  None
4753}
4754
4755/// The binding a class without one of its own is read with: Perl's step (Package.pm:2711-2716), the longest binding
4756/// name the class starts with (`mysvjour3` → `svjour3`, `mn2ebis` → `mn2e`, `IEEEtranTCOM` → `IEEEtran`), then a
4757/// Rust-only step: one equal to it but for case (2406.08163); `None` → OmniBus. A path-prefixed class whose basename
4758/// names a binding (`misc/ieeetran` → `IEEEtran`, 2105.02087) never gets here: [`class_binding_for_path`] loads that
4759/// binding first.
4760fn class_binding_alternate(name: &str) -> Option<&'static str> {
4761  let mut sorted: Vec<&'static str> = get_class_binding_names()
4762    .into_iter()
4763    .filter(|n| *n != "OmniBus" && *n != name)
4764    .collect();
4765  sorted.sort_by_key(|n| std::cmp::Reverse(n.len()));
4766  // Strict-case prefix first (Perl-faithful: `$class =~ /^\Q$_\E/`), then
4767  // a case-insensitive fallback for binding entries that differ from the
4768  // class name ONLY in capitalization (e.g. `WileyNJDv5` class vs a
4769  // `wileyNJDv5` binding entry, witness 2406.08163). The ci fallback uses
4770  // FULL equality, NOT prefix: a ci-PREFIX match wrongly fired
4771  // `AAAI-Std` → `aa` (the 2-char A&A astronomy binding) because
4772  // `"aaai-std".starts_with("aa")` — but Perl's case-SENSITIVE `/^aa/`
4773  // against `AAAI-Std` finds nothing, so Perl falls back to OmniBus
4774  // (which defines `\address`). Full ci-equality keeps the
4775  // capitalization-only Wiley case while matching Perl's no-match →
4776  // OmniBus for AAAI-Std. Witness 2008.08548.
4777  sorted
4778    .iter()
4779    .copied()
4780    .find(|candidate| name.starts_with(candidate))
4781    .or_else(|| {
4782      sorted
4783        .iter()
4784        .copied()
4785        .find(|candidate| name.eq_ignore_ascii_case(candidate))
4786    })
4787}
4788
4789/// The class binding a path-prefixed class names by its basename (`lib-acm/acmart` → `acmart`, `misc/ieeetran` →
4790/// `IEEEtran`, `JINST-Sample-files/JINST` → `JINST`): exact case first, then a case-insensitive full match, as
4791/// `pathname_find` matches (Util/Pathname.pm:383-389). Perl finds that binding directly, as the request's own file:
4792/// `FindFile_aux` looks `<class>.cls.ltxml` up in the installation's `Package` directory, which `candidate_pathnames`
4793/// adds WITHOUT the request's directory (Package.pm:2125-2128, Util/Pathname.pm:340-341) — so it loads it silently, with
4794/// no missing status, no warning and no dependency scan of the shipped class (`LoadClass`'s alternate branch,
4795/// Package.pm:2715-2733, is never reached). A full match only, never a prefix (`AAAI-Std` is not `aa`). Witnesses
4796/// 2605.00109, 2605.25901, 2606.20651 (`lib-acm/acmart`, 43 such tasks in 2605+2606), 1504.01965 (JINST),
4797/// 2105.02087 (misc/ieeetran).
4798fn class_binding_for_path(name: &str) -> Option<&'static str> {
4799  let basename = name.rsplit(['/', '\\']).next().unwrap_or(name);
4800  if basename == name || basename.is_empty() {
4801    return None;
4802  }
4803  let names = get_class_binding_names();
4804  names
4805    .iter()
4806    .copied()
4807    .find(|candidate| *candidate == basename)
4808    .or_else(|| {
4809      names
4810        .iter()
4811        .copied()
4812        .find(|candidate| basename.eq_ignore_ascii_case(candidate))
4813    })
4814}
4815
4816/// Is a class loading now ([`load_class`] entered and not yet returned)? A class's own tests are answered by what it
4817/// has defined so far (`state::class_defines_chapter`).
4818pub fn class_loading() -> bool { CLASS_LOAD_DEPTH.with(|d| d.get()) > 0 }
4819
4820/// Load a document class — the `\documentclass` / `\LoadClass` implementation,
4821/// port of Perl `Package.pm:LoadClass` L2702-2730.
4822///
4823/// Resolution runs [`input_definitions`] for `<name>.cls`, then Perl's
4824/// three-step fallback when that yields no binding: the longest known
4825/// `.cls.ltxml` binding whose name PREFIXES the requested class (so an
4826/// author-renamed `IEEEtranTCOM.cls` still gets the `IEEEtran` binding), and
4827/// failing that `OmniBus`, the generic class supplying frontmatter, counter and
4828/// theorem definitions. Whether the raw `.cls` may be read at all is the
4829/// `INCLUDE_CLASSES` value's call (`searchpaths` restricts it to local
4830/// sources); by default it may not, because a raw load that "succeeds" would
4831/// suppress the OmniBus fallback while providing none of its bindings.
4832///
4833/// See [`load_class_with_options`] for Perl's `withoptions => 1` variant. The
4834/// per-branch reasoning, and the arXiv witnesses behind it, are in the body
4835/// comments.
4836pub fn load_class(name: &str, options: Vec<String>, after: Tokens) -> Result<()> {
4837  // (a stand-in class's copy has one class slot, which its binding's base class fills: OXIDIZED_DESIGN_DIVERGENCES
4838  // #736)
4839  if binding_base_class_stands_for(name)? {
4840    return Ok(());
4841  }
4842  // A class loaded while another class loads — `\LoadClass`, a binding's
4843  // base class, the dependency scan's harvest — is nested: its options are
4844  // its own, never global options (`InputDefinitionOptions::nested_class`,
4845  // KPE #304). Only the outermost load is the document class.
4846  let nested_class = CLASS_LOAD_DEPTH.with(|d| d.get()) > 0;
4847  CLASS_LOAD_DEPTH.with(|d| d.set(d.get() + 1));
4848  struct ClassLoadDepthGuard;
4849  impl Drop for ClassLoadDepthGuard {
4850    fn drop(&mut self) { CLASS_LOAD_DEPTH.with(|d| d.set(d.get() - 1)); }
4851  }
4852  let _guard = ClassLoadDepthGuard;
4853
4854  // Perl Package.pm LoadClass: $options{notex}=1 unless LookupValue('INCLUDE_CLASSES').
4855  // Defaults to NOT loading raw .cls. Only .cls.ltxml bindings are considered;
4856  // if the binding is missing, fall through to OmniBus (below). Allowing raw
4857  // .cls to "succeed" the load prevents the OmniBus fallback that provides
4858  // generic frontmatter / counter / theorem bindings.
4859  //
4860  // PERL-FAITHFUL (2026-05-30): a directory prefix does NOT force a raw .cls
4861  // load. Perl resolves a path-prefixed class to its basename BINDING when one
4862  // exists, else OmniBus — it loads `IEEEtran.cls.ltxml` for
4863  // `\documentclass{misc/ieeetran}` and falls to OmniBus for
4864  // `\documentclass{JINST-Sample-files/JINST}`. The basename→binding match is
4865  // handled in the `alternate` search below (it strips the path). Forcing a raw
4866  // load for any path-prefixed name (the old `&& !has_path_prefix` exception)
4867  // broke bundled classes whose raw load is semantically incomplete — JINST's
4868  // begin-document `\author`/`\abstract` checks fire and `\abstract@cs` is left
4869  // undefined, where Perl (OmniBus) is clean. Witness 1504.01965; the
4870  // misc/ieeetran case (2105.02087) now matches Perl via the basename binding.
4871  // That holds under `notex` (INCLUDE_CLASSES off). The arXiv profile's raw classes (`localrawclasses` since 62k,
4872  // `rawclasses` since 67v; DIVERGENCES #444) interpret a class with no binding route raw, path-prefixed or not. JINST's begin-document checks,
4873  // which then fired (1504.01965, 1011.5969, 1310.6454), are met by its binding since 64k (OXIDIZED_DESIGN #482).
4874  // A path-prefixed class whose basename names a binding is that binding, loaded as the request's own file
4875  // ([`class_binding_for_path`]; Perl Package.pm:2125-2128): ahead of any raw read of the shipped copy, as Perl's
4876  // `FindFile_aux` prefers the `.ltxml`. It is loaded under its own name, as LaTeX names a path-prefixed class by its
4877  // basename (`\@currname` = `acmart`; latex.ltx:18740-18748 `\@onefilewithoptions`, whose `\@currpath` =
4878  // `lib-acm/` is not set here), so `\@ifclassloaded{acmart}` holds (OXIDIZED_DESIGN #444); `fallback_request` keeps the path for a family binding that reads the shipped
4879  // copy (`shipped_class::requested_shipped_class`: JINST, 1504.01965).
4880  if let Some(binding) = class_binding_for_path(name) {
4881    let outer_request = lookup_string("fallback_request");
4882    let outer_target = lookup_string("fallback_target");
4883    assign_value("fallback_request", name.to_string(), Some(Scope::Global));
4884    assign_value("fallback_target", binding.to_string(), Some(Scope::Global));
4885    let loaded = input_definitions(binding, InputDefinitionOptions {
4886      extension: Some(Cow::Borrowed("cls")),
4887      options,
4888      after,
4889      notex: true,
4890      handleoptions: true,
4891      noerror: true,
4892      nested_class,
4893      ..InputDefinitionOptions::default()
4894    });
4895    assign_value("fallback_request", outer_request, Some(Scope::Global));
4896    assign_value("fallback_target", outer_target, Some(Scope::Global));
4897    return loaded;
4898  }
4899  let notex_default = !lookup_bool("INCLUDE_CLASSES");
4900  // Perl Package.pm:2705-2707: LoadClass can be limited to local SEARCHPATHS when
4901  // `localrawclasses` option sets `INCLUDE_CLASSES => 'searchpaths'`.
4902  let searchpaths_only = !notex_default && lookup_string("INCLUDE_CLASSES") == "searchpaths";
4903  // With raw classes on — `rawclasses` (the arXiv profile since 67v) or `localrawclasses` (62k) — a raw class stands
4904  // in for OmniBus only: every binding route comes first — the basename binding of a path-prefixed class above, the
4905  // exact and versioned ones inside `input_definitions`, the prefix/case alternate here — so a shipped
4906  // `IEEEtranTCOM.cls`, `IEEEtran4PSCC.cls` or `misc/ieeetran.cls` keeps the IEEEtran binding (2105.02087) instead of
4907  // loading raw (OXIDIZED_DESIGN_DIVERGENCES #444).
4908  // Nor is a class written for another format read raw (`\NeedsTeXFormat{pLaTeX2e}`, jarticle.cls:16; TeX Live's 19
4909  // pLaTeX and upLaTeX classes, [`declared_other_format`]): LaTeX reads the declaration and processes the file no
4910  // further (latex.ltx:18663-18674), and a raw read of the rest met the other format's primitives (jarticle.cls:26-28
4911  // `\hour`, `\minute` registers; a Fatal, 1203.0762) where OmniBus kept the paper's text. The declaration itself is
4912  // read, so the format's input profile is the document's (K12: `\NeedsTeXFormat{pLaTeX2e}` letters kanji in
4913  // control-word names, sect05.rs), and the class falls to OmniBus.
4914  // A name's prefix marks an author's renamed copy beside the document, or a sibling shipped with the binding's class
4915  // (`jmlrbook` beside `jmlr.cls`, `acmart-tagged` beside `acmart.cls`), not any other published class of the TeX
4916  // distribution, which is a class of its own, read raw as LaTeX reads it: `bookest` ("Extension for the book class"),
4917  // `bookcover`, `beamerswitch`, `bookshelf`, `postcards`, `aalok`, `anima` took `book`, `beamer`, `pos`, `aa`, `an` and
4918  // lost their commands (sweep #150; docs/perfect_kernel/MANUAL_REGRESSIONS.md cluster A). Perl reads the file first,
4919  // too (Package.pm:2700-2716: the prefix scan is the no-file branch).
4920  let alternate =
4921    class_binding_alternate(name).filter(|binding| !is_unrelated_distribution_class(name, binding));
4922  let other_format = (!notex_default && alternate.is_none())
4923    .then(|| declared_other_format(name))
4924    .flatten();
4925  if let Some(format) = &other_format {
4926    digest(crate::mouth::tokenize_internal(TeXString::assembled(s!(
4927      "\\NeedsTeXFormat{{{format}}}"
4928    ))))?;
4929  }
4930  let raw_excluded = !notex_default && (alternate.is_some() || other_format.is_some());
4931  let notex_first = notex_default || raw_excluded;
4932
4933  let result = input_definitions(name, InputDefinitionOptions {
4934    extension: Some(Cow::Borrowed("cls")),
4935    options: options.clone(),
4936    after: after.clone(),
4937    notex: notex_first,
4938    searchpaths_only: searchpaths_only && !raw_excluded,
4939    handleoptions: true,
4940    noerror: true,
4941    nested_class,
4942    ..InputDefinitionOptions::default()
4943  });
4944  // A Fatal raised while the class loaded (an unbalanced expansion, a bail)
4945  // is not a missing class: it aborts the conversion (Perl dies), and the top
4946  // level logs it once. Falling to OmniBus hid the Fatal line (jarticle.cls:94
4947  // `\ds@tate`; `unbalanced_expansion_is_fatal`).
4948  if result.is_err() && get_status(LogStatus::Fatal) > 0 {
4949    return result;
4950  }
4951  // Perl Package.pm L2700-2716: if no direct binding, try a prefix-match fallback.
4952  // Scan all known cls bindings (longest-first), pick the first whose name is a
4953  // prefix of the requested class. This catches author-renamed classes like
4954  //   mysvjour3.cls → ProvidesClass{svjour3} → binding: svjour3
4955  //   mn2ebis.cls   → starts with "mn2e"   → binding: mn2e
4956  //   IEEEtranTCOM.cls → starts with "IEEEtran" → binding: IEEEtran
4957  // Fall through to OmniBus only when nothing matches.
4958  let will_fallback = (result.is_err()
4959    || (!lookup_bool(&format!("{name}.cls_loaded"))
4960      && !lookup_bool(&format!("{name}.cls_raw_loaded"))))
4961    && name != "OmniBus"
4962    && name != "article"
4963    && !lookup_bool("OmniBus.cls_loaded")
4964    && !lookup_bool("OmniBus.cls_raw_loaded");
4965
4966  // Perl Package.pm L2679 (LoadClass branch): scan the raw .cls for
4967  // \usepackage/\RequirePackage/\LoadClass dependencies when no .cls.ltxml
4968  // binding was found. This matters for unknown classes that nonetheless
4969  // pull in well-known packages (e.g. ijms-preprint.cls loads amsmath);
4970  // without it, downstream code like `\eqref{foo_bar}` sees `\eqref` as
4971  // undefined and the `_` characters then reach the stomach as subscript
4972  // catcodes, triggering runaway error recovery (arxiv 1003.0934 OOM).
4973  // Skip deps-scan only when a real `.cls.ltxml` binding has been
4974  // loaded — that binding is responsible for its own
4975  // `\RequirePackage` calls. The `cls_loaded` flag is set even for
4976  // a successful raw .cls load (no binding), so we MUST check the
4977  // binding-specific `cls.ltxml_loaded` flag instead. Without
4978  // this, paper-bundled .cls files (e.g. myclass.cls bundling
4979  // caption + many others, witness 2202.11535) raw-load
4980  // successfully but their `\RequirePackage` calls do NOT trigger
4981  // our binding loaders, leaving \captionsetup / \href / \affil
4982  // undefined.
4983  //
4984  // PERL-FAITHFUL ORDER: when we will fall through to an alternate
4985  // class binding (OmniBus or a prefix-match), DEFER the deps-scan
4986  // until AFTER the alternate is loaded. Otherwise the deps-scan
4987  // pulls natbib (et al.) ahead of OmniBus, and OmniBus's later
4988  // `Let('\lx@OmniBus@saved@bibitem', '\bibitem')` +
4989  // `DefMacro('\bibitem', ...)` clobbers natbib's `\lx@nat@bibitem`
4990  // binding — infinite-loop chain on
4991  // `\bibitem[\protect\citeauthoryear{...}{...}{...}]{key}`.
4992  // Witness: 1001.1919, 1001.5004, 0809.4358 (statsoc.cls),
4993  // 0904.3132, 0912.1617 (ectj.cls), 0904.3938 (compositio.cls),
4994  // 0908.3882 (third-input timeout), 0911.1590 (\tag\textsc cascade).
4995  // Perl's load order in this branch: warn missing-binding → load
4996  // alternate (OmniBus) → deps-scan pulls natbib LAST → natbib's Let
4997  // overrides OmniBus correctly.
4998  // ALSO skip the deps-scan after a SUCCESSFUL raw .cls load: Perl only ever
4999  // scans in the missing-binding branch, after loading the ALTERNATE
5000  // (Package.pm L2731-2733; a successful InputDefinitions returns at L2713-14
5001  // with no scan). An executed raw `\RequirePackage` triggers our binding
5002  // loaders directly (verified: bundled myclass.cls → caption_sty.rs loads,
5003  // \captionsetup/\eqref clean), so the scan only ADDS the harvest of
5004  // `\LoadClass`/`\RequirePackage` lines from UNEXECUTED branches — the
5005  // beamerswitch witness: its article branch ran, but the scan saw the three
5006  // `\LoadClass{beamer}` in never-taken branches and loaded beamer_cls.rs
5007  // AFTER raw beamerarticle, clobbering the correct raw `\mode`/`\only`
5008  // (perfect-kernel; Perl converts the same doc with 0 errors).
5009  if !lookup_bool(&s!("{name}.cls.ltxml_loaded"))
5010    && !lookup_bool(&s!("{name}.cls_raw_loaded"))
5011    && !will_fallback
5012  {
5013    maybe_require_dependencies(name, "cls");
5014  }
5015  if will_fallback {
5016    note_status(LogStatus::Missing, Some(&format!("{name}.cls")));
5017
5018    // Perl: @classes = sort { -(length($a) <=> length($b)) } available_cls_names
5019    //       my ($alternate) = grep { $class =~ /^\Q$_\E/ } @classes;
5020    // Flatten across ALL registered binding crates (latexml_package +
5021    // latexml_contrib + any future extensions) so contrib classes like
5022    // `memoir`, `siamltex`, `scrbook` are eligible alternates too.
5023    let alternate = class_binding_alternate(name);
5024
5025    let target = alternate.unwrap_or("OmniBus");
5026    Warn!(
5027      "missing_file",
5028      name,
5029      format!("Can't find binding for class {name} (using {target})"),
5030      "Anticipate undefined macros or environments"
5031    );
5032    // The request the alternate answers, as for a versioned fallback (`fallback_request` in
5033    // `input_definitions`): a binding serving a family of classes reads which one was asked for
5034    // (informs_cls.rs: `informs3noheader` prints no blind-review notice, 2609.04127).
5035    let outer_request = lookup_string("fallback_request");
5036    let outer_target = lookup_string("fallback_target");
5037    assign_value("fallback_request", name.to_string(), Some(Scope::Global));
5038    assign_value("fallback_target", target.to_string(), Some(Scope::Global));
5039    let loaded = input_definitions(target, InputDefinitionOptions {
5040      extension: Some(Cow::Borrowed("cls")),
5041      options,
5042      after,
5043      notex: true,
5044      handleoptions: true,
5045      noerror: true,
5046      nested_class,
5047      ..InputDefinitionOptions::default()
5048    });
5049    assign_value("fallback_request", outer_request, Some(Scope::Global));
5050    assign_value("fallback_target", outer_target, Some(Scope::Global));
5051    // Perl Package.pm L2715: after loading the alternate class binding, scan
5052    // the raw class file for \usepackage/\RequirePackage/\LoadClass — the
5053    // alternate rarely covers all dependencies the renamed class adds.
5054    // Run for BOTH a real prefix-match alternate AND the pure-OmniBus
5055    // fallback (the deps were deferred above so their require_package
5056    // calls fire AFTER OmniBus is in place).
5057    maybe_require_dependencies(name, "cls");
5058    return loaded;
5059  }
5060  result
5061}
5062
5063/// configuration for searching for a file in the local filesystem
5064#[derive(Default)]
5065pub struct FindFileOptions {
5066  // TODO: this is no longer used in find_file, rather a level earlier
5067  pub forbid_ltxml:      bool,
5068  pub notex:             bool,
5069  pub ext_type:          Option<Cow<'static, str>>,
5070  pub search_paths_only: bool,
5071}
5072
5073/// search for a file as prescribed by a `FindFileOptions` configuration
5074pub fn find_file(file: &str, options: Option<FindFileOptions>) -> Option<String> {
5075  let options = options.unwrap_or_default();
5076  if pathname::is_literaldata(file) {
5077    // If literal protocol return immediately (unless notex!)
5078    if options.notex {
5079      None
5080    } else {
5081      // TODO: Consider returning a Cow<str> instead to optimize
5082      Some(file.to_string())
5083    }
5084  } else if pathname::is_literaldata(file) || pathname::is_url(file) {
5085    // If a known special protocol return immediately
5086    Some(file.to_string())
5087  } else if let Some(ref ext) = options.ext_type {
5088    // Otherwise, it's some kind of "real" file, and we might have to search for it
5089    // Specific type requested? Search for it.
5090    // Add the extension, if it isn't already there. Perl tests with the DOT
5091    // (`/\.\Q$type\E$/`): a bare `\bibliography{mybib}` must retry
5092    // `mybib.bib` — the dotless ends_with("bib") matched the basename itself
5093    // and searched the literal `mybib`, silently disabling the .bib fallback
5094    // (PR_READINESS must-fix 6).
5095    let dotted = s!(".{}", ext);
5096    let aux_file = if file.ends_with(&dotted) {
5097      file.to_string()
5098    } else {
5099      s!("{}.{}", file, ext)
5100    };
5101    find_file_aux(&aux_file, &options)
5102  } else if file.ends_with(".tex") {
5103    // If no type given, we MAY expect .tex, or maybe NOT!!
5104    // No requested type, then .tex; Of course, it may already have it!
5105    find_file_aux(file, &options)
5106  } else {
5107    match find_file_aux(&s!("{}.tex", file), &options) {
5108      None => find_file_aux(file, &options),
5109      Some(f) => Some(f),
5110    }
5111  }
5112}
5113
5114/// Does a compiled binding answer an existence test (`\IfFileExists`, `\InputIfFileExists`) for `file`, as the file
5115/// itself? Perl's `\IfFileExists` asks `FindFile` (latex_constructs.pool.ltxml:5692-5697), which takes a `.ltxml`
5116/// binding for the file (Package.pm:2125-2128): pgf's `\IfFileExists{pgfsys-latexml.def}` (LaTeXML's own driver) and
5117/// babel's `\InputIfFileExists{italian.ldf}` on a tree without it find their bindings. A binding standing in for an
5118/// author's file is not that file ([`crate::binding::author_files`]): TeX finds `arxiv.sty` only where the paper ships
5119/// it, and so do we, by the disk search the caller makes first (2606.27281; Perl answers true for its own such
5120/// bindings, `neurips.sty`, KNOWN_PERL_ERRORS #770).
5121pub fn binding_counts_as_file(file: &str) -> bool {
5122  !crate::binding::author_files::stands_in_for_an_author_file(file)
5123    && find_file(
5124      file,
5125      Some(FindFileOptions {
5126        notex: true,
5127        ..Default::default()
5128      }),
5129    )
5130    .is_some()
5131}
5132
5133/// Suffixes with separator (Perl @find_fallback_suffixes).
5134static FALLBACK_SUFFIX: std::sync::LazyLock<Regex> = std::sync::LazyLock::new(|| {
5135  Regex::new(r"(?i)[._-](arx|arxiv|conference|workshop|tmp|alternate|preprint|fixed|[vV]?[-_.\d]+|old|new|final|clean|mine|priv|rev|mod|modified|edited|custom|altered|rtx)$").unwrap()
5136});
5137/// Glued suffixes without separator.
5138static FALLBACK_GLUED: std::sync::LazyLock<Regex> =
5139  std::sync::LazyLock::new(|| Regex::new(r"(?i)([vV]?[-_.\d]+|arxiv)$").unwrap());
5140/// Prefixes. Perl Package.pm L2182: `^((?:rw|my|preprint)[-_.]?)` — separator is OPTIONAL, so `mysvjour3` strips to
5141/// `svjour3` (not just `mysvjour`). Caught on arxiv 1206.0536 (\documentclass{mysvjour3}).
5142static FALLBACK_PREFIX: std::sync::LazyLock<Regex> =
5143  std::sync::LazyLock::new(|| Regex::new(r"(?i)^((?:rw|my|preprint)[-_.]?)").unwrap());
5144
5145/// Perl Package.pm L2141-2210: FindFile_fallback
5146/// Pure-check variant of [`find_file_fallback`]: strips the same
5147/// suffix/prefix patterns and reports whether the fallback name has a
5148/// registered binding, but DOES NOT eagerly invoke the binding's
5149/// `load_definitions`. Use this from pre-flight existence checks (e.g.
5150/// `class_cls_via_fallback` in tex_job.rs) where firing the binding's
5151/// body has side effects (\LoadClass, \RequirePackage) that contaminate
5152/// the subsequent real load. Witness: astro-ph0002213 — the `\psfig`
5153/// cluster fix (mn1 → mn fallback was eagerly running mn.cls's
5154/// `\LoadClass{article}` before the `\documentstyle[epsfig]{mn1}`
5155/// option-pass-through machinery had a chance to enqueue `epsfig` into
5156/// `opt@article.cls`).
5157pub fn find_file_fallback_exists(name: &str, ext_type: &str) -> bool {
5158  use crate::state::binding_exists;
5159  // Mirror find_file_fallback's regex set exactly.
5160  let (suffix_rx, glued_rx, prefix_rx) = (&*FALLBACK_SUFFIX, &*FALLBACK_GLUED, &*FALLBACK_PREFIX);
5161  let basename = pathname::file_name(name);
5162  let mut base = if basename.is_empty() {
5163    name.to_string()
5164  } else {
5165    basename
5166  };
5167  // Mirror find_file_fallback: a directory strip ALONE must not qualify (a `subdir/<name>` is a
5168  // path, not a version — it must not name-match `<name>`'s binding). Only a real version
5169  // suffix/prefix strip counts; guard the `^`-anchored prefix strip behind `!dir_stripped` so
5170  // `sty/myunits` doesn't become `units`.
5171  let dir_stripped = base != name;
5172  let mut suffix_stripped = false;
5173  loop {
5174    if let Some(m) = suffix_rx.find(&base) {
5175      base = base[..m.start()].to_string();
5176      suffix_stripped = true;
5177      continue;
5178    }
5179    if let Some(m) = glued_rx.find(&base) {
5180      base = base[..m.start()].to_string();
5181      suffix_stripped = true;
5182      continue;
5183    }
5184    if !dir_stripped && let Some(m) = prefix_rx.find(&base) {
5185      base = base[m.end()..].to_string();
5186      suffix_stripped = true;
5187      continue;
5188    }
5189    break;
5190  }
5191  if !suffix_stripped || base.is_empty() || base == name {
5192    return false;
5193  }
5194  binding_exists(&base, ext_type)
5195}
5196
5197/// Kind of fallback that matched, returned by [`find_file_fallback`].
5198///
5199/// `Versioned` — suffix/prefix stripping changed the basename (Perl
5200/// FindFile_fallback's core function). Drivers: 1206.0536 (mysvjour3
5201/// → svjour3), astro-ph0005021 (./aaspp4 → ./aaspp).
5202///
5203/// `BasenameOnly` — the *only* change was directory-prefix removal,
5204/// matching the binding registry by leaf name. This is a Rust-specific
5205/// extension on top of Perl's FindFile_fallback that exists because
5206/// our contrib-binding registry is keyed by basename. Drivers:
5207/// 2105.02087 (misc/ieeetran → IEEEtran binding); 2405.18387
5208/// (assets/equations → equations binding).
5209///
5210/// Both kinds win unconditionally over local raw `.sty`/`.cls` files
5211/// at the call site (see `input_definitions` Step 3): the `.rs`
5212/// bindings are hand-tuned for the conversion, so a binding match
5213/// always supersedes a co-located vendored copy. The variant is
5214/// preserved for diagnostics and potential future policy tweaks.
5215#[derive(Debug, Clone, Copy, PartialEq, Eq)]
5216pub enum FallbackKind {
5217  Versioned,
5218  BasenameOnly,
5219}
5220
5221/// Strip version/arxiv suffixes from package names to find existing bindings.
5222/// Returns the fallback filename (with extension) and which kind of fallback fired.
5223pub fn find_file_fallback(name: &str, ext_type: &str) -> Option<(String, FallbackKind)> {
5224  let (suffix_rx, glued_rx, prefix_rx) = (&*FALLBACK_SUFFIX, &*FALLBACK_GLUED, &*FALLBACK_PREFIX);
5225
5226  // Strip a leading directory path (Perl Package.pm L2167-2170: FindFile_fallback
5227  // calls `pathname_name($name)` first, so e.g. `\documentclass{./sty/IEEEtran}`
5228  // routes the basename `IEEEtran` through the binding-name registry. Without
5229  // this, `IEEEtran.cls.ltxml` is missed because `./sty/IEEEtran.cls.ltxml`
5230  // never matches the @ltxml_paths registry. Driver paper: arXiv:1308.6663.
5231  let basename = pathname::file_name(name);
5232  let mut base = if basename.is_empty() {
5233    name.to_string()
5234  } else {
5235    basename
5236  };
5237  let dir_stripped = base != name;
5238  let mut suffix_stripped = false;
5239  // Iteratively strip suffixes, then glued, then prefixes.
5240  //
5241  // Perl's FindFile_fallback (Package.pm:2174) version-strips the FULL `$file`
5242  // *with its directory prefix intact*, so the prefix regex `^(rw|my|preprint)`
5243  // — anchored at the very START of the string — never matches a name that
5244  // begins with a directory (e.g. `sty/myunits` starts with `sty/`, not `my`).
5245  // We strip the directory FIRST (to allow a basename-exact binding match like
5246  // `misc/ieeetran` → IEEEtran), so we must NOT then apply the `^`-anchored
5247  // prefix strip to the basename, or `sty/myunits` wrongly becomes `units` and
5248  // loads the stock units.sty instead of the paper-local myunits.sty (which
5249  // defines `\T`/`\fC`/`\Cm` via its `\defUnit` mechanism). Witness 1702.05093.
5250  // The suffix/glued strips ARE `$`-anchored, so they still match the tail of a
5251  // dir-prefixed name in Perl (`./aaspp4` → `./aaspp`); keep applying them.
5252  loop {
5253    if let Some(m) = suffix_rx.find(&base) {
5254      base = base[..m.start()].to_string();
5255      suffix_stripped = true;
5256      continue;
5257    }
5258    if let Some(m) = glued_rx.find(&base) {
5259      base = base[..m.start()].to_string();
5260      suffix_stripped = true;
5261      continue;
5262    }
5263    if !dir_stripped && let Some(m) = prefix_rx.find(&base) {
5264      base = base[m.end()..].to_string();
5265      suffix_stripped = true;
5266      continue;
5267    }
5268    break;
5269  }
5270
5271  // A directory strip ALONE no longer triggers a fallback: `subdir/<name>` is a PATH, not a
5272  // version, so it must not name-match `<name>`'s binding (that shadowed paper-local subdir
5273  // packages, e.g. `utils/mathenv` -> the unrelated CTAN mathenv binding, leaving the local
5274  // file — and its cleveref/theorem defs — unloaded). Only a real VERSION suffix/prefix strip
5275  // (neurips_2026->neurips, mysvjour3->svjour3) qualifies, matching Perl's FindFile_fallback
5276  // (which never strips a directory). The retired `BasenameOnly` convenience (2105.02087
5277  // misc/ieeetran, 2405.18387 assets/equations) now falls to raw-load/OmniBus like Perl.
5278  if !suffix_stripped {
5279    return None;
5280  }
5281  if base.is_empty() || base == name {
5282    return None;
5283  }
5284
5285  let kind = FallbackKind::Versioned;
5286
5287  let fallback_filename = format!("{base}.{ext_type}");
5288  // Whether a binding answers to the stripped name, asked of the registry without running it, as Perl's
5289  // `FindFile_fallback` asks `pathname_find("$fallback_query.ltxml")` (Package.pm:2221-2226) — case-insensitively, as
5290  // the dispatchers match (`jhep…` → `JHEP.cls`). Running it here as the probe ran it twice with the caller's load: a
5291  // binding that saves a command before redefining it saved its own redefinition the second time —
5292  // `\usepackage{hyperref.2.0}` made `\H@refstepcounter` expand to itself, and every `\section` looped (Fatal;
5293  // 2606.14467). A binding the registry cannot name (a runtime binding) is probed by loading it; the caller's load of
5294  // the same name is then skipped (`reloadable: false`), so it still runs once.
5295  let registered = get_binding_names().iter().any(|names| {
5296    names
5297      .iter()
5298      .any(|(n, e)| n.eq_ignore_ascii_case(&base) && e.eq_ignore_ascii_case(ext_type))
5299  });
5300  if registered
5301    || matches!(load_binding(&fallback_filename), Ok(Some(_)))
5302    || matches!(load_external_binding(&fallback_filename), Ok(Some(_)))
5303  {
5304    Some((fallback_filename, kind))
5305  } else {
5306    None
5307  }
5308}
5309
5310fn find_file_aux(file: &str, options: &FindFileOptions) -> Option<String> {
5311  // If cached, return simple path (it's a key into the cache). An empty
5312  // virtual file is still a file: tcolorbox's `\tcbwritetemp` over an empty
5313  // `posterboxenv` body stores "" and `\tcbusetemp` then `\input`s it —
5314  // falling through to disk raised `missing_file:<job>.tcbtemp` ×7 on
5315  // tcolorbox/tcolorbox-tutorial-poster (+ the bfh-ci/tuda-ci poster demos).
5316  // expl3's `\file_full_name:n` (and `\input{./x}`) hand a `./`-prefixed
5317  // name to `\openin`; the cache key is the name `filecontents` was given.
5318  let vkey = file.strip_prefix("./").unwrap_or(file);
5319  if crate::binding::virtual_files::vfs_exists(vkey) {
5320    return Some(vkey.to_string());
5321  }
5322  if pathname::is_absolute(file) {
5323    // Perl Package.pm L2089-2093:
5324    //   if pathname_is_absolute($file) {
5325    //     if (!$options{noltxml}) {
5326    //       return $file . '.ltxml' if -f ($file . '.ltxml'); }
5327    //     return $file if -f $file;
5328    //     return; }
5329    // No `<file>.ltxml` lookup (Perl checked one here): a `.ltxml` is a Perl
5330    // LaTeXML binding latexml-oxide can never read. Binding availability is
5331    // decided by the DISPATCHER (compiled + Rhai) in the relative-path branch's
5332    // `notex` fast-path, not by a file on disk.
5333    if pathname::is_readable_file(file) {
5334      Some(file.to_string())
5335    } else {
5336      None
5337    }
5338  } else if pathname::is_nasty(file) {
5339    // If it is a nasty filename, we won't touch it.
5340    // we DO NOT want to pass this to kpathse or such!
5341    None
5342  } else {
5343    // Note that the strategy is complicated by the fact that
5344    // (1) we prefer .ltxml bindings, if present
5345    // (2) those MAY be present in kpsewhich's DB (although our searchpaths take precedence!)
5346    // (3) BUT we want to avoid kpsewhich if we can, since it's slower
5347    // (4) depending on switches we may EXCLUDE .ltxml OR raw tex OR allow both.
5348    let paths: Vec<String> = get_search_paths();
5349    // let _urlbase = state!().lookup_value("URLBASE");
5350    // let _nopaths = lookup_bool("REMOTE_REQUEST");
5351    // let _ltxml_paths: Vec<String> = if nopaths { vec![] } else { paths.clone() };
5352
5353    // Rust equivalent of Perl's ".ltxml" check: if the binding dispatcher
5354    // has an entry for this file, consider it "found". This is how Perl's
5355    // FindFile discovers pgfsys-latexml.def.ltxml etc.
5356    //
5357    // Two registry kinds are consulted (in order of cost):
5358    //  1. The per-call `{file}_binding_available` runtime flag, which packages can set to
5359    //     pre-announce their availability (used by pgf_sty for `pgfsys-latexml.def`).
5360    //  2. The compile-time class registry (latexml_package's `BINDINGS`) surfaced via
5361    //     `state::get_class_binding_names()`. Without this, `find_file("revtex4-1.cls",
5362    //     notex=true)` returned None for compiled-in bindings — so AIAA.cls's
5363    //     `\LoadClass{revtex4-1}` was silently skipped, breaking the eager natbib transitive load
5364    //     (1709.05096 / AIAA → 60s wall-clock SIGABRT in the autoload- trapped-by-abstract loop).
5365    // Binding-marker fast paths. ONLY fire when caller has requested
5366    // `notex=true` (i.e. caller wants binding-only search, not a real
5367    // disk path). Without this gate, raw `\openin` /`\IfFileExists`
5368    // calls (notex=false) get a literal binding name back as if it were
5369    // a path — `Mouth::open_file` then fails / produces an empty mouth
5370    // and `\ifeof` returns true, masking the file as missing. Mirrors
5371    // Perl `pathname_find`: only `noltxml=>0,notex=>1` returns binding
5372    // names; the disk-search variant only resolves real files.
5373    // Triggered by 2026-04-26 t1enc.def-cascade investigation: raw
5374    // fonttext.ltx's `\input  {t1enc.def}` opens via raw `\openin` /
5375    // `\IfFileExists`; without this gate find_file returned literal
5376    // "t1enc.def" → empty mouth → kernel's `\@missingfileerror` → 1M
5377    // TooManyErrors during latex.ltx dump-build.
5378    if !options.forbid_ltxml && options.notex {
5379      if lookup_bool(&s!("{file}_binding_available")) {
5380        return Some(file.to_string());
5381      }
5382      // Check the compile-time binding registries from latexml_package and
5383      // latexml_contrib for ANY (name, ext) pair that matches `file`.
5384      // Matches both multi-dot extensions (e.g. pgfmath.code.tex -> ("pgfmath", "code.tex"))
5385      // and multi-dot package names (e.g. phy-ab.braket.sty -> ("phy-ab.braket", "sty")).
5386      if file.contains('.') {
5387        // Perl pathname_find L383-389: strict-case first, then case-insensitive
5388        // fallback (mirrors the dispatcher's lookup). Without this, requests
5389        // like `find_file("jhep.cls", notex=true)` would miss `("JHEP","cls")`
5390        // entries that derive from Perl's `JHEP.cls.ltxml` filename.
5391        let exact = get_binding_names().iter().any(|slice| {
5392          slice.iter().any(|(n, e)| {
5393            file.len() == n.len() + 1 + e.len()
5394              && file.starts_with(n)
5395              && file.as_bytes()[n.len()] == b'.'
5396              && file.ends_with(e)
5397          })
5398        });
5399        let nocase = exact
5400          || get_binding_names().iter().any(|slice| {
5401            slice.iter().any(|(n, e)| {
5402              file.len() == n.len() + 1 + e.len()
5403                && file[..n.len()].eq_ignore_ascii_case(n)
5404                && file.as_bytes()[n.len()] == b'.'
5405                && file[n.len() + 1..].eq_ignore_ascii_case(e)
5406            })
5407          });
5408        if nocase {
5409          return Some(file.to_string());
5410        }
5411      }
5412    } else if !options.forbid_ltxml {
5413      // Narrow notex=false (disk-search) fallback: ONLY honor explicit
5414      // `<file>_binding_available` runtime flags, NOT the broad compile-time
5415      // registry. Mirrors Perl `\openin` calling default-args FindFile —
5416      // see `TeX_FileIO.pool.ltxml:50-64` "we SHOULD find an .ltxml version!"
5417      //
5418      // Use case: pgf.sty's pgfsys.code.tex `\pgfutil@InputIfFileExists{\pgfsysdriver}`
5419      // → `\pgfutil@IfFileExists{pgfsys-latexml.def}` → `\openin` with notex=false.
5420      // The openin impl in tex_file_io.rs creates an empty Mouth on
5421      // Mouth::create failure, so \ifeof=false → pgf inputs the driver.
5422      // pgf_sty.rs sets `pgfsys-latexml.def_binding_available=true` to
5423      // enable this; without the flag we don't fake-find arbitrary binding
5424      // names (which would re-introduce the t1enc.def `\@missingfileerror`
5425      // cascade documented above the notex=true branch).
5426      if lookup_bool(&s!("{file}_binding_available")) {
5427        return Some(file.to_string());
5428      }
5429    }
5430    // Perl L2123-2125: `elsif !notex && !interpreting && pathname_find($file,
5431    // paths=>$paths)` — search local paths for raw TeX.
5432    // (Rust does not yet honour `INTERPRETING_DEFINITIONS` — minor TODO,
5433    //  acknowledged in the audit.)
5434    if !options.notex
5435      && let Some(path) = pathname::find(file, PathnameFindOptions {
5436        paths: Some(paths),
5437        ..PathnameFindOptions::default()
5438      })
5439    {
5440      return Some(path);
5441    }
5442    // Perl L2131-2136: build kpsewhich candidate list:
5443    //   @candidates = ( "$file.ltxml" if !noltxml && !nopaths,
5444    //                   $file        if !notex );
5445    //   if (!searchpaths_only) && pathname_kpsewhich(@candidates) → -f $result
5446    // Perl gates the kpsewhich call only on `!searchpaths_only`; `notex`
5447    // and `noltxml` instead control which candidate names are tried.
5448    if options.search_paths_only {
5449      return (!options.notex).then(|| find_on_input_path(file)).flatten();
5450    }
5451    // NB: we do NOT add a `<file>.ltxml` candidate. That was Perl's logic —
5452    // a `.ltxml` is a Perl LaTeXML binding, and latexml-oxide can never read
5453    // one (as TeX or otherwise). The Rust equivalent of "is there a binding for
5454    // this name?" is the BINDING DISPATCHER (the `get_binding_names()` /
5455    // `{file}_binding_available` fast-path above, gated on `notex`), not a
5456    // `.ltxml` on disk. Otherwise kpsewhich returns e.g. `stex.sty.ltxml` (it
5457    // ships in TeX Live) ahead of the raw `stex.sty`, and the raw-loader
5458    // tokenizes the Perl source as TeX (`$out =~ s/^\s+//;` → "Script ^…",
5459    // `\DefMacroI` undefined, …). A missing binding falls through to the raw
5460    // `.sty`, matching pdflatex.
5461    let mut candidates: Vec<String> = Vec::new();
5462    if !options.notex {
5463      candidates.push(file.to_string());
5464    }
5465    if candidates.is_empty() {
5466      return None;
5467    }
5468    let refs: Vec<&str> = candidates.iter().map(|s| s.as_str()).collect();
5469    match pathname::kpsewhich(&refs) {
5470      // Perl L2136: `(-f $result ? $result : undef)` — re-confirm existence.
5471      Some(p) if Path::new(&p).exists() => Some(p),
5472      _ => find_on_input_path(file),
5473    }
5474  }
5475}
5476
5477/// l3file's `\file_full_name:n` (expl3-code.tex:12585-12612), which `\IfFileExists` asks (latex.ltx:9667-9696): a
5478/// file kpathsea does not find is looked for under each entry of `\input@path`, in order, a `/` added to an entry
5479/// lacking one (`\__file_full_name_slash:n`). Perl's FindFile has no such step, so a paper keeping its style files in a
5480/// subdirectory (`\def\input@path{{styles/}}`, 71 papers in 2609; 2609.02998's class style, whose natbib every
5481/// `\citep` needed) loaded none of them.
5482fn find_on_input_path(file: &str) -> Option<String> {
5483  let entries = input_path_entries();
5484  if entries.is_empty() {
5485    return None;
5486  }
5487  let paths = get_search_paths();
5488  entries.into_iter().find_map(|dir| {
5489    // (l3file makes an empty entry `/`, the filesystem root; the local paths, searched already, stand in for it)
5490    let candidate = if dir.is_empty() || dir.ends_with('/') {
5491      s!("{dir}{file}")
5492    } else {
5493      s!("{dir}/{file}")
5494    };
5495    if crate::binding::virtual_files::vfs_exists(&candidate) {
5496      return Some(candidate);
5497    }
5498    pathname::find(&candidate, PathnameFindOptions {
5499      paths: Some(paths.clone()),
5500      ..PathnameFindOptions::default()
5501    })
5502  })
5503}
5504
5505/// The entries of `\input@path` (each brace group one directory, as `\tl_map_tokens:Nn` takes them; latex.ltx's
5506/// `\@iffileonpath` and l3file's `\file_full_name:n` look a file up under each in turn); none where it is undefined.
5507pub fn input_path_entries() -> Vec<String> {
5508  let Some(Stored::Expandable(defn)) = lookup_meaning(&T_CS!("\\input@path")) else {
5509    return Vec::new();
5510  };
5511  let Some(ExpansionBody::Tokens(body)) = defn.get_expansion() else {
5512    return Vec::new();
5513  };
5514  // `\tl_map_tokens:Nn` takes each brace group as one entry (a bare token would be an entry of its own, never a
5515  // directory).
5516  let mut entries: Vec<String> = Vec::new();
5517  let mut depth = 0usize;
5518  let mut entry = String::new();
5519  for token in body.unlist_ref() {
5520    match token.get_catcode() {
5521      Catcode::BEGIN => {
5522        if depth > 0 {
5523          entry.push_str(&token.to_string());
5524        }
5525        depth += 1;
5526      },
5527      Catcode::END if depth > 0 => {
5528        depth -= 1;
5529        if depth == 0 {
5530          entries.push(std::mem::take(&mut entry));
5531        } else {
5532          entry.push_str(&token.to_string());
5533        }
5534      },
5535      _ if depth > 0 => entry.push_str(&token.to_string()),
5536      _ => {},
5537    }
5538  }
5539  entries
5540}
5541
5542//======================================================================
5543// Declaring and Adjusting the Document Model.
5544//======================================================================
5545
5546/// Declare document-model properties for one element tag — Perl's `Tag`
5547/// (`Package.pm` L2015-2030).
5548///
5549/// Merges `properties` into the tag's existing entry rather than replacing it,
5550/// which is what lets a class, a package and the engine each contribute to the
5551/// same tag. How a property merges depends on the property: `auto_open` /
5552/// `auto_close` overwrite when given, while the accumulating properties
5553/// (`afterOpen`, `afterClose`, …) prepend or append per
5554/// [`TagOptionName::is_prepend`]/[`TagOptionName::is_append`] — Perl's
5555/// `$tag_prepend_options` / `$tag_append_options` tables, whose order decides
5556/// whether a late binding's hook runs before or after the engine's.
5557pub fn install_tag(tag: &str, mut properties: TagOptions) {
5558  let tag_ticket = arena::pin(tag);
5559  with_tag_property_mut(tag_ticket, |options| {
5560    if properties.auto_open.is_some() {
5561      options.auto_open = properties.auto_open;
5562    }
5563    if properties.auto_close.is_some() {
5564      options.auto_close = properties.auto_close;
5565    }
5566    for name in &TagOptionName::all() {
5567      if name.is_prepend() {
5568        options.prepend(name, properties.remove(name));
5569      } else if name.is_append() {
5570        options.append(name, properties.remove(name));
5571      } else {
5572        // we'll handle the regular ones out of the loop
5573      }
5574    }
5575  });
5576}
5577
5578/// Selects the RelaxNG schema defining the XML output language
5579pub fn select_relaxng_schema(schema: &str, namespaces: Option<HashMap<String, String>>) {
5580  // What verb here? Set, Choose,...
5581  model::set_relaxng_schema(schema);
5582  if let Some(namespaces) = namespaces {
5583    for (prefix, value) in namespaces {
5584      model::register_document_namespace(&prefix, Some(&value));
5585    }
5586  }
5587}
5588
5589/// Merge font attributes into the current font, **locally** — the change
5590/// reverts with the enclosing TeX group.
5591///
5592/// Port of Perl `Package.pm:MergeFont` L441-444
5593/// (`AssignValue(font => LookupValue('font')->merge(@kv), 'local')`). Only the
5594/// attributes `font` actually specifies are taken; the rest are inherited from
5595/// the font in force. See [`merge_font_ref`] to merge without moving the font.
5596///
5597/// In text, NFSS's font state follows the merge (`sync_nfss_font_state`).
5598pub fn merge_font(font: Font) { merge_font_ref(&font); }
5599
5600/// Like `merge_font` but borrows the font. Saves a clone when the caller
5601/// has a shared reference (e.g. via Rc) to the font being merged.
5602pub fn merge_font_ref(font: &Font) {
5603  let current = lookup_font().unwrap();
5604  let mut new_font = current.merge_ref(font);
5605  if font.name.is_some() {
5606    // A `\font` identifier changes `cur_font` only (tex.web §1217): NFSS's codes stay, and
5607    // the font they name is kept for the next selection to return to (`Font::nfss_font`).
5608    new_font.nfss_font = current
5609      .nfss_font
5610      .clone()
5611      .or_else(|| Some(std::sync::Arc::new((*current).clone())));
5612  } else {
5613    sync_nfss_font_state(&current, font, &new_font);
5614  }
5615  assign_font(Rc::new(new_font), Some(Scope::Local));
5616}
5617
5618/// Merge the font `\selectfont` chose from the NFSS codes (sect13.rs): those
5619/// codes are the state already, so nothing is written back — a family whose
5620/// table entry also carries a shape (`cmsltt`) must not overwrite the
5621/// `\f@shape` the next step selects.
5622pub fn merge_selected_font(font: &Font) {
5623  let new_font = lookup_font().unwrap().merge_ref(font);
5624  assign_font(Rc::new(new_font), Some(Scope::Local));
5625}
5626
5627/// Name `font` in `\f@family`, `\f@series`, `\f@shape` and `\f@size`, every
5628/// component as if selected: the NFSS half of a font reset that assigns the
5629/// font directly (base_utilities.rs `neutralize_font`), as `\reset@font` —
5630/// `\normalfont`, latex.ltx:14122 — does in `\@footnotetext`
5631/// (latex.ltx:17658-17659 `\reset@font\footnotesize`).
5632pub fn sync_nfss_font_codes(font: &Font) { sync_nfss_font_state(font, font, font); }
5633
5634/// Keep LaTeX's text-font state — `\f@family`, `\f@series`, `\f@shape`,
5635/// `\f@size` — naming the font a merge puts in force.
5636///
5637/// In LaTeX every text font change goes through those macros: `\bfseries` is
5638/// `\fontseries\bfdefault\selectfont`, `\tt` is `\normalfont\ttfamily`
5639/// (article.cls:490-493), `\small` is `\@setfontsize`, whose `\fontsize`
5640/// records `\f@size` (latex.ltx:12587, 14103-14107). The class and package
5641/// bindings switch the font directly, so a later `\selectfont` — a raw class's
5642/// `\small`, a size switch (dialect.rs) — re-selected stale codes: under
5643/// scrartcl `{\tt\small \foo}` came out roman (repro
5644/// `fonts-nfss/old_font_switch_then_size.tex`).
5645///
5646/// A component the merge specifies, or changes, is written back as an NFSS
5647/// code unless the code in force already denotes it, so `pcr` or `zi4`
5648/// survives a `\ttfamily`. A family with no NFSS code (`nullfont`, `graphic`,
5649/// `math`, and binding-only ones such as `oldstyle`) leaves `\f@family`
5650/// alone, as a raw TeX font switch leaves LaTeX's. A `\font`-defined
5651/// identifier writes no code (`merge_font_ref`), as in TeX, so
5652/// `\font\x=cmtt10 \x\small` is roman again. Math fonts are not
5653/// NFSS's text font (`\mathbf` leaves `\f@series`), so in math only an
5654/// explicit size is recorded, as `\@setfontsize`'s `\fontsize` does there
5655/// (latex.ltx:14103-14107). The writes are local, like the font assignment.
5656fn sync_nfss_font_state(current: &Font, merged: &Font, new_font: &Font) {
5657  if lookup_bool_sym(pin!("IN_MATH")) {
5658    if let Some(points) = merged.size {
5659      sync_nfss_size(points);
5660    }
5661    return;
5662  }
5663  let touched = |specified: bool, old: Option<&str>, new: Option<&str>| specified || old != new;
5664  if touched(
5665    merged.family.is_some(),
5666    current.family.as_deref(),
5667    new_font.family.as_deref(),
5668  ) && let Some(family) = new_font.family.as_deref()
5669  {
5670    sync_nfss_code(
5671      T_CS!("\\f@family"),
5672      family,
5673      |code| font::lookup_font_family(code).and_then(|f| f.family.as_deref()),
5674      font::nfss_family_code,
5675    );
5676  }
5677  if touched(
5678    merged.series.is_some() || merged.forcebold == Some(true),
5679    current.series.as_deref(),
5680    new_font.series.as_deref(),
5681  ) && let Some(series) = new_font.series.as_deref()
5682  {
5683    sync_nfss_code(
5684      T_CS!("\\f@series"),
5685      series,
5686      |code| font::lookup_font_series(code).and_then(|f| f.series.as_deref()),
5687      font::nfss_series_code,
5688    );
5689  }
5690  if touched(
5691    merged.shape.is_some() || merged.emph == Some(true),
5692    current.shape.as_deref(),
5693    new_font.shape.as_deref(),
5694  ) && let Some(shape) = new_font.shape.as_deref()
5695  {
5696    sync_nfss_code(
5697      T_CS!("\\f@shape"),
5698      shape,
5699      |code| font::lookup_font_shape(code).and_then(|f| f.shape.as_deref()),
5700      font::nfss_shape_code,
5701    );
5702  }
5703  let size_touched =
5704    merged.size.is_some() || merged.scale.is_some() || current.size != new_font.size;
5705  if size_touched && let Some(points) = new_font.size {
5706    sync_nfss_size(points);
5707  }
5708}
5709
5710/// Write `points` into `\f@size` unless it already holds that size.
5711fn sync_nfss_size(points: f64) {
5712  if points > 0.0
5713    && let Some(in_force) = nfss_code_text(T_CS!("\\f@size"))
5714    && !in_force
5715      .trim()
5716      .parse::<f64>()
5717      .is_ok_and(|p| (p - points).abs() < 0.005)
5718  {
5719    define_nfss_code(T_CS!("\\f@size"), &font::format_points(points));
5720  }
5721}
5722
5723/// Write `value`'s NFSS code into `cs` unless the code there already denotes
5724/// `value` (`denotes`) or `value` has none (`code_of`).
5725fn sync_nfss_code(
5726  cs: Token,
5727  value: &str,
5728  denotes: impl Fn(&str) -> Option<&'static str>,
5729  code_of: fn(&str) -> Option<&'static str>,
5730) {
5731  // A value with no code leaves the macro alone; plain TeX has no NFSS state.
5732  let Some(code) = code_of(value) else {
5733    return;
5734  };
5735  let Some(in_force) = nfss_code_text(cs) else {
5736    return;
5737  };
5738  if denotes(&in_force) != Some(value) {
5739    define_nfss_code(cs, code);
5740  }
5741}
5742
5743/// `\edef`-like local definition of an NFSS state macro to `code`.
5744fn define_nfss_code(cs: Token, code: &str) {
5745  let body = ExpansionBody::Tokens(crate::mouth::tokenize_internal(TeXString::assembled(
5746    code.to_string(),
5747  )));
5748  let options = ExpandableOptions {
5749    scope: Some(Scope::Local),
5750    ..ExpandableOptions::default()
5751  };
5752  // Only an unbalanced body can fail to define; a code is characters.
5753  let _ = def_macro(cs, None, Some(body), Some(options));
5754}
5755
5756/// The text an NFSS state macro (`\f@family`, …) holds, `None` while it is
5757/// undefined (plain TeX). An `\edef` leaves a parameterless list of
5758/// characters, read directly; anything else is expanded, as `\selectfont`
5759/// reads it.
5760pub fn nfss_code_text(cs: Token) -> Option<String> {
5761  let direct = with_meaning(&cs, |meaning| match meaning {
5762    None => Some(None),
5763    Some(Stored::Expandable(e)) if e.paramlist.is_none() => match &e.expansion {
5764      None => Some(Some(String::new())),
5765      Some(ExpansionBody::Tokens(body))
5766        if body
5767          .unlist_ref()
5768          .iter()
5769          .all(|t| matches!(t.get_catcode(), Catcode::LETTER | Catcode::OTHER)) =>
5770      {
5771        Some(Some(body.to_string()))
5772      },
5773      _ => None,
5774    },
5775    _ => None,
5776  });
5777  match direct {
5778    Some(text) => text,
5779    None => Some(do_expand(Tokens::from(cs)).map_or_else(|_| String::new(), |t| t.to_string())),
5780  }
5781}
5782
5783/// Define a named color (Perl: DefColor).
5784/// Stores as color_{name} and also defines \\color@{name} macro.
5785pub fn def_color(
5786  name: &str,
5787  color: &crate::common::color::Color,
5788  scope: Option<Scope>,
5789) -> Result<()> {
5790  use crate::common::color;
5791  // Check ifglobalcolors — Perl: $scope='global' if lookupDefinition(\ifglobalcolors) &&
5792  // IfCondition(\ifglobalcolors) Guard with lookup first: xcolor may not be loaded (e.g.
5793  // colordvi-only documents).
5794  //
5795  // ALSO force global when `color_force_global` is set: color_sty.rs's lazy
5796  // dvipsnam.def loader (recovering a dropped `\usepackage[dvipsnames]{color}`
5797  // option, e.g. when hyperref preloaded `color` first) fires from WITHIN a
5798  // grouped digestion (`\textcolor{Blue}{…}` / a listings keywordstyle), so the
5799  // 68 dvips colors would otherwise be defined in that local group and revert
5800  // before the next `\color{…}` — making only the FIRST color resolve. Perl
5801  // loads dvipsnam at the preamble (top level), so its colors persist; the flag
5802  // reproduces that global effect. Witness 1705.06183.
5803  let effective_scope = if (lookup_definition(&T_CS!("\\ifglobalcolors"))?.is_some()
5804    && if_condition(&T_CS!("\\ifglobalcolors"))? == Some(true))
5805    || lookup_bool("color_force_global")
5806  {
5807    Some(Scope::Global)
5808  } else {
5809    scope
5810  };
5811  // Store in state as "model c1 c2 ..."
5812  let stored = color.to_stored();
5813  // The lookup side (`color_sty::color_key`) trims; store under the same key.
5814  let name = name.trim();
5815  assign_value(
5816    &s!("color_{name}"),
5817    Stored::String(arena::pin(stored)),
5818    effective_scope,
5819  );
5820  // Define \\color@{name} macro for reversion
5821  // Perl: DefMacroI('\\color@'.$name, undef,
5822  //   '\relax\relax{model spec}{model}{spec_commas}')
5823  let model = color.model();
5824  let comps = color.components();
5825  let spec_parts: Vec<String> = comps.iter().map(|c| color::format_component(*c)).collect();
5826  let spec_space = spec_parts.join(" ");
5827  let spec_comma = spec_parts.join(",");
5828  let drv_spec = match model {
5829    "cmyk" => s!("{spec_space} k {spec_space} K"),
5830    "rgb" => s!("{spec_space} rg {spec_space} RG"),
5831    "gray" => s!("{spec_space} g {spec_space} G"),
5832    _ => s!("{model} {spec_space}"),
5833  };
5834  let model_spec = if lookup_definition(&T_CS!("\\xcolor@"))?.is_some() {
5835    s!("\\xcolor@{{}}{{{drv_spec}}}{{{model}}}{{{spec_comma}}}")
5836  } else {
5837    s!("\\relax\\relax{{{model} {spec_space}}}{{{model}}}{{{spec_comma}}}")
5838  };
5839  def_macro(
5840    T_CS!(s!("\\\\color@{name}")),
5841    None,
5842    crate::mouth::tokenize_internal(TeXString::assembled(model_spec)),
5843    Some(ExpandableOptions {
5844      scope: effective_scope,
5845      ..Default::default()
5846    }),
5847  )?;
5848  Ok(())
5849}
5850
5851/// Define a derived color model (Perl: DefColorModel).
5852/// Stores conversion functions for a derived color model.
5853pub fn def_color_model(model: &str, coremodel: &str) {
5854  assign_value(
5855    &s!("derived_color_model_{model}"),
5856    Stored::String(arena::pin(coremodel)),
5857    Some(Scope::Global),
5858  );
5859}
5860
5861/// Digest tokens in text mode, whatever mode the caller is in.
5862///
5863/// Port of Perl `Package.pm:DigestText` L405-411: brackets the digestion in
5864/// `beginMode`/`endMode` so that material which must come out as text — a
5865/// title, a caption, an alt string — does so even when it is being read from
5866/// inside math. Compare [`digest_literal`], which additionally forces an ASCII
5867/// encoding and leaves the mode's other side-effects out.
5868pub fn digest_text(stuff: Tokens) -> Result<Digested> {
5869  begin_mode("text")?;
5870  let value = digest(stuff);
5871  end_mode("text")?;
5872  value
5873}
5874
5875pub fn digest_literal<T: Into<Tokens>>(stuff: T) -> Result<Digested> {
5876  let stuff: Tokens = stuff.into();
5877  // Perhaps should do StartSemiverbatim, but is it safe to push a frame? (we might cover over
5878  // valid changes of state!)
5879  begin_mode("text")?;
5880
5881  // Fall back to the global text default if no font is currently assigned —
5882  // avoids a panic at this hot path (called from e.g. RefStepID / label
5883  // digestion) when the state's "font" slot hasn't been initialised. Matches
5884  // the same fallback `assign_value("font", Font::text_default(), …)` that
5885  // stomach::init uses at startup.
5886  let font = lookup_font().unwrap_or_else(|| Rc::new(Font::text_default()));
5887  assign_font(
5888    Rc::new(font.merge(fontmap!(encoding => "ASCII"))),
5889    Some(Scope::Local),
5890  ); // try to stay as ASCII as possible
5891
5892  let value = digest(stuff);
5893  assign_font(font, None);
5894  end_mode("text")?;
5895  value
5896}
5897
5898pub fn digest_if(token: Token) -> Result<Option<Digested>> {
5899  if lookup_definition(&token)?.is_some() {
5900    match digest(Tokens!(token)) {
5901      Ok(t) => Ok(Some(t)),
5902      Err(e) => Err(e),
5903    }
5904  } else {
5905    Ok(None)
5906  }
5907}
5908
5909/// A name macro a heading is set from (`\contentsname`, `\refname`, `\indexname`, `\acknowledgmentsname`, …)
5910/// digested in a group of its own, `{\name}`, as a class's `\section*{\name}` sets it: a font switch it makes
5911/// unbraced stays in the heading (RecipeBook.cls:207-209 `\renewcommand{\contentsname}{\hypertarget{contents}{\bfseries
5912/// \fontsize{…}{…}\selectfont Recipes}}` leaked into every later heading and paragraph; recipebook/LongSample), and a
5913/// `\centering` in it, applied at the group's end, still centres the heading (tidyres-doc.tex:9). Perl digests the
5914/// list names as text (`DigestText`, latex_constructs.pool.ltxml:734; Package.pm:405-411, a restricted-horizontal
5915/// mode frame that scopes the font but drops the `\centering`; the port's bare digest leaked, Rust-only) and
5916/// `\refname`/`\bibname`/`\indexname` bare (`DigestIf`, :3885, :4523), which leaks the font in Perl too.
5917/// OXIDIZED_DESIGN #528, KNOWN_PERL_ERRORS #606.
5918pub fn digest_in_group(token: Token) -> Result<Digested> {
5919  digest(Tokens::new(vec![T_BEGIN!(), token, T_END!()]))
5920}
5921
5922/// [`digest_in_group`] for a name that may be undefined (Perl's `DigestIf`).
5923pub fn digest_in_group_if(token: Token) -> Result<Option<Digested>> {
5924  if lookup_definition(&token)?.is_some() {
5925    digest_in_group(token).map(Some)
5926  } else {
5927    Ok(None)
5928  }
5929}
5930
5931/// Test a conditional `\ifXXX` and return its boolean result (Perl: IfCondition).
5932/// Looks up the conditional's test closure and invokes it.
5933pub fn if_condition(if_token: &Token) -> Result<Option<bool>> {
5934  use crate::definition::conditional::ConditionalType;
5935  if let Some(defn) = lookup_definition(if_token)?
5936    && defn.get_conditional_type() == Some(ConditionalType::If)
5937    && let Some(test) = defn.get_test()
5938  {
5939    // Read arguments for the conditional test
5940    let args = match defn.get_parameters() {
5941      Some(params) => params.read_arguments(Some(defn.as_ref()))?,
5942      None => Vec::new(),
5943    };
5944    return Ok(Some(test(args)?));
5945  }
5946  if x_equals(if_token, &T_CS!("\\iftrue")) {
5947    return Ok(Some(true));
5948  }
5949  if x_equals(if_token, &T_CS!("\\iffalse")) {
5950    return Ok(Some(false));
5951  }
5952  Error!(
5953    "expected",
5954    "conditional",
5955    s!("Expected a conditional, got '{}'", if_token.stringify())
5956  );
5957  Ok(None)
5958}
5959
5960/// Set the boolean value of a `\newif`-type conditional (Perl: SetCondition).
5961/// This is only for simple conditionals taking no arguments.
5962pub fn set_condition(if_token: &Token, value: bool, scope: Option<Scope>) {
5963  if let Ok(Some(defn)) = lookup_definition(if_token)
5964    && defn.get_parameters().is_none()
5965  {
5966    let target = if value {
5967      T_CS!("\\iftrue")
5968    } else {
5969      T_CS!("\\iffalse")
5970    };
5971    let_i(if_token, &target, scope);
5972    return;
5973  }
5974  emit_error(
5975    "expected",
5976    "newif",
5977    &format!(
5978      "Expected a conditional defined by \\newif, got '{}'",
5979      if_token.stringify()
5980    ),
5981  );
5982}
5983
5984/// Creates a single `Tokens` representing a TeX invocation of the lead `token` over a list of
5985/// arguments.
5986///
5987/// Note: currently this is near the `Mouth` representation of data, and deals purely with tokens.
5988/// A more generic version of this method may be able to support `ArgWrap` for the argument list.
5989/// Indeed, the return type may also be lifted to a new generic ArgWrap::Invocation, if there was
5990/// benefit.
5991pub fn build_invocation<T: Into<Token>>(token: T, args: Vec<Option<Tokens>>) -> Result<Tokens> {
5992  let token: Token = token.into();
5993  build_invocation_token(token, args)
5994}
5995
5996/// String entry point for `Invocation` (Perl: `Invocation($token,@args)` with a string
5997/// first argument). The string is tokenized via `TokenizeInternal`; if it yields a single
5998/// token, this reduces to `build_invocation` on that token. Otherwise the tokens are
5999/// treated as an "anonymous macro" containing parameter markers like `#1`, and the
6000/// arguments are substituted in.
6001pub fn build_invocation_str(
6002  spec: impl Into<TeXString>,
6003  args: Vec<Option<Tokens>>,
6004) -> Result<Tokens> {
6005  let spec = spec.into();
6006  let tokens = crate::mouth::tokenize_internal(spec.clone());
6007  let mut list = tokens.unlist();
6008  if list.len() > 1 {
6009    // Treat as anonymous macro
6010    let cow_args: Vec<Option<Cow<Tokens>>> = args.into_iter().map(|a| a.map(Cow::Owned)).collect();
6011    return Ok(
6012      Tokens::new(list)
6013        .pack_parameters()?
6014        .substitute_parameters(&cow_args),
6015    );
6016  }
6017  match list.pop() {
6018    Some(cs) => build_invocation_token(cs, args), // reduce to single token.
6019    None => {
6020      Error!(
6021        "unexpected",
6022        "invocation",
6023        s!("Can't invoke empty token spec '{}'", spec)
6024      );
6025      Ok(Tokens::default())
6026    },
6027  }
6028}
6029
6030fn build_invocation_token(token: Token, args: Vec<Option<Tokens>>) -> Result<Tokens> {
6031  // Note: token may have been \let to another defn!
6032  match lookup_definition(&token)? {
6033    Some(defn) => {
6034      let mut invoked_tokens = vec![token];
6035      if let Some(params) = defn.get_parameters() {
6036        invoked_tokens.extend(params.revert_arguments(args)?);
6037      }
6038      Ok(Tokens::new(invoked_tokens))
6039    },
6040    _ => {
6041      let message = s!("Can't invoke {:?}; it is undefined", token.stringify());
6042      token.with_cs_name(|csname| {
6043        Error!("undefined", csname, message);
6044        Ok(())
6045      })?;
6046      let mut invoked_tokens = vec![token];
6047      // DefConstructor!(token, convert_latex_args(args.len(), 0),
6048      // sub { LaTeXML::Core::Stomach::makeError($_[0], 'undefined', token); });
6049      let wrapped_args: Vec<Token> = args
6050        .into_iter()
6051        .flat_map(|arg_opt| {
6052          let mut wrapped = vec![T_BEGIN!()];
6053          if let Some(arg) = arg_opt {
6054            wrapped.extend(arg.unlist());
6055          }
6056          wrapped.push(T_END!());
6057          wrapped
6058        })
6059        .collect();
6060      invoked_tokens.extend(wrapped_args);
6061      Ok(Tokens::new(invoked_tokens))
6062    },
6063  }
6064}
6065
6066/// latex.ltx:18840 `\@onefilewithoptions`: `\global\let\ver@<file>\@empty` before the read, so the file and what it loads
6067/// hear `\@ifpackageloaded` true while it loads (1411.7188's l3names.sty asks for expl3; Perl marks `<file>_loaded`
6068/// before the read, Package.pm:2328, :2363). Returns the mark and what it replaced, unless `filename` is loaded already.
6069fn mark_loading(filename: &str) -> Option<(Stored, Option<Stored>)> {
6070  if file_loaded(filename) {
6071    return None;
6072  }
6073  let ver_cs = T_CS!(s!("\\ver@{filename}"));
6074  let was = lookup_meaning(&ver_cs);
6075  let_i(&ver_cs, &T_CS!("\\@empty"), Some(Scope::Global));
6076  lookup_meaning(&ver_cs).map(|mark| (mark, was))
6077}
6078
6079/// Is `\ver@<filename>` still the very `\@empty` [`mark_loading`] set (the read declared no version)?
6080fn still_loading_mark(filename: &str, mark: &Option<(Stored, Option<Stored>)>) -> bool {
6081  match (mark, lookup_meaning(&T_CS!(s!("\\ver@{filename}")))) {
6082    (Some((Stored::Expandable(mark), _)), Some(Stored::Expandable(ver))) => Rc::ptr_eq(mark, &ver),
6083    _ => false,
6084  }
6085}
6086
6087/// Mark `filename` loaded as its load does (Perl Package.pm:2326 `\fmtversion`): `\ver@<filename>` is the version its
6088/// installed file declares ([`provides_version_of`]), so a date test reads the package's date, else `\fmtversion`.
6089fn mark_version(filename: &str) -> Result<()> {
6090  let ver_cs = T_CS!(s!("\\ver@{filename}"));
6091  match provides_version_of(filename) {
6092    Some(version) => def_macro(
6093      ver_cs,
6094      None,
6095      crate::mouth::tokenize(TeXString::assembled(version)),
6096      Some(ExpandableOptions {
6097        scope: Some(Scope::Global),
6098        ..ExpandableOptions::default()
6099      }),
6100    ),
6101    None => {
6102      let_i(&ver_cs, &T_CS!("\\fmtversion"), Some(Scope::Global));
6103      Ok(())
6104    },
6105  }
6106}
6107
6108/// The `[<date> <version> <info>]` a package or class declares with
6109/// `\ProvidesPackage{name}[…]` / `\ProvidesClass{name}[…]` (ltclass:
6110/// `\ver@name.ext` is that text), or the `date v<version> <info>` that
6111/// `\ProvidesExplPackage{name}{date}{version}{info}` makes of its groups
6112/// (expl3.sty:35-48), read from the installed file so a binding exposes the
6113/// same string as the raw file would. latex.ltx `\xdef`s the text, so the
6114/// kernel's `\space` and `\ ` read as spaces; `None` when no file is on disk,
6115/// it declares nothing, or the text holds any other control sequence — the
6116/// file's own macros (listings.sty:19-23 `\filedate\space\fileversion`, set by
6117/// a prologue a binding never runs), whose expansion a static read cannot know.
6118pub fn provides_version_of(filename: &str) -> Option<String> {
6119  let path = find_file(filename, None)?;
6120  let text = std::fs::read(&path).ok()?;
6121  provided_text(&String::from_utf8_lossy(&text), filename)
6122    .and_then(|declared| expand_declared(&declared))
6123}
6124
6125/// The text the file declares with `\Provides…{name}`, its source lines joined
6126/// with `%` comments dropped, as TeX reads them.
6127fn provided_text(text: &str, filename: &str) -> Option<String> {
6128  let name = filename.rsplit_once('.').map_or(filename, |(n, _)| n);
6129  let mut rest: &str = text;
6130  while let Some(idx) = rest.find("\\Provides") {
6131    rest = &rest[idx + 9..];
6132    let Some(kw) = ["ExplPackage", "ExplClass", "Package", "Class", "File"]
6133      .into_iter()
6134      .find(|k| rest.starts_with(k))
6135    else {
6136      continue;
6137    };
6138    let Some((arg, tail)) = leading_brace_group(&rest[kw.len()..]) else {
6139      continue;
6140    };
6141    if arg.trim() != name {
6142      continue;
6143    }
6144    // expl3.sty:35-48 `\ProvidesExpl…{name}{date}{version}{description}` stores
6145    // `date v<version> description` (the `v` kept once, left out with no version).
6146    if kw.starts_with("Expl") {
6147      let mut groups = Vec::with_capacity(3);
6148      let mut rest_groups = tail;
6149      for _ in 0..3 {
6150        let Some((group, after)) = leading_brace_group(rest_groups) else {
6151          break;
6152        };
6153        groups.push(group.split_whitespace().collect::<Vec<_>>().join(" "));
6154        rest_groups = after;
6155      }
6156      if groups.len() < 3 {
6157        continue;
6158      }
6159      let (date, version, description) = (&groups[0], &groups[1], &groups[2]);
6160      let version = if version.is_empty() {
6161        String::new()
6162      } else if version.starts_with('v') {
6163        s!("{version} ")
6164      } else {
6165        s!("v{version} ")
6166      };
6167      return Some(s!("{date} {version}{description}"));
6168    }
6169    // A `%` comment may stand between the name and the bracket (multirow.sty:25-27, fourier.sty:5-6).
6170    let Some(bracket) = skip_space_and_comments(tail).strip_prefix('[') else {
6171      continue;
6172    };
6173    let Some(end) = bracket.find(']') else {
6174      continue;
6175    };
6176    return Some(
6177      bracket[..end]
6178        .lines()
6179        .map(|l| l.split('%').next().unwrap_or("").trim())
6180        .filter(|l| !l.is_empty())
6181        .collect::<Vec<_>>()
6182        .join(" "),
6183    );
6184  }
6185  None
6186}
6187
6188/// `declared` as `\xdef` leaves it when its only control sequences are the
6189/// kernel's `\space` and `\ ` (a space after a control word is skipped);
6190/// `None` for an empty text or any other control sequence.
6191fn expand_declared(declared: &str) -> Option<String> {
6192  let mut out = String::with_capacity(declared.len());
6193  let mut chars = declared.chars().peekable();
6194  while let Some(c) = chars.next() {
6195    if c != '\\' {
6196      out.push(c);
6197      continue;
6198    }
6199    let mut word = String::new();
6200    while let Some(&l) = chars.peek()
6201      && l.is_ascii_alphabetic()
6202    {
6203      word.push(l);
6204      chars.next();
6205    }
6206    match word.as_str() {
6207      // tex.web §354: the spaces after a control space are skipped too.
6208      "" if chars.next() == Some(' ') => {
6209        while chars.next_if_eq(&' ').is_some() {}
6210        out.push(' ');
6211      },
6212      "space" => {
6213        while chars.next_if_eq(&' ').is_some() {}
6214        out.push(' ');
6215      },
6216      _ => return None,
6217    }
6218  }
6219  let out = out.trim();
6220  (!out.is_empty()).then(|| out.to_string())
6221}
6222
6223/// `text` past leading whitespace and whole `%` comment lines.
6224fn skip_space_and_comments(text: &str) -> &str {
6225  let mut text = text.trim_start();
6226  while let Some(comment) = text.strip_prefix('%') {
6227    text = comment
6228      .split_once('\n')
6229      .map_or("", |(_, next)| next)
6230      .trim_start();
6231  }
6232  text
6233}
6234
6235/// The leading `{…}` group of `text` (after optional whitespace and `%`
6236/// comments; nested braces balanced) and the text after it.
6237fn leading_brace_group(text: &str) -> Option<(&str, &str)> {
6238  // Whitespace and `%` comments may separate the groups (fontspec.sty:
6239  // `\ProvidesExplPackage{fontspec}%` then the date group on the next line).
6240  let body = skip_space_and_comments(text).strip_prefix('{')?;
6241  let mut depth = 0usize;
6242  for (i, c) in body.char_indices() {
6243    match c {
6244      '{' => depth += 1,
6245      '}' if depth == 0 => return Some((&body[..i], &body[i + 1..])),
6246      '}' => depth -= 1,
6247      _ => {},
6248    }
6249  }
6250  None
6251}
6252
6253/// One `\def`-body reading of a token list's parameter characters: `##`
6254/// (two PARAM tokens) becomes one PARAM token; a lone PARAM before a digit is
6255/// TeX's "Illegal parameter number" (kept as is here, no error).
6256fn halve_param_tokens(tks: &Tokens) -> Tokens {
6257  let toks = tks.clone().unlist();
6258  let mut out: Vec<Token> = Vec::with_capacity(toks.len());
6259  let mut i = 0;
6260  while i < toks.len() {
6261    let t = toks[i];
6262    if t.code == Catcode::PARAM && i + 1 < toks.len() && toks[i + 1].code == Catcode::PARAM {
6263      out.push(t);
6264      i += 2;
6265      continue;
6266    }
6267    out.push(t);
6268    i += 1;
6269  }
6270  Tokens::new(out)
6271}
6272
6273/// Convert a LaTeX-style argument spec to our Package form.
6274/// Ie. given $nargs and $optional, being the two optional arguments to
6275/// something like \newcommand, convert it to the form we use
6276pub fn convert_latex_args(
6277  mut nargs: usize,
6278  optional: Option<Tokens>,
