6279) -> Result<Option<Parameters>> {
6280  let mut params = Vec::new();
6281  if let Some(tks) = optional {
6282    // The kernel stores an optional default through TWO `\def` bodies —
6283    // `\@xargdef`'s `\def\foo{\@protected@testopt\foo\\foo{<default>}}`
6284    // (latex.ltx:1249, `\@xargdef` 1245-1258) and `\kernel@ifnextchar`'s `\def\reserved@b{#3}`
6285    // (:14131) — each of which reads `##` as one parameter character, so
6286    // `\newcommand{\x}[4][########1]` hands `\\x` a default of `##1`
6287    // (pdflatex-probed `\detokenize{#1}` = `####1`). etoolbox's
6288    // `\patchcmd` idiom relies on exactly that (biditools.sty:769
6289    // `\newcommand{\bidi@@patchcmd}[4][########1]` — the raw `#`s reached
6290    // the stomach and every biditools-loading manual errored `misdefined:#`,
6291    // 7 docs). Perl's convertLaTeXArgs stores the default raw (KNOWN_PERL_ERRORS
6292    // #134). Guard: `perfect_kernel_batch54::newcommand_default_halves_param_tokens_twice`.
6293    let tks = halve_param_tokens(&halve_param_tokens(&tks));
6294    params.push(
6295      Parameter {
6296        name: pin!("Optional"),
6297        spec: arena::pin(s!("[Default:{}]", tks.clone().untex())),
6298        extra: vec![tks],
6299        // `\@protected@testopt`'s peek: a number scan ends at the macro
6300        // (tex.web §445; `\cc=1\foo` assigns 1, pdflatex).
6301        testopt: true,
6302        ..Parameter::default()
6303      }
6304      .init()?,
6305    );
6306    // Perl `convertLaTeXArgs` does `$nargs-- if $optional`; with `$nargs==0`
6307    // that just yields an empty `1..$nargs` range. Saturate so the malformed
6308    // `\newcommand\foo[0][d]{}` (0 args yet an optional default — outside
6309    // LaTeX's 0..9 domain) can't underflow `usize` to ~2^64 and spin the loop
6310    // below. Mirrors the sibling `convert_twoopt_args`.
6311    nargs = nargs.saturating_sub(1);
6312  }
6313
6314  for _ in 1..=nargs {
6315    params.push(
6316      Parameter {
6317        name: pin!("Plain"),
6318        spec: pin!("{}"),
6319        ..Parameter::default()
6320      }
6321      .init()?,
6322    );
6323  }
6324  if params.is_empty() {
6325    Ok(None)
6326  } else {
6327    Ok(Some(Parameters::new(params)))
6328  }
6329}
6330
6331/// Two-optional variant of `convert_latex_args` — mirrors Perl's
6332/// `convert2optArgs` helper in twoopt.sty.ltxml. `\newcommandtwoopt{\cs}[n][d1][d2]{…}`
6333/// builds a signature of `[Default d1][Default d2]{…}…` where the remaining
6334/// `n - 2` args are plain required.
6335pub fn convert_twoopt_args(
6336  mut nargs: usize,
6337  opt1: Option<Tokens>,
6338  opt2: Option<Tokens>,
6339) -> Result<Option<Parameters>> {
6340  let mut params = Vec::new();
6341  if let Some(tks) = opt1 {
6342    params.push(
6343      Parameter {
6344        name: pin!("Optional"),
6345        spec: arena::pin(s!("[Default:{}]", tks.clone().untex())),
6346        extra: vec![tks],
6347        // twoopt.sty:49-53 defines the command with `\newcommand#2#3[1][{#5}]`.
6348        testopt: true,
6349        ..Parameter::default()
6350      }
6351      .init()?,
6352    );
6353    nargs = nargs.saturating_sub(1);
6354  }
6355  if let Some(tks) = opt2 {
6356    params.push(
6357      Parameter {
6358        name: pin!("Optional"),
6359        spec: arena::pin(s!("[Default:{}]", tks.clone().untex())),
6360        extra: vec![tks],
6361        ..Parameter::default()
6362      }
6363      .init()?,
6364    );
6365    nargs = nargs.saturating_sub(1);
6366  }
6367  for _ in 1..=nargs {
6368    params.push(
6369      Parameter {
6370        name: pin!("Plain"),
6371        spec: pin!("{}"),
6372        ..Parameter::default()
6373      }
6374      .init()?,
6375    );
6376  }
6377  if params.is_empty() {
6378    Ok(None)
6379  } else {
6380    Ok(Some(Parameters::new(params)))
6381  }
6382}
6383
6384/// Decode a codepoint using the fontmap for a given font and/or encoding (Perl: FontDecode).
6385/// Returns the decoded glyph (if any) and the possibly-adjusted font.
6386pub fn font_decode(
6387  code: i32,
6388  encoding_opt: Option<&str>,
6389  font_opt: Option<Rc<Font>>,
6390) -> (Option<char>, Option<Rc<Font>>) {
6391  if code < 0 {
6392    return (None, font_opt);
6393  }
6394  let font = font_opt.unwrap_or_else(|| lookup_font().unwrap());
6395  let encoding = match encoding_opt {
6396    Some(enc) => enc.to_string(),
6397    None => font
6398      .get_encoding()
6399      .map_or_else(|| "OT1".to_string(), |c| c.to_string()),
6400  };
6401  let map = load_font_map(&encoding);
6402  // Check for family-specific map. Use with_value to avoid cloning the
6403  // Stored envelope when the From<&Stored>→Option<Fontmap> impl only
6404  // needs the enum variant discriminant + an Rc bump.
6405  let (effective_map, _effective_enc) = if let Some(family) = font.get_family() {
6406    let fam_key = s!("{}_{}_fontmap", encoding, family);
6407    let fam_map: Option<Fontmap> = with_value(&fam_key, |v| v.and_then(|s| s.into()));
6408    if let Some(fm) = fam_map {
6409      (Some(fm), s!("{}_{}", encoding, family))
6410    } else {
6411      (map, encoding)
6412    }
6413  } else {
6414    (map, encoding)
6415  };
6416  let glyph = effective_map
6417    .as_ref()
6418    .and_then(|m| m.get(code as usize).copied().flatten());
6419  // In-math alphanumeric mathstyle handling is done in decodeMathChar instead
6420  (glyph, Some(font))
6421}
6422
6423/// Decode a string using the fontmap for a given encoding (Perl: FontDecodeString).
6424/// If `implicit` is true, codepoints missing from the map decode to themselves.
6425pub fn font_decode_string(string: &str, encoding_opt: Option<&str>, implicit: bool) -> String {
6426  let font = lookup_font().unwrap();
6427  let encoding = match encoding_opt {
6428    Some(enc) => enc.to_string(),
6429    None => font
6430      .get_encoding()
6431      .map_or_else(|| "OT1".to_string(), |c| c.to_string()),
6432  };
6433  let map = load_font_map(&encoding);
6434  // Check for family-specific map — same with_value motivation as above.
6435  let effective_map = if let Some(family) = font.get_family() {
6436    let fam_key = s!("{}_{}_fontmap", encoding, family);
6437    let fam_map: Option<Fontmap> = with_value(&fam_key, |v| v.and_then(|s| s.into()));
6438    fam_map.or(map)
6439  } else {
6440    map
6441  };
6442  let input_enc = lookup_string("INPUT_ENCODING");
6443  let map_max: usize = if input_enc == "utf8" { 128 } else { 256 };
6444  // Also limit for short font maps
6445  let map_max = if let Some(ref m) = effective_map {
6446    if m.len() < map_max { m.len() } else { map_max }
6447  } else {
6448    map_max
6449  };
6450
6451  let mut result = String::new();
6452  for ch in string.chars() {
6453    let code = ch as usize;
6454    if implicit {
6455      if let Some(ref m) = effective_map {
6456        if code < map_max {
6457          if let Some(Some(glyph)) = m.get(code) {
6458            result.push(*glyph);
6459          }
6460        } else {
6461          result.push(ch);
6462        }
6463      } else {
6464        result.push(ch);
6465      }
6466    } else if let Some(ref m) = effective_map
6467      && let Some(Some(glyph)) = m.get(code)
6468    {
6469      result.push(*glyph);
6470    }
6471  }
6472  result
6473}
6474
6475// Memoized key symbols for the per-encoding fontmap Value-table entries.
6476// `decode_string` runs per digested character run and used to rebuild (format!
6477// + arena probe) the `"{encoding}_fontmap"`-family keys on every call — ~1M
6478// allocations on a 1.3 s paper (2026-08-23 audit R6, dhat). Encodings are few
6479// (OT1/T1/OML/…), so the (encoding → key syms) maps stay tiny. Value lookups
6480// still go through the state tables — only the key STRINGS are memoized.
6481// Keyed by arena SymStr, so MUST be cleared with `arena::reset()`
6482// (`reset_fontmap_key_memo`, called from `reset_thread_engine`).
6483thread_local! {
6484  static FONTMAP_KEY_MEMO: std::cell::RefCell<HashMap<String, FontmapKeySyms>> =
6485    std::cell::RefCell::new(HashMap::default());
6486  static FONTMAP_FAMILY_KEY_MEMO: std::cell::RefCell<HashMap<(String, String), (SymStr, SymStr)>> =
6487    std::cell::RefCell::new(HashMap::default());
6488}
6489
6490#[derive(Clone, Copy)]
6491pub struct FontmapKeySyms {
6492  /// `"{encoding}_fontmap"`
6493  pub map:       SymStr,
6494  /// `"{encoding}_fontmap_failed_to_load"`
6495  pub fail:      SymStr,
6496  /// `"{encoding}_fontmap_multichar"`
6497  pub multichar: SymStr,
6498}
6499
6500/// The memoized key symbols for `encoding`'s fontmap Value-table entries.
6501pub fn fontmap_key_syms(encoding: &str) -> FontmapKeySyms {
6502  FONTMAP_KEY_MEMO.with(|m| {
6503    if let Some(keys) = m.borrow().get(encoding) {
6504      return *keys;
6505    }
6506    let keys = FontmapKeySyms {
6507      map:       arena::pin(s!("{encoding}_fontmap")),
6508      fail:      arena::pin(s!("{encoding}_fontmap_failed_to_load")),
6509      multichar: arena::pin(s!("{encoding}_fontmap_multichar")),
6510    };
6511    m.borrow_mut().insert(encoding.to_string(), keys);
6512    keys
6513  })
6514}
6515
6516/// The memoized `"{encoding}_{family}_fontmap"` and `"{encoding}_{family}_fontmap_multichar"` key symbols.
6517pub fn fontmap_family_key_syms(encoding: &str, family: &str) -> (SymStr, SymStr) {
6518  FONTMAP_FAMILY_KEY_MEMO.with(|m| {
6519    if let Some(syms) = m.borrow().get(&(encoding.to_string(), family.to_string())) {
6520      return *syms;
6521    }
6522    let syms = (
6523      arena::pin(s!("{encoding}_{family}_fontmap")),
6524      arena::pin(s!("{encoding}_{family}_fontmap_multichar")),
6525    );
6526    m.borrow_mut()
6527      .insert((encoding.to_string(), family.to_string()), syms);
6528    syms
6529  })
6530}
6531
6532/// Clear the fontmap key-symbol memos. Companion to `arena::reset()` — the
6533/// cached `SymStr`s dangle after a reset renumbers the arena.
6534pub fn reset_fontmap_key_memo() {
6535  FONTMAP_KEY_MEMO.with(|m| m.borrow_mut().clear());
6536  FONTMAP_FAMILY_KEY_MEMO.with(|m| m.borrow_mut().clear());
6537}
6538
6539pub fn load_font_map(encoding: &str) -> Option<Fontmap> {
6540  let keys = fontmap_key_syms(encoding);
6541  let _ = preload_font_map_keyed(encoding, keys); // infallible in practice; swallow Result
6542  // with_value avoids the Stored::clone; the Fontmap extraction is a cheap
6543  // Rc bump on the inner slice regardless.
6544  with_value_sym(keys.map, |v| v.and_then(|s| s.into()))
6545}
6546pub fn preload_font_map(encoding: &str) -> Result<()> {
6547  preload_font_map_keyed(encoding, fontmap_key_syms(encoding))
6548}
6549fn preload_font_map_keyed(encoding: &str, keys: FontmapKeySyms) -> Result<()> {
6550  // This check is done as a "preload" step for mutability reasons.
6551  if has_value_sym(keys.map) {
6552    return Ok(());
6553  }
6554  let failed_flag = lookup_bool_sym(keys.fail);
6555  if !failed_flag {
6556    assign_value_sym(keys.fail, true, None); // Stop recursion?
6557    let _ = input_definitions(&encoding.to_lowercase(), InputDefinitionOptions {
6558      extension: Some(Cow::Borrowed("fontmap")),
6559      noerror: true,
6560      ..InputDefinitionOptions::default()
6561    });
6562    if has_value_sym(keys.map) {
6563      // Got map?
6564      assign_value_sym(keys.fail, false, None);
6565    } else {
6566      assign_value_sym(keys.fail, true, Some(Scope::Global));
6567    }
6568  }
6569  Ok(())
6570}
6571
6572#[cfg(test)]
6573mod declared_info_tests {
6574  use super::*;
6575
6576  /// A standard-class derivative's read a panic unwinds out of leaves no entry: the next read is no derivative's.
6577  #[test]
6578  fn a_derivative_read_a_panic_ends_is_popped() {
6579    let unwound = std::panic::catch_unwind(|| {
6580      let _read = StandardClassDerivativeRead::enter(HashSet::default());
6581      panic!("a binding's bug");
6582    });
6583    assert!(unwound.is_err());
6584    assert!(STANDARD_CLASS_DERIVATIVE.with(|reads| reads.borrow().is_empty()));
6585  }
6586
6587  /// `\Provides…` text as latex.ltx's `\xdef` leaves it: the kernel's `\space` and `\ ` read as spaces
6588  /// (cite.sty `[2015/02/27 \space v 5.5]`); a file's own macros (listings.sty:22-23 `\filedate\space\fileversion`,
6589  /// vmargin's `\Vmargin`) give nothing.
6590  #[test]
6591  fn declared_text_expands_like_xdef() {
6592    assert_eq!(
6593      expand_declared("2015/02/27 \\space v 5.5").as_deref(),
6594      Some("2015/02/27  v 5.5")
6595    );
6596    assert_eq!(
6597      expand_declared("2015/02/27\\ v5.5").as_deref(),
6598      Some("2015/02/27 v5.5")
6599    );
6600    assert_eq!(
6601      expand_declared("2015/02/27\\  v5.5").as_deref(),
6602      Some("2015/02/27 v5.5")
6603    );
6604    assert_eq!(
6605      expand_declared("2024/11/12 v2.9 Span multiple rows").as_deref(),
6606      Some("2024/11/12 v2.9 Span multiple rows")
6607    );
6608    assert_eq!(
6609      expand_declared("\\filedate\\space\\fileversion\\space(Carsten Heinz)"),
6610      None
6611    );
6612    assert_eq!(expand_declared("\\Vmargin"), None);
6613    assert_eq!(expand_declared(""), None);
6614  }
6615
6616  /// A `%` comment between the name and the bracket (multirow.sty:25-27); a match without a bracket goes on to the
6617  /// next `\Provides`.
6618  #[test]
6619  fn provided_text_skips_comments_before_the_bracket() {
6620    let multirow =
6621      "\\ProvidesPackage{multirow}%\n  % note\n  [2024/11/12 v2.9 Span multiple rows of a table]\n";
6622    assert_eq!(
6623      provided_text(multirow, "multirow.sty").as_deref(),
6624      Some("2024/11/12 v2.9 Span multiple rows of a table")
6625    );
6626    let twice = "\\ProvidesPackage{x}\n\\ProvidesPackage{x}[2020/01/01 v1]";
6627    assert_eq!(
6628      provided_text(twice, "x.sty").as_deref(),
6629      Some("2020/01/01 v1")
6630    );
6631  }
6632}
